📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of viral hepatitis  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/jvh.70258  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 33, 12, e70258

ABI-4334的安全性、药代动力学及抗病毒药物活性研究:一种新型下一代乙型肝炎病毒衣壳组装调节剂

The Safety, Pharmacokinetics, and Antiviral Activity of ABI-4334, A Novel Next-Generation Hepatitis B Capsid Assembly Modulator.
作者： Edward J Gane, Christian Schwabe, Nelli Ghicavii, Cristina Ticot, Katie Zomorodi, Steven J Knox, Kathryn M Kitrinos, Ran Yan, Jieming Liu, Grace Wang
摘要中文翻译需要针对 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染 (cHBV) 的新型、耐受性良好的短期疗法。ABI-4334 是一种针对 HBV 核心蛋白的下一代衣壳组装调节剂。在两项 1 期研究中检查了 ABI-4334 在健康志愿者和 cHBV 参与者中的安全性、耐受性和药代动力学,以及 抗病毒药物 对 cHBV 参与者的影响。研究101是一项针对健康志愿者的安慰剂对照研究,包括四名单次递增剂量队列研究和两项每日一次多次递增剂量队列研究随机6:2(ABI-4334:安慰剂),以及一项交叉食物效应队列研究随机3:3(进食:禁食)。研究 102 是一项随机、盲法、安慰剂对照研究,其中 cHBV 参与者按 8:2 随机分配(ABI-4334 [2 剂量 队列研究]:安慰剂)。总体而言,研究 101 和研究 102 分别纳入了 54 名健康志愿者和 20 名患有 cHBV 的参与者。研究 102 中的大多数参与者 (75%) 乙型肝炎 e 抗原 (HBeAg) 阴性,并且基线 HBV DNA 和前基因组 RNA (pgRNA) 水平普遍较低。ABI-4334 耐受性良好,没有出现 治疗 出现的严重不良事件 (AE) 或 治疗 出现的导致研究中止的 AE。两项研究中不同剂量的 ABI-4334 平均血浆半衰期范围相当。cHBV 参与者多次给药后,稳态平均血浆半衰期为 17.5 至 27.5 小时。在不同剂量的 HBeAg 阳性 (n = 3) 和阴性 (n = 13) 参与者中观察到 HBV DNA 下降。在基线时可检测到 pgRNA 的参与者中观察到 pgRNA 减少。ABI-4334 具有良好的耐受性,药代动力学和 抗病毒药物 结果支持 ABI-4334 作为 cHBV 单一疗法或新型联合方案的组成部分的持续开发。试验注册:研究 101 (NCT05569941)。研究 102 (NCT06384131)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
对于慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染(慢性乙型肝炎,cHBV)需要开发新型、耐受性良好、短期作用的抗病毒药物。本研究旨在评估ABI-4334的安全性、耐受性和抗病毒药物活性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及两项 1 期试验:研究 101,在健康志愿者中检查 ABI-4334 的多剂量 队列研究 和食物效应 队列研究;研究 102,在 cHBV 参与者中进行随机、盲法、安慰剂对照试验。总体而言,研究纳入了 54 名健康志愿者和 20 名患有 cHBV 的参与者。
核心方法
ABI-4334 的安全性、耐受性和药代动力学通过两项 1 期研究进行评估。研究101包括单次递增和多次递增剂量队列研究,而研究102是一项随机、盲法、安慰剂对照试验。抗病毒药物 效果是通过评估 HBV DNA 和 pgRNA 水平来测量的。
主要结果
ABI-4334具有良好的耐受性,未观察到与治疗相关的严重不良事件或导致研究中断的事件。在不同剂量下,观察到HBeAg阳性与阴性参与者中HBV DNA水平下降。对于基线时检测到pgRNA的参与者,观察到pgRNA水平下降。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,ABI-4334在慢性乙型肝炎患者中具有良好的耐受性和抗病毒药物活性,支持其作为单药或联合治疗方案的进一步开发。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为ABI-4334作为潜在治疗用于慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的进一步开发提供了重要数据,可能为患者带来更优的治疗选择。
局限与证据强度
The studies were Phase 1 trials with limited sample sizes, which may restrict generalizability. However, the evidence is strong due to the well-controlled design and consistent antiviral results across different participant groups.

The Safety, Pharmacokinetics, and Antiviral Activity of ABI-4334, A Novel Next-Generation Hepatitis B Capsid Assembly Modulator.

Authors: Edward J Gane, Christian Schwabe, Nelli Ghicavii, Cristina Ticot, Katie Zomorodi, Steven J Knox, Kathryn M Kitrinos, Ran Yan, Jieming Liu, Grace Wang
Original abstract (English)
Novel, well-tolerated, short-term therapeutics for chronic hepatitis B virus infection (cHBV) are needed. ABI-4334 is a next-generation capsid assembly modulator that targets the HBV core protein. The safety, tolerability, and pharmacokinetics of ABI-4334 in healthy volunteers and participants with cHBV and antiviral effects in participants with cHBV were examined in two Phase 1 studies. Study 101 was a placebo-controlled study of healthy volunteers that included four single-ascending dose cohorts and two once-daily multiple-ascending dose cohorts randomized 6:2 (ABI-4334:placebo), and one crossover food-effect cohort randomized 3:3 (fed:fasted). Study 102 was a randomized, blinded, placebo-controlled study where participants with cHBV were randomized 8:2 (ABI-4334 [2 dose cohorts]:placebo). Overall, 54 healthy volunteers and 20 participants with cHBV were included in Studies 101 and 102, respectively. Most participants (75%) in Study 102 were hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg) negative and generally had lower baseline HBV DNA and pregenomic RNA (pgRNA) levels. ABI-4334 was well tolerated, with no treatment-emergent serious adverse events (AEs) or treatment-emergent AEs leading to study discontinuation. The mean ABI-4334 plasma half-life ranges were comparable across doses in both studies. Following multiple-dose administration in participants with cHBV, the steady-state mean plasma half-life ranged from 17.5 to 27.5 h. HBV DNA declines were observed in both HBeAg-positive (n = 3) and -negative (n = 13) participants across doses. Reductions in pgRNA were observed in participants with detectable pgRNA at baseline. ABI-4334 was well tolerated, with pharmacokinetics and antiviral results supporting the continued development of ABI-4334 as a monotherapy or component of novel combination regimens for cHBV. Trial Registration: Study 101 (NCT05569941). Study 102 (NCT06384131).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Novel, well-tolerated, short-term therapeutics for chronic hepatitis B virus infection (cHBV) are needed. The study aimed to evaluate the safety, tolerability, and antiviral activity of ABI-4334.
Design and population
The study involved two Phase 1 trials: Study 101, which examined ABI-4334 in healthy volunteers with multiple dose cohorts and a food-effect cohort, and Study 102, a randomized, blinded, placebo-controlled trial in participants with cHBV. Overall, 54 healthy volunteers and 20 participants with cHBV were included in the studies.
Core methods
The safety, tolerability, and pharmacokinetics of ABI-4334 were assessed through two Phase 1 studies. Study 101 included single-ascending and multiple-ascending dose cohorts, while Study 102 was a randomized, blinded, placebo-controlled trial. The antiviral effects were measured by evaluating HBV DNA and pgRNA levels.
Main results
ABI-4334 was well tolerated, with no treatment-emergent serious adverse events or events leading to study discontinuation. HBV DNA declines were observed in both HBeAg-positive and -negative participants across doses. Reductions in pgRNA were noted in participants with detectable pgRNA at baseline.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that ABI-4334 is well tolerated and has antiviral activity in participants with cHBV, supporting its development as a monotherapy or component of combination regimens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide important data for the continued development of ABI-4334 as a potential treatment for chronic hepatitis B virus infection, which could lead to improved therapeutic options for patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The studies were Phase 1 trials with limited sample sizes, which may restrict generalizability. However, the evidence is strong due to the well-controlled design and consistent antiviral results across different participant groups.

婴儿期六价和五价 疫苗接种 后,乙型肝炎表面抗体减弱,但直至 10 岁仍无突破性感染的血清学证据。

Waning of hepatitis B surface antibody but no serological evidence of breakthrough infection up to 10 years of age after hexavalent and pentavalent vaccination in infancy.
作者： Thanunrat Thongmee, Nasamon Wanlapakorn, Thaksaporn Thatsanathorn, Sirapa Klinfueng, Preeyaporn Vichaiwattana, Yong Poovorawan
摘要中文翻译泰国将乙型肝炎 (HepB) 疫苗 纳入婴儿组合 疫苗。目前可用的制剂有五价 (DTwP-HB-Hib) 和六价 (DTaP-IPV-HB-Hib) 疫苗。国家政策建议在出生时接种单价 HepB 疫苗 后,在 2、4 和 6 个月大时使用这些组合 疫苗。在这项研究中,收集了在 2、4、6 和 18 个月时接受六价或五价 疫苗 的随机 队列研究 儿童的血清样本(ClinicalTrials.gov NCT02408926)。该队列研究比国家时间表多接受了一剂HepB(总共5剂)。我们评估了抗 HBs 的动力学,并使用 HepB 核心抗原(抗 HBc)抗体筛选突破性 HepB 感染的证据。从 2 岁到 10 岁,每年检测一次抗 HBs,并在 10 岁时检测抗 HBc。共有 153 名 10 岁儿童完成了这项研究。10 年时六价组的血清保护(抗 HBs ≥ 10 mIU/mL)为 75.8%,而五价组为 32.8%,在整个随访过程中,六价组的几何平均浓度仍高出 1 个对数。尽管抗 HBs 减弱,但所有儿童在 10 岁时抗 HBc 检测结果均为阴性。这表明没有突破性感染的血清学证据,即使在抗 HBs < 10 mIU/mL 的儿童中也是如此。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估乙型肝炎表面抗体(抗 HBs)的动力学,并筛选婴儿期六价和五价 疫苗接种 后突破性乙型肝炎感染的证据。背景突出了泰国将乙型肝炎 疫苗 纳入婴儿组合 疫苗 中。
设计与对象
对 2、4、6 和 18 个月时接受六价或五价 疫苗 的儿童进行了一项随机 队列研究 研究。从这些儿童身上采集血清样本进行分析。
核心方法
从 2 岁到 10 岁,每年测量一次抗 HBs 水平,并在 10 岁时测试抗 HBc 以筛查突破性感染。
主要结果
10 岁时,六价组的血清保护(抗 HBs ≥ 10 mIU/mL)为 75.8%,五价组为 32.8%。在整个随访过程中,六价组的几何平均浓度仍然高出一个对数。所有儿童 10 岁时的抗 HBc 检测均为阴性,表明没有突破性感染的血清学证据。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,尽管抗 HBs 水平下降,但没有证据表明 队列研究 中出现突破性乙型肝炎感染,即使在抗 HBs 低于保护阈值的人群中也是如此。
科研与公卫意义
结果为六价和五价 疫苗 在预防乙型肝炎感染方面的长期有效性提供了重要见解,这可以为 疫苗接种 策略和 监测 工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计受到其依赖于血清学标记而没有直接病毒学确认感染的限制。由于随访的观察性质以及潜在的未测量的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Waning of hepatitis B surface antibody but no serological evidence of breakthrough infection up to 10 years of age after hexavalent and pentavalent vaccination in infancy.

Authors: Thanunrat Thongmee, Nasamon Wanlapakorn, Thaksaporn Thatsanathorn, Sirapa Klinfueng, Preeyaporn Vichaiwattana, Yong Poovorawan
Original abstract (English)
Thailand incorporated the hepatitis B (HepB) vaccine into infant combination vaccines. The current available formulations are pentavalent (DTwP-HB-Hib) and hexavalent (DTaP-IPV-HB-Hib) vaccines. The national policy recommends these combination vaccines at 2, 4, and 6 months of age, following a monovalent HepB vaccine at birth. In this study, sera samples of a randomized cohort of children who received hexavalent or pentavalent vaccines at 2, 4, 6, and 18 months were collected (ClinicalTrials.gov NCT02408926). This cohort received one more HepB dose than the national schedule (total 5 doses). We evaluated the kinetics of anti-HBs and screened for evidence of breakthrough HepB infection using antibody to HepB core antigen (anti-HBc). Anti-HBs was measured annually from 2 through 10 years of age, and anti-HBc was tested at 10 years. A total of 153 children completed the study at age 10 years. Seroprotection (anti-HBs ≥ 10 mIU/mL) at 10 years was 75.8% in the hexavalent group vs. 32.8% in the pentavalent group, with geometric mean concentrations remaining one log higher in the hexavalent group throughout follow-up. Despite the waning of anti-HBs, all children tested negative for anti-HBc at 10 years. This indicates no serological evidence of breakthrough infection, even in children with anti-HBs < 10 mIU/mL.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the kinetics of hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) and screen for evidence of breakthrough hepatitis B infection following hexavalent and pentavalent vaccination in infancy. The background highlights the incorporation of the hepatitis B vaccine into infant combination vaccines in Thailand.
Design and population
A randomized cohort study was conducted on children who received hexavalent or pentavalent vaccines at 2, 4, 6, and 18 months of age. Sera samples were collected from these children for analysis.
Core methods
Anti-HBs levels were measured annually from 2 through 10 years of age, and anti-HBc was tested at 10 years to screen for breakthrough infection.
Main results
At 10 years of age, seroprotection (anti-HBs ≥ 10 mIU/mL) was 75.8% in the hexavalent group and 32.8% in the pentavalent group. The geometric mean concentrations remained one log higher in the hexavalent group throughout follow-up. All children tested negative for anti-HBc at 10 years, indicating no serological evidence of breakthrough infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that despite the waning of anti-HBs levels, there was no evidence of breakthrough hepatitis B infection in the cohort, even among those with anti-HBs below the protective threshold.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide important insights into the long-term effectiveness of hexavalent and pentavalent vaccines in preventing hepatitis B infection, which can inform vaccination strategies and surveillance efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design is limited by its reliance on serological markers without direct virological confirmation of infection. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the follow-up and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors.

Journal: Journal of medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/jmv.71224  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 98, 12, e71224
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

比较分析揭示了 HBV 感染和 HBV 相关肝细胞癌模型中不同的 宿主 染色体外环状 DNA 图谱。

Comparative Analysis Reveals Distinct Host Extrachromosomal Circular DNA Profiles Across HBV Infection and HBV-Associated Models of Hepatocellular Carcinoma.
作者： Andrea Jurado, Yuanjie Liu, Tinghe Zhang, Jithinchandran Ramachandran, Zhentao Liu, Elena S. Kim, Yufei Huang, Haitao Guo
摘要中文翻译摘要 染色体外环状 DNA (eccDNA) 范围从小型 microDNA 和小型多分散环状 DNA 到兆碱基规模的染色体外 DNA (ecDNA)。尽管 ecDNA 与病毒相关癌症有关,但致癌病毒如何影响更广泛的 宿主 eccDNA 景观仍然知之甚少。在这里,我们对感染野生型 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 或缺乏调节性 X 蛋白 (HBVΔX) 的 HBV 突变体 (HBVΔX) 的原代人肝细胞 (PHH) 中的 eccDNA 进行了表征,并将这些图谱与 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC) 细胞系 Hep3B 和 PLC/PRF/5 的图谱进行了比较。使用 基因组 全范围测序、计算分析和有针对性的实验验证,我们应用以基因为中心的框架来识别 eccDNA 上经常出现的基因座,并优先考虑与 HBV 相关肝癌发生相关的候选基因。我们观察到异质 eccDNA 景观,其中包括急性 HBV 感染期间检测到的复发基因相关基因座的子集以及 HBV-HCC 模型中选定候选者的表达改变。这些发现建立了一个框架,用于整合急性感染和转化细胞环境中的 eccDNA 图谱。更广泛地说,他们支持进一步研究 eccDNA 作为病毒相关基因组应激的潜在分子记录和 HBV 相关疾病进展的候选贡献者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查致癌病毒如何影响更广泛的 宿主 染色体外环状 DNA (eccDNA) 景观,特别是在 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染和 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的背景下。
设计与对象
该研究对感染野生型 HBV 或缺乏调节性 X 蛋白 (HBVΔX) 的 HBV 突变体 (HBVΔX) 的原代人肝细胞 (PHH) 中的 eccDNA 进行了表征,并将这些图谱与 HBV 相关 HCC 细胞系 Hep3B 和 PLC/PRF/5 的图谱进行了比较。
核心方法
研究人员使用 基因组 全测序、计算分析和有针对性的实验验证,应用以基因为中心的框架来识别 eccDNA 上经常出现的基因座,并优先考虑与 HBV 相关肝癌发生相关的候选基因。
主要结果
观察到异质 eccDNA 景观,包括急性 HBV 感染期间检测到的复发基因相关位点子集以及 HBV-HCC 模型中选定候选者的表达改变。
解释与创新
研究结果建立了一个框架,用于整合急性感染和转化细胞环境中的 eccDNA 图谱,强调了 eccDNA 在病毒相关基因组应激和疾病进展中的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 HBV 相关疾病进展的分子机制的见解,并表明 eccDNA 分析可用于理解和监测病毒诱导的肝细胞癌基因组变化。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注特定的乙型肝炎病毒感染模型和细胞系,可能无法完全代表人类乙型肝炎病毒感染的复杂性。由于受控的实验设置,证据的强度适中。

Comparative Analysis Reveals Distinct Host Extrachromosomal Circular DNA Profiles Across HBV Infection and HBV-Associated Models of Hepatocellular Carcinoma.

Authors: Andrea Jurado, Yuanjie Liu, Tinghe Zhang, Jithinchandran Ramachandran, Zhentao Liu, Elena S. Kim, Yufei Huang, Haitao Guo
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Extrachromosomal circular DNAs (eccDNAs) range from small microDNAs and small polydispersed circular DNAs to megabase‐scale extrachromosomal DNA (ecDNA). Although ecDNA has been implicated in virus‐associated cancers, how oncogenic viruses influence the broader host eccDNA landscape remains poorly understood. Here, we characterized eccDNA in primary human hepatocytes (PHHs) infected with wild‐type hepatitis B virus (HBV) or an HBV mutant lacking the regulatory X protein (HBVΔX) and compared these profiles with those of the HBV‐associated hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) cell lines Hep3B and PLC/PRF/5. Using genome‐wide sequencing, computational analyses, and targeted experimental validation, we applied a gene‐centric framework to identify loci recurrently represented on eccDNA and prioritized candidates relevant to HBV‐associated hepatocarcinogenesis. We observed a heterogeneous eccDNA landscape that included a subset of recurrent gene‐associated loci detected during acute HBV infection and altered expression of selected candidates in HBV‐HCC models. These findings establish a framework for integrating eccDNA profiles across acute infection and transformed cellular contexts. More broadly, they support further investigation of eccDNAs as potential molecular records of virus‐associated genomic stress and as candidate contributors to HBV‐associated disease progression.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how oncogenic viruses influence the broader host extrachromosomal circular DNA (eccDNA) landscape, particularly in the context of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).
Design and population
The study characterized eccDNA in primary human hepatocytes (PHHs) infected with wild-type HBV or an HBV mutant lacking the regulatory X protein (HBVΔX) and compared these profiles with those of HBV-associated HCC cell lines Hep3B and PLC/PRF/5.
Core methods
The researchers used genome-wide sequencing, computational analyses, and targeted experimental validation to apply a gene-centric framework for identifying loci recurrently represented on eccDNA and prioritizing candidates relevant to HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis.
Main results
A heterogeneous eccDNA landscape was observed, including a subset of recurrent gene-associated loci detected during acute HBV infection and altered expression of selected candidates in HBV-HCC models.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish a framework for integrating eccDNA profiles across acute infection and transformed cellular contexts, highlighting the potential role of eccDNAs in virus-associated genomic stress and disease progression.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms underlying HBV-associated disease progression and suggest the utility of eccDNA profiling for understanding and monitoring virus-induced genomic changes in hepatocellular carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on specific HBV infection models and cell lines, which may not fully represent the complexity of human HBV infection. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the controlled experimental setting.

滴度指导强化乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 策略对癌症儿童的免疫原性。

Immunogenicity of titer guided intensified hepatitis B vaccination strategy in children with cancer.
作者： Priyanka Aggarwal, Chandradeep Srivastava, Vineeta Gupta
摘要中文翻译目的:评估滴度引导的强化乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 方案对接受化疗的恶性肿瘤儿童的免疫原性。方法:这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 100 mIU/mL 的儿童,未接受额外的 疫苗接种。评估后续抗-HBs滴度以评估免疫应答。结果:在 169 名儿童中,有 135 名儿童获得了后续抗 HBs 滴度。基线时,79 名 (46.7%) 儿童的抗 HBs 为 100 mIU/mL。在 94 名接种疫苗的儿童中,47 名 (50.0%) 达到了稳健的血清学反应 (> 100 mIU/mL)。基线抗 HBs 滴度为 10-100 mIU/mL 的患者出现强烈反应的频率更高。年幼儿童的反应率较高,但差异无统计学意义。结论:滴度引导的强化乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 策略与强大的血清学反应相关,特别是对于已有部分免疫力的儿童。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估滴度引导的强化乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 策略对接受化疗的恶性肿瘤儿童的免疫原性,解决免疫功能低下患者维持免疫力的挑战。
设计与对象
对 100 mIU/mL 的儿童进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,未接受额外的 疫苗接种。
核心方法
该研究涉及后续抗 HBs 滴度来评估 免疫应答,特别关注基线滴度和 疫苗接种 方案。
主要结果
在 169 名儿童中,有 135 名儿童获得了后续抗 HBs 滴度。基线时,79 名 (46.7%) 儿童的抗 HBs 滴度为 100 mIU/mL。在 94 名接种疫苗的儿童中,47 名 (50.0%) 达到了稳健的血清学反应 (> 100 mIU/mL)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,滴度引导的强化乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 策略与强大的血清学反应相关,特别是对于已有部分免疫力的儿童。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,根据免疫状态调整乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 策略可以改善接受化疗的癌症儿童的血清学反应。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和数据收集中的潜在偏差而受到限制,但由于 疫苗接种 策略与血清学反应之间的明确关联,证据是强有力的。

Immunogenicity of titer guided intensified hepatitis B vaccination strategy in children with cancer.

Authors: Priyanka Aggarwal, Chandradeep Srivastava, Vineeta Gupta
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To assess the immunogenicity of a titer-guided, intensified hepatitis B vaccination protocol in children with malignancies receiving chemotherapy. METHODS: This retrospective cohort study included children aged 100 mIU/mL received no additional vaccination. Follow-up anti-HBs titers were assessed to evaluate immune response. RESULTS: Among 169 children, follow-up anti-HBs titers were available for 135. At baseline, 79 (46.7%) children had anti-HBs 100 mIU/mL. Among 94 vaccinated children, 47 (50.0%) achieved a robust serological response (> 100 mIU/mL). Robust responses were more frequent in those with baseline anti-HBs titers of 10-100 mIU/mL. Response rates were higher in younger children, but the difference was not statistically significant. CONCLUSION: A titer-guided intensified hepatitis B vaccination strategy was associated with robust serological response, particularly in children with partial pre-existing immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the immunogenicity of a titer-guided, intensified hepatitis B vaccination strategy in children with malignancies receiving chemotherapy, addressing the challenge of maintaining immunity in immunocompromised patients.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on children aged 100 mIU/mL received no additional vaccination.
Core methods
The study involved follow-up anti-HBs titers to evaluate immune response, with specific attention to baseline titers and vaccination protocols.
Main results
Among 169 children, follow-up anti-HBs titers were available for 135. At baseline, 79 (46.7%) children had anti-HBs titers of 100 mIU/mL. Among 94 vaccinated children, 47 (50.0%) achieved a robust serological response (> 100 mIU/mL).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that a titer-guided intensified hepatitis B vaccination strategy was associated with robust serological response, particularly in children with partial pre-existing immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that adjusting hepatitis B vaccination strategies based on immune status could improve serological response in children with cancer undergoing chemotherapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential biases in data collection, but the evidence is strong due to the clear association between vaccination strategy and serological response.

HBeAg 阳性 慢性乙型肝炎 的 HCC 风险:低基线 HBsAg 和 治疗 下降不佳的影响。

HCC risk in HBeAg-positive chronic hepatitis B: impact of low baseline HBsAg and poor on-treatment decline.
作者： Tetsuya Hosaka, Fumitaka Suzuki, Yasue Takeuchi, Hitomi Sezaki, Satoshi Saitoh, Shunya Goto, Shigeki Yamamoto, Yasuka Eriksson, Yoshiyuki Suzuki, Yasuji Arase
摘要中文翻译目的:长期核苷(酸)类似物(NUC)治疗期间乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)与肝细胞癌(HCC)发展之间的关联仍不清楚。本研究旨在调查接受 NUC 治疗 治疗的基线 HBeAg 阳性患者的定量 HBsAg (qHBsAg) 水平与 HCC 发病率 之间的关联。方法:总体而言,纳入了 951 名基线 HBeAg 阳性且已接受 NUC 治疗 至少 1 年的患者。根据基线和 治疗 HBsAg 动力学评估的结果是 HCC 发病率 和 HBsAg 丢失 (< 0.05 IU/mL)。结果:在中位 治疗 持续时间 14.7 年中,98 名患者发展为 HCC。低基线 qHBsAg 水平(< 10,000 IU/mL)与高基线 qHBsAg 水平(≥ 10,000 IU/mL)相比,与 HCC 风险高三到四倍显着相关。正如先前报道的那样,在单变量分析中,基线 HBV DNA 水平也显示出与 HCC 的相关性。在多变量分析和受试者工作特征曲线分析中,qHBsAg 在预测 HCC 风险方面优于 HBV DNA。此外,基线 qHBsAg 水平较低和 治疗 第一年 qHBsAg 水平下降幅度较小的患者独立地具有较高的 HCC 风险。相反,基线 qHBsAg 水平低且 qHBsAg 水平下降幅度较小的患者的 HBsAg 血清清除率较低。结论:基线 qHBsAg 水平低和 qHBsAg 下降缓慢与 HCC 的高风险相关,并且基线 HBeAg 阳性且接受 NUC 治疗 的患者 HBsAg 消失的罕见性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
长期核苷类似物 (NUC) 治疗 期间乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 与肝细胞癌 (HCC) 发展之间的关联仍不清楚。
设计与对象
本研究旨在调查接受 NUC 治疗 治疗的基线 HBeAg 阳性患者的定量 HBsAg (qHBsAg) 水平与 HCC 发病率 之间的关联。总体而言,纳入了 951 名基线 HBeAg 阳性且已接受 NUC 治疗 至少 1 年的患者。
核心方法
根据基线和 治疗 HBsAg 动力学评估的结果是 HCC 发病率 和 HBsAg 丢失 (< 0.05 IU/mL)。
主要结果
在中位 治疗 持续时间 14.7 年中,98 名患者发展为 HCC。低基线 qHBsAg 水平(< 10,000 IU/mL)与高基线 qHBsAg 水平(≥ 10,000 IU/mL)相比,与 HCC 风险高三到四倍显着相关。
解释与创新
基线 qHBsAg 水平低和 qHBsAg 下降缓慢与 HCC 的高风险相关,并且接受 NUC 治疗 的基线 HBeAg 阳性患者中 HBsAg 消失的罕见性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,qHBsAg 水平可能是预测接受 NUC 治疗 的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者 HCC 风险的有价值的生物标志物。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于样本量大和随访期长,证据有力。

HCC risk in HBeAg-positive chronic hepatitis B: impact of low baseline HBsAg and poor on-treatment decline.

Authors: Tetsuya Hosaka, Fumitaka Suzuki, Yasue Takeuchi, Hitomi Sezaki, Satoshi Saitoh, Shunya Goto, Shigeki Yamamoto, Yasuka Eriksson, Yoshiyuki Suzuki, Yasuji Arase
Original abstract (English)
AIM: The association between hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) development during long-term nucleos(t)ide analog (NUC) treatment remains unclear. This study aimed to investigate the association between quantitative HBsAg (qHBsAg) levels and HCC incidence in patients with baseline HBeAg positivity who were receiving NUC treatment. METHODS: Overall, 951 patients with chronic hepatitis B with baseline HBeAg positivity who had received NUC treatment for at least 1 year were included. The outcomes evaluated were HCC incidence and HBsAg loss (< 0.05 IU/mL) according to baseline and on-treatment HBsAg kinetics. RESULTS: Over a 14.7-year median treatment duration, 98 patients developed HCC. A low baseline qHBsAg level (< 10,000 IU/mL) was significantly associated with a three to four times higher risk of HCC than a high baseline qHBsAg level (≥ 10,000 IU/mL). Baseline HBV DNA levels also showed the association with HCC in univariate analysis as previously reported. qHBsAg was superior to HBV DNA for predicting the risk of HCC in multivariable analysis and receiver operating characteristic curves analysis. Furthermore, patients with low baseline qHBsAg levels and low decline in qHBsAg levels during the first year of treatment independently had a high risk of HCC. Conversely, the HBsAg seroclearance rates were lower in patients with low baseline qHBsAg levels and low decline in qHBsAg levels. CONCLUSIONS: Low baseline qHBsAg levels and poor decline in qHBsAg were associated with a high risk of HCC, and the rarity of HBsAg loss in patients with baseline HBeAg positivity who were receiving NUC treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The association between hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) development during long-term nucleos(t)ide analog (NUC) treatment remains unclear.
Design and population
This study aimed to investigate the association between quantitative HBsAg (qHBsAg) levels and HCC incidence in patients with baseline HBeAg positivity who were receiving NUC treatment. Overall, 951 patients with chronic hepatitis B with baseline HBeAg positivity who had received NUC treatment for at least 1 year were included.
Core methods
The outcomes evaluated were HCC incidence and HBsAg loss (< 0.05 IU/mL) according to baseline and on-treatment HBsAg kinetics.
Main results
Over a 14.7-year median treatment duration, 98 patients developed HCC. A low baseline qHBsAg level (< 10,000 IU/mL) was significantly associated with a three to four times higher risk of HCC than a high baseline qHBsAg level (≥ 10,000 IU/mL).
Interpretation and novelty
Low baseline qHBsAg levels and poor decline in qHBsAg were associated with a high risk of HCC, and the rarity of HBsAg loss in patients with baseline HBeAg positivity who were receiving NUC treatment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that qHBsAg levels could be a valuable biomarker for predicting HCC risk in patients with chronic hepatitis B undergoing NUC treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and long follow-up period.

澳大利亚 治疗 患者的抗病毒 治疗 资格和 治疗 的使用情况:REACH-B 研究。

Anti-Viral Treatment Eligibility and Treatment Uptake Among People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Australia: The REACH-B Study.
作者： Behzad Hajarizadeh, Renjy Nelson, Jacob George, Josh Hanson, Jane Davies, Marianne Martinello, Tracey Brown, Ian Wong, Tasnim Hasan, Lester Mascarenhas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估澳大利亚 治疗 患者的 治疗 资格和接受情况,强调不同指南中 治疗 标准的差异。
设计与对象
REACH-B 研究招募了大量 队列研究 人员,这些人员通过全国医疗保健服务网络进行管理。参与者被分为三组:接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的组、尽管符合资格但未接受治疗的组、以及未经治疗且不符合资格的组。
核心方法
治疗 资格是根据澳大利亚药品福利计划 (PBS) 标准和国际临床指南确定的。根据 治疗 状态和资格,参与者被分为三组。
主要结果
在 3648 名参与者中,47% 正在接受 抗病毒药物 治疗,45% 未接受治疗且不符合资格,7% 尽管根据澳大利亚 PBS 标准符合资格但未接受治疗。当应用 2024 年世界卫生组织指南时,尽管符合资格但未接受治疗的比例增加至 13%。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了不同指南中 治疗 资格的差异以及人口因素对 治疗 吸收的影响。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了对当前 治疗 模式和 慢性乙型肝炎 患者护理差异的见解,这可以为未来的 监测 和有针对性的干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于大型 队列研究,但依赖于自我报告的数据和调整后的分析,这可能会引入偏差。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Anti-Viral Treatment Eligibility and Treatment Uptake Among People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Australia: The REACH-B Study.

Authors: Behzad Hajarizadeh, Renjy Nelson, Jacob George, Josh Hanson, Jane Davies, Marianne Martinello, Tracey Brown, Ian Wong, Tasnim Hasan, Lester Mascarenhas
Original abstract (English)
Treatment indication for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) varies across clinical guidelines. This study evaluated treatment eligibility and uptake in a large Australian cohort of people with CHB. The REACH-B study recruited people with CHB managed through a national network of healthcare services. Participants were categorised into three groups: (1) receiving antiviral therapy; (2) untreated despite eligibility; (3) untreated and ineligible. Treatment eligibility was determined using Australian Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme (PBS) criteria, and by international clinical guidelines. Among 3648 participants (56% male; median age 48 years; 49% born in North-East or South-East Asia; 27% First Nations Australians), 14% were HBeAg positive and 7% had cirrhosis. Overall, 47% (n = 1666) were on antiviral therapy, 45% (n = 1573) were untreated and ineligible and 7% (n = 251) were untreated despite eligibility based on Australian PBS criteria. In adjusted analysis, the likelihood of remaining untreated despite eligibility was higher among women (aOR 1.59, 95% CI 1.34, 1.88) and younger participants (age 41-60 vs. > 60 years: aOR 1.51, 95% CI 1.01-2.28; ≤ 40 vs. > 60 years: aOR 2.65, 95% CI 1.60, 4.40). The proportion untreated despite eligibility increased to 13% when applying 2024 World Health Organisation guidelines and ranged 2%-44% based on other guidelines, highlighting the variability across the guidelines. In conclusion, among REACH-B participants, most eligible individuals receive treatment. Lower treatment uptake among women and younger people despite eligibility partly reflects lower threshold for elevated ALT in women, and clinician determination of non-CHB related ALT elevation. As treatment guidelines evolve and eligibility criteria broaden, monitoring treatment patterns is important.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess treatment eligibility and uptake among people with chronic hepatitis B in Australia, highlighting the variability in treatment criteria across different guidelines.
Design and population
The REACH-B study recruited a large cohort of people with chronic hepatitis B managed through a national network of healthcare services. Participants were categorised into three groups: those receiving antiviral therapy, untreated despite eligibility, and untreated and ineligible.
Core methods
Treatment eligibility was determined using Australian Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme (PBS) criteria and international clinical guidelines. Participants were categorised into three groups based on their treatment status and eligibility.
Main results
Among 3648 participants, 47% were on antiviral therapy, 45% were untreated and ineligible, and 7% were untreated despite eligibility based on Australian PBS criteria. The proportion untreated despite eligibility increased to 13% when applying 2024 World Health Organisation guidelines.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the variability in treatment eligibility across different guidelines and the impact of demographic factors on treatment uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the current treatment patterns and disparities in care for people with chronic hepatitis B, which can inform future surveillance and targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a large cohort but relies on self-reported data and adjusted analyses, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

用替诺福韦或恩替卡韦治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者发生肝细胞癌的风险比较:一项基于人群的连锁研究。

Comparative risk of hepatocellular carcinoma among people with chronic hepatitis B treated with tenofovir or entecavir: A population-based linkage study.
作者： Yarrow Ruane, Shane Tillakeratne, Marion Barault, Heather Valerio, Marianne Martinello, Jessica Howell, Mark Danta, Jacob George, Purnima Bhat, Gregory J Dore
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肝细胞癌 (HCC) 发病率 和 死亡率 在澳大利亚和全球呈上升趋势。抗病毒药物 疗法恩替卡韦 (ETV) 和富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 广泛用于治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 和降低 HCC 风险,但其相对 HCC 预防作用仍然存在不确定性。
设计与对象
我们使用关联的公共卫生数据集在澳大利亚新南威尔士州进行了一项基于人群的回顾性 队列研究 研究:10,698 名慢性乙型肝炎患者在接受至少 12 个月的 ETV (n=7,139) 或 TDF (n=3,559) 稳定 抗病毒药物 治疗后符合资格。随访时间为 2007 年至 2022 年。
核心方法
计算了 HCC 的粗年 发病率 率和调整后的 Cox 比例风险比。中位随访时间为 86.3 个月,其中接受 TDF 和 ETV 的参与者分别为 89.2 个月和 82.3 个月。
主要结果
During follow-up, 300 (2.8%) participants were diagnosed with HCC, an incidence of 4.12 per 1000 person years. Participants treated with TDF had a lower hazard ratio for HCC (aHR: 0.61, 95% CI: 0.46-0.82) compared to those treated with ETV.
解释与创新
与 HCC 风险增加相关的基线变量是年龄较大、男性、饮酒障碍和失代偿性肝硬化病史。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持在临床上合适的情况下考虑将 TDF 作为首选 抗病毒药物,并强调需要制定 HCC 筛查指南来考虑 治疗 状态以及已确定的风险因素,例如年龄较大和酒精使用障碍。
局限与证据强度
由于 治疗 不是随机的,并且无法完全减轻残余混杂因素,因此需要进一步的前瞻性研究来证实这种关联。提供的证据未报告合理的高/中/低评估。

Comparative risk of hepatocellular carcinoma among people with chronic hepatitis B treated with tenofovir or entecavir: A population-based linkage study.

Authors: Yarrow Ruane, Shane Tillakeratne, Marion Barault, Heather Valerio, Marianne Martinello, Jessica Howell, Mark Danta, Jacob George, Purnima Bhat, Gregory J Dore
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) incidence and mortality are rising in Australia and globally. Antiviral therapies entecavir (ETV) and tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) are widely used to treat chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and reduce HCC risk, but uncertainty persists regarding their relative HCC prevention impact. This study characterises HCC incidence among people with CHB treated with ETV or TDF, including sub-populations recommended for HCC surveillance. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective population-based cohort study in New South Wales, Australia, using linked public health datasets: 10,698 people with CHB were eligible after receiving at least 12 months of stable antiviral therapy with ETV (n=7,139) or TDF (n=3,559). Follow-up occurred from 2007-2022. Crude annual incidence rates and adjusted Cox proportional hazard ratios were calculated for HCC. RESULTS: Median follow-up was 86.3 months, including 89.2 months and 82.3 months among participants receiving TDF and ETV, respectively. During follow-up, 300 (2.8%) participants were diagnosed with HCC, an incidence of 4.12 per 1000 person years. Participants treated with TDF had a lower hazard ratio for HCC (aHR: 0.61, 95% CI: 0.46-0.82) compared to those treated with ETV. Baseline variables associated with increased HCC risk were older age (reference <50 years: 50-59 years aHR: 2.15, 95% CI: 1.45-3.18; 60-69 years aHR: 3.42, 95% CI: 2.38-4.91; 70-79 years aHR: 4.28, 95% CI: 2.89-6.34; ≥80 years aHR: 3.95, 95% CI: 2.51-6.22), male sex (aHR: 3.14; 95% CI: 2.31-4.25), alcohol use disorder (aHR: 2.33; 95% CI: 1.28-4.25), and history of decompensated cirrhosis (aHR: 2.28; 95% CI: 1.27-4.07). CONCLUSION: In people with chronic hepatitis B in Australia on stable antiviral therapy hepatocellular carcinoma risk was lower among those receiving entecavir than those receiving tenofovir disoproxil fumarate, although confounding remains a potential explanation. IMPACT AND IMPLICATIONS: Comparative HCC risk between entecavir and tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) in chronic hepatitis B remains uncertain outside East Asian cohorts. This population-based linkage study of people with chronic hepatitis B in Australia found TDF was associated with lower HCC risk than entecavir, a finding relevant to clinicians and researchers working on HCC prevention. These results support consideration of TDF as a preferred antiviral where clinically appropriate and highlight the need for HCC screening guidelines to account for treatment status alongside established risk factors such as older age and alcohol use disorder. As treatment was not randomised and residual confounding could not be completely mitigated, further prospective studies are needed to confirm this association.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) incidence and mortality are rising in Australia and globally. Antiviral therapies entecavir (ETV) and tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) are widely used to treat chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and reduce HCC risk, but uncertainty persists regarding their relative HCC prevention impact.

2018 年 1 月至 2026 年 6 月美国乙型肝炎出生剂量 疫苗接种。

Hepatitis B Birth Dose Vaccination in the United States, January 2018-June 2026.
作者： Nina B. Masters, Brianna M Goodwin Cartwright, Karen Gilbert Farrar, Johnathan M. Lancaster, Demetre C Daskalakis
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估基于孕产妇 HBV 状况的更新乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 政策对美国新生儿 疫苗接种 率的影响。由于乙型肝炎预防建议不断变化,这一点很重要。
设计与对象
The study analyzed data from January 2018 through June 2026 using a subset of Truveta Data, which includes over 140 million people with linked mother-child dyads. It focused on infants born to mothers with HBV test results.
核心方法
研究人员使用电子健康记录数据库来描述新生儿 HBV 疫苗接种 率,并根据孕产妇 HBV 检测结果报告这些比率,重点关注 2018 年 1 月至 2026 年 6 月期间。
主要结果
2018 年新生儿 疫苗接种 率为 76.4%,2020 年至 2022 年平均为 80.0%,但 2026 年下降至 65.6%。
解释与创新
作者将 疫苗接种 费率下降解释为更新政策的潜在后果,强调需要进一步调查其实施和有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,传播 政策的变化可能会影响覆盖率,这可能会对旨在预防乙型肝炎 传播 的公共卫生策略产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于摘要数据,这可能会限制分析的深度和所得出结论的可信度。

Hepatitis B Birth Dose Vaccination in the United States, January 2018-June 2026.

Authors: Nina B. Masters, Brianna M Goodwin Cartwright, Karen Gilbert Farrar, Johnathan M. Lancaster, Demetre C Daskalakis
Original abstract (English)
AbstractOn December 5, 2025, the Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices voted to replace its 1991 recommendation for universal newborn hepatitis B vaccination (HBV) at birth with vaccination based on maternal HBV status. Using a subset of Truveta Data, an electronic health record database with deterministically linked mother-child dyads that includes data on over 140 million people with geographic representation across the United States, we describe newborn HBV vaccination rates and report rates based on maternal HBV test results. From January 2018 through June 2026, we identified 1,338,961 infants born to 1,075,767 mothers; 90.5% of pregnant mothers had evidence of an HBV test, of which 0.18% were HBsAg-positive. Newborn vaccination rates were 76.4% in 2018 and a mean of 80.0% from 2020 to 2022, then began to decline in 2023, falling to 65.6% in 2026.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of the updated hepatitis B vaccination policy based on maternal HBV status on newborn vaccination rates in the United States. This is important due to the changing recommendations for hepatitis B prevention.
Design and population
The study analyzed data from January 2018 through June 2026 using a subset of Truveta Data, which includes over 140 million people with linked mother-child dyads. It focused on infants born to mothers with HBV test results.
Core methods
The researchers used an electronic health record database to describe newborn HBV vaccination rates and report these rates based on maternal HBV test results, focusing on the period from January 2018 through June 2026.
Main results
Newborn vaccination rates were 76.4% in 2018 and averaged 80.0% from 2020 to 2022, but declined to 65.6% in 2026.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the decline in vaccination rates as a potential consequence of the updated policy, highlighting the need for further investigation into its implementation and effectiveness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that changes in vaccination policy may affect coverage rates, which could have implications for public health strategies aimed at preventing hepatitis B transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only data, which may limit the depth of analysis and confidence in the conclusions drawn.

发现 XT1061(一种 HBV 衣壳组装调节剂)作为 HBV 感染临床 治疗 的有前景的治疗候选药物。

Discovery of XT1061, a HBV Capsid Assembly Modulator, as a Promising Therapeutic Candidate for Clinical Treatment of HBV Infection.
作者： Weibo Guo, Chenghao Wang, Chi Zhang, Kangjia Sheng, Dengke Zhang, Xiuhong Wen, Yuan Wang, Weidong Yue, Yungen Xu
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是一种尚未解决的主要传染病。患者通常需要终生服用核苷(酸)类似物(NA)或干扰素,但很少达到功能性治愈。最近的研究表明,HBV核心组装调节剂(CAM)与NAs和干扰素联合使用时,可以更有效地降低血浆中的HBsAg,从而促进功能性治愈。在这项研究中,我们通过之前报道的GLP-26的结构优化发现了化合物39(命名为XT1061),其在体外表现出有效的抗HBV活性(EC50 = 1.0 ± 0.1 nM)。在小鼠 AAV/HBV 模型中,XT1061 诱导 HBV DNA 快速减少,并在整个 治疗 期间持续清除病毒 DNA。值得注意的是,XT1061 与甲磺酸普拉德福韦 (PDV) 的联合治疗与单一疗法相比显示出显着增强的疗效。进一步的临床前评估和I期临床试验(NCT06280534)证实XT1061具有良好的药物样特性和较低的安全风险。总的来说,XT1061代表了一种有前途的抗乙型肝炎治疗候选药物,为慢性乙型肝炎感染的临床管理提供了可行的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是一种尚未解决的主要传染病。患者通常需要终生服用核苷(酸)类似物(NA)或干扰素,但很少达到功能性治愈。
设计与对象
本研究旨在通过对先前报道的化合物进行结构优化,发现治疗 HBV 感染的新候选药物。该研究的重点是鉴定具有有效体外抗乙肝病毒活性的化合物。
核心方法
该研究涉及先前报道的 GLP-26 的结构优化,以发现化合物 39 (XT1061)。使用 EC50 测量值测试了该化合物的体外抗 HBV 活性。
主要结果
在这项研究中,我们通过之前报道的GLP-26的结构优化发现了化合物39(命名为XT1061),其在体外表现出有效的抗HBV活性(EC50 = 1.0 ± 0.1 nM)。
解释与创新
与单一疗法相比,XT1061与甲磺酸普拉德福韦(PDV)的联合治疗显示出显着增强的疗效。
科研与公卫意义
XT1061代表了一种有前途的抗乙肝治疗候选药物,为慢性乙肝感染的临床管理提供了可行的策略。
局限与证据强度
进一步的临床前评估和I期临床试验(NCT06280534)证实XT1061具有良好的药物样特性和较低的安全风险。

Discovery of XT1061, a HBV Capsid Assembly Modulator, as a Promising Therapeutic Candidate for Clinical Treatment of HBV Infection.

Authors: Weibo Guo, Chenghao Wang, Chi Zhang, Kangjia Sheng, Dengke Zhang, Xiuhong Wen, Yuan Wang, Weidong Yue, Yungen Xu
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) persists as a major unresolved infectious disease. Patients typically require lifelong administration of nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) or interferons but seldom achieve functional cure. Recent studies indicate that HBV core assembly modulators (CAMs), when combined with NAs and interferons, can more effectively reduce HBsAg in plasma, thereby facilitating functional cure. In this research, we discovered compound 39 (designated XT1061) through structural optimization of previously reported GLP-26, which exhibited potent anti-HBV activity in vitro (EC50 = 1.0 ± 0.1 nM). In the murine AAV/HBV model, XT1061 induced rapid reduction of HBV DNA and sustained viral DNA clearance throughout treatment. Notably, Combination therapy of XT1061 with pradefovir mesylate (PDV) exhibited significantly enhanced efficacy compared with monotherapy. Further preclinical evaluation and Phase I clinical trials (NCT06280534) confirmed that XT1061 possesses favorable drug-like properties and low safety risks. Collectively, XT1061 represents a promising anti-HBV therapeutic candidate, providing a viable strategy for the clinical management of chronic HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) persists as a major unresolved infectious disease. Patients typically require lifelong administration of nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) or interferons but seldom achieve functional cure.
Design and population
This study aimed to discover a new therapeutic candidate for HBV infection through structural optimization of previously reported compounds. The research focused on identifying compounds with potent anti-HBV activity in vitro.
Core methods
The study involved structural optimization of previously reported GLP-26 to discover compound 39 (XT1061). The compound was tested for its anti-HBV activity in vitro using EC50 measurements.
Main results
In this research, we discovered compound 39 (designated XT1061) through structural optimization of previously reported GLP-26, which exhibited potent anti-HBV activity in vitro (EC50 = 1.0 ± 0.1 nM).
Interpretation and novelty
Combination therapy of XT1061 with pradefovir mesylate (PDV) exhibited significantly enhanced efficacy compared with monotherapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
XT1061 represents a promising anti-HBV therapeutic candidate, providing a viable strategy for the clinical management of chronic HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further preclinical evaluation and Phase I clinical trials (NCT06280534) confirmed that XT1061 possesses favorable drug-like properties and low safety risks.

识字成人的 乙型肝炎病毒 意识和预防行为:巴西和秘鲁的比较横断面研究

Awareness of Hepatitis B Virus and Preventive Behaviour among Literate Adults: A Comparative Cross-Sectional Study of Brazil and Peru
作者： Patrícia Ferreira, Mateo Sánchez
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是一个重大的全球健康问题,患病率 和 死亡率 发病率很高。该研究旨在缩小巴西和秘鲁识字成年人对乙型肝炎病毒认识的知识差距。
设计与对象
采用比较横断面调查设计,通过从巴西(圣保罗和巴伊亚州)和秘鲁(利马和库斯科地区)城市地区按比例多阶段分层抽样选出来自每个国家的 783 名参与者样本(总 N = 1,566)。
核心方法
使用统一的三语工具——乙型肝炎认知调查问卷——跨文化版本(HBAQ-CC)——由李克特五点量表上的 34 个项目组成。调查问卷经过五位专家评审和回译验证,两国Cronbach's alpha ≥ 0.70证实了其可靠性。使用描述性统计、独立样本 t 检验、单向和双向方差分析、卡方检验和 0.05 显着性水平的逻辑回归分析数据。
主要结果
该研究直接观察了巴西和秘鲁识字成年人对乙型肝炎的认知水平,并进行了具体的统计分析,以比较人口和信息因素的认知得分。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为强调需要采取有针对性的公共卫生干预措施来提高乙型肝炎的认识,特别是在免费筛查和 疫苗接种 服务的使用率仍然较低的地区。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为拉丁美洲国家的公共卫生策略提供参考,以提高乙肝病毒意识并提高筛查和 疫苗接种 服务的采用率。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,不允许进行因果推断,而且其重点关注城市人口,这可能会限制普遍性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Awareness of Hepatitis B Virus and Preventive Behaviour among Literate Adults: A Comparative Cross-Sectional Study of Brazil and Peru

Authors: Patrícia Ferreira, Mateo Sánchez
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains one of the leading causes of liver cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma worldwide, with an estimated 254 million people living with chronic infection and approximately 1.1 million deaths annually (World Health Organization, 2024). In Latin America, awareness of the disease remains suboptimal, with a persistent gap between the availability of free screening and vaccination services within public health systems and the actual uptake of these services (Bittencourt et al., 2023). Objective: This study aimed to measure the level of awareness of hepatitis B virus among literate adults in Brazil and Peru, to compare the two countries in terms of overall awareness, knowledge of transmission routes, and vaccine literacy, and to examine the effects of gender, educational attainment, religious affiliation, and information sources on awareness scores. Methods: A comparative cross-sectional survey design was employed. The sample comprised 783 participants from each country (total N = 1,566), selected through proportional multistage stratified sampling from urban areas in Brazil (São Paulo and Bahia states) and Peru (Lima and Cusco regions). A uniform trilingual instrument—the Hepatitis B Awareness Questionnaire–Cross-Cultural Version (HBAQ-CC)—consisting of 34 items on a five-point Likert scale was administered following validity testing (five-expert review and back-translation) and reliability testing (Cronbach's alpha ≥ 0.70 in both countries). Data were analysed using descriptive statistics, independent-samples t-tests, one-way and two-way analysis of variance, chi-square tests, and logistic regression at a 0.05 significance level.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a significant global health issue, with high prevalence and mortality rates. The study aimed to address the knowledge gap regarding awareness of HBV among literate adults in Brazil and Peru.
Design and population
A comparative cross-sectional survey design was employed, with a sample of 783 participants from each country (total N = 1,566), selected through proportional multistage stratified sampling from urban areas in Brazil (São Paulo and Bahia states) and Peru (Lima and Cusco regions).
Core methods
A uniform trilingual instrument—the Hepatitis B Awareness Questionnaire–Cross-Cultural Version (HBAQ-CC)—was used, consisting of 34 items on a five-point Likert scale. The questionnaire was validated through five-expert review and back-translation, and its reliability was confirmed with Cronbach's alpha ≥ 0.70 in both countries. Data were analysed using descriptive statistics, independent-samples t-tests, one-way and two-way analysis of variance, chi-square tests, and logistic regression at a 0.05 significance level.
Main results
The study directly observed the level of awareness of HBV among literate adults in Brazil and Peru, with specific statistical analyses conducted to compare awareness scores across demographic and informational factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the need for targeted public health interventions to improve HBV awareness, particularly in regions where uptake of free screening and vaccination services remains low.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies to enhance HBV awareness and improve the uptake of screening and vaccination services in Latin American countries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not allow for causal inferences, and its focus on urban populations, which may limit generalisability. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

针对乙型肝炎表面抗原主要免疫逃逸变体的 突变 包含多表位 疫苗 的计算设计

Computational design of a mutation-inclusive multi-epitope vaccine targeting major immune-escape variants of hepatitis B surface antigen
作者： Seid Mohammed Abdu, Ebrahim Msaye Assefa, Biniam Moges Esekeziyaw
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是全球主要的健康负担,每年导致数百万人因慢性肝脏并发症死​​亡。目前,乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)内,特别是“a”决定簇区域内免疫逃逸突变的出现,继续对现有疫苗的有效性构成挑战。因此,本研究采用计算机免疫信息学方法设计了包含 突变 的多表位 疫苗,靶向全球循环的 HBV 变异体的“a”决定簇区域。该研究通过结构化的十步免疫信息学流程进行,包括:抗原序列检索、共有序列识别、细胞毒性 T 淋巴细胞 (CTL)、辅助 T 淋巴细胞 (HTL) 和 B 细胞表位预测、多表位 疫苗 构建、结构预测和验证、理化和免疫特性评估、与免疫受体的分子对接、免疫应答 模拟和密码子优化,然后进行计算机模拟克隆。从国家生物技术信息中心 (NCBI) 数据库中检索了总共 78 个 HBV 表面蛋白序列,并使用多重序列比对进行分析,以确定共有序列。共有序列鉴定后,根据表位与主要组织相容性复合体 (MHC) 分子的结合亲和力来预测和选择表位。所选表位的估计全球群体覆盖率为 99.55%,并与佐剂和接头组装以构建 939 个氨基酸的多表位 疫苗 (MEV)。使用 AlphaFold3 对 MEV 进行结构建模揭示了稳定的球状-柔性混合架构。该模型使用 Galaxy Web 服务器进一步完善,在拉马钱德兰 (Ramachandran) 青睐的地区显示出 97.01% 的残留物,MolProbity 得分为 1.22。分子对接预测与 Toll 样受体 4 (TLR4) 的良好相互作用,HDOCK 评分为 - 323.89,ClusPro 能量评分为 - 1715.4 kcal/mol,同时预测与主要组织相容性复合物 II 类 (MHC-II) 和 Toll 样受体 2 (TLR2) 受体的相互作用。计算免疫模拟预测了 Th1 偏向和体液 免疫应答,包括免疫球蛋白产生增加、细胞因子激活和持续免疫记忆发展。密码子优化实现密码子适应指数(CAI)为1.0,GC含量为66.67%。计算机克隆进一步表明了在大肠杆菌表达系统中表达该构建体的理论上的可行性。设计的多表位 疫苗 构建体表现出良好的预测结构特征、计算评估的安全相关特性以及广泛的估计人群覆盖范围。这些发现支持进一步研究该构建体作为针对 HBV 免疫逃逸变体的候选者。需要进行体外和体内实验验证以确定其免疫原性、安全性和保护功效。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是全球主要的健康负担,每年导致数百万人因慢性肝脏并发症死​​亡。目前,乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)内,特别是“a”决定簇区域内免疫逃逸突变的出现,继续对现有疫苗的有效性构成挑战。
设计与对象
本研究采用计算机免疫信息学方法设计了包含 突变 的多表位 疫苗,针对全球循环的 HBV 变异体的“a”决定簇区域。从国家生物技术信息中心 (NCBI) 数据库中检索了总共 78 个 HBV 表面蛋白序列,并使用多重序列比对进行分析,以确定共有序列。
核心方法
该研究通过结构化的十步免疫信息学流程进行,包括:抗原序列检索、共有序列识别、细胞毒性 T 淋巴细胞 (CTL)、辅助 T 淋巴细胞 (HTL) 和 B 细胞表位预测、多表位 疫苗 构建、结构预测和验证、理化和免疫特性评估、与免疫受体的分子对接、免疫应答 模拟和密码子优化,然后进行计算机模拟克隆。该模型使用 Galaxy Web 服务器进一步完善,在拉马钱德兰 (Ramachandran) 青睐的地区显示出 97.01% 的残留物,MolProbity 得分为 1.22。
主要结果
所选表位的估计全球群体覆盖率为 99.55%,并与佐剂和接头组装以构建 939 个氨基酸的多表位 疫苗 (MEV)。使用 AlphaFold3 对 MEV 进行结构建模揭示了稳定的球状-柔性混合架构。计算免疫模拟预测了 Th1 偏向和体液 免疫应答,包括免疫球蛋白产生增加、细胞因子激活和持续免疫记忆发展。设计的多表位 疫苗 构建体表现出良好的预测结构特征、计算评估的安全相关特性以及广泛的估计人群覆盖范围。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,设计的 疫苗 构建体可能会引发针对 HBV 免疫逃逸变异的广泛 免疫应答。这些发现支持进一步研究该构建体作为针对 HBV 免疫逃逸变体的候选者。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为 疫苗 的开发奠定了基础,该药物可以解决 HBV 中免疫逃逸 突变 的挑战,有可能改善全球 疫苗接种 策略并减轻与 HBV 感染相关的慢性肝脏并发症的负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于计算模型,不包括实验验证,这限制了对 疫苗 免疫原性、安全性和保护功效的信心。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于计算机预测,而没有体外或体内测试。

Computational design of a mutation-inclusive multi-epitope vaccine targeting major immune-escape variants of hepatitis B surface antigen

Authors: Seid Mohammed Abdu, Ebrahim Msaye Assefa, Biniam Moges Esekeziyaw
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major global health burden, responsible for the deaths of millions annually due to chronic liver complications. Currently, the emergence of immune-escape mutations within the hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), particularly within the “a” determinant region, continues to pose a challenge to the effectiveness of existing vaccines. Therefore, this study employed an in silico immunoinformatics approach to design a mutation-inclusive multi-epitope vaccine targeting globally circulating HBV variants at the “a” determinant region. The study was conducted through a structured ten-step immunoinformatics pipeline, comprising: antigen sequence retrieval, consensus sequence identification, prediction of cytotoxic T lymphocyte (CTL), helper T lymphocyte (HTL), and B-cell epitopes, multi-epitope vaccine construction, structural prediction and validation, assessment of physicochemical and immunological properties, molecular docking with immune receptors, immune response simulation, and codon optimization followed by in silico cloning. A total of 78 HBV surface protein sequences were retrieved from the National Center for Biotechnology Information (NCBI) database and analyzed using multiple sequence alignment to identify a consensus sequence. Following consensus sequence identification, epitopes were predicted and selected based on their binding affinity to major histocompatibility complex (MHC) molecules. The selected epitopes showed an estimated global population coverage of 99.55% and were assembled with adjuvant and linkers to construct a 939-amino-acid multi-epitope vaccine (MEV). Structural modeling of the MEV using AlphaFold3 revealed a stable globular-flexible hybrid architecture. The model was further refined using the Galaxy Web server and exhibited 97.01% of residues in favored Ramachandran regions and a MolProbity score of 1.22. Molecular docking predicted favorable interaction with Toll-like receptor 4 (TLR4), with an HDOCK score of − 323.89 and a ClusPro energy score of − 1715.4 kcal/mol, alongside predicted interactions with major histocompatibility complex class II (MHC-II) and Toll-like receptor 2 (TLR2) receptors. Computational immune simulations predicted Th1-skewed and humoral immune responses, including increased immunoglobulin production, cytokine activation, and sustained immune memory development. Codon optimization achieved a codon adaptation index (CAI) of 1.0 and a GC content of 66.67%. In silico cloning further indicated the theoretical feasibility of expressing the construct in an Escherichia coli expression system. The designed multi-epitope vaccine construct demonstrated favorable predicted structural characteristics, computationally assessed safety-related properties, and broad estimated population coverage. These findings support further investigation of the construct as a candidate targeting HBV immune-escape variants. Experimental validation in vitro and in vivo is required to establish its immunogenicity, safety, and protective efficacy.

Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major global health burden, responsible for the deaths of millions annually due to chronic liver complications. Currently, the emergence of immune-escape mutations within the hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), particularly within the “a” determinant region, continues to pose a challenge to the effectiveness of existing vaccines.
Design and population
This study employed an in silico immunoinformatics approach to design a mutation-inclusive multi-epitope vaccine targeting globally circulating HBV variants at the “a” determinant region. A total of 78 HBV surface protein sequences were retrieved from the National Center for Biotechnology Information (NCBI) database and analyzed using multiple sequence alignment to identify a consensus sequence.
Core methods
The study was conducted through a structured ten-step immunoinformatics pipeline, comprising: antigen sequence retrieval, consensus sequence identification, prediction of cytotoxic T lymphocyte (CTL), helper T lymphocyte (HTL), and B-cell epitopes, multi-epitope vaccine construction, structural prediction and validation, assessment of physicochemical and immunological properties, molecular docking with immune receptors, immune response simulation, and codon optimization followed by in silico cloning. The model was further refined using the Galaxy Web server and exhibited 97.01% of residues in favored Ramachandran regions and a MolProbity score of 1.22.
Main results
The selected epitopes showed an estimated global population coverage of 99.55% and were assembled with adjuvant and linkers to construct a 939-amino-acid multi-epitope vaccine (MEV). Structural modeling of the MEV using AlphaFold3 revealed a stable globular-flexible hybrid architecture. Computational immune simulations predicted Th1-skewed and humoral immune responses, including increased immunoglobulin production, cytokine activation, and sustained immune memory development. The designed multi-epitope vaccine construct demonstrated favorable predicted structural characteristics, computationally assessed safety-related properties, and broad estimated population coverage.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the designed vaccine construct could potentially elicit broad immune responses against HBV immune-escape variants. These findings support further investigation of the construct as a candidate targeting HBV immune-escape variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for the development of a vaccine that could address the challenge of immune-escape mutations in HBV, potentially improving global vaccination strategies and reducing the burden of chronic liver complications associated with HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on computational models and does not include experimental validation, which limits the confidence in the vaccine's immunogenicity, safety, and protective efficacy. The evidence is moderate as it relies on in silico predictions without in vitro or in vivo testing.

科特迪瓦献血者医疗随访中心 (CMSDS) 对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性献血者中的 δ 型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 进行 患病率 跟踪

Prevalence of hepatitis delta virus (HDV) among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) in Cte dIvoire
作者： Renaud Dechi Jacques, Jeanne Armande Ake Aya, Kabore Saydou, Philippe N'Din Jean-Louis, Moussa Salimata, Oura Doris, Dembélé Bamory, Siransy Kouabla Liliane, Mamadou Sekongo Yassongui, Saydou Kaboré
摘要中文翻译慢性丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染会加剧 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关的肝病。在科特迪瓦,丁型肝炎病毒在乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性的献血者中没有系统地进行筛查,大多数地区的估计依赖于抗丁型肝炎病毒 血清学,这表明暴露但不活跃复制。本研究旨在利用巢式逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 确定在科特迪瓦国家输血中心 (CNTSCI) 的献血者医疗随访中心 (CMSDS) 随访的 HBsAg 阳性献血者中 HDV RNA 复制阳性情况。2021 年 3 月进行了一项基于非概率便利样本的横断面研究。通过针对两个基因组区域(核苷酸 888-1122 和 313-485)的巢式 RT-PCR 检测 HDV RNA。在 51 名捐赠者中,75% 是男性 (n = 38),平均年龄为 37 ± 9 岁。43 名捐献者 (84%) 可获得 HBV DNA 结果。其中,可检测到 HBV 病毒血症的 HDV RNA 阳性率为 88.9% (32/36),而病毒载量未检测到的患者 HDV RNA 阳性率为 100% (7/7),总体阳性率为 90.7% (39/43)。该数字仅适用于具有可用病毒载量数据的捐赠者,而不是 51 名捐赠者的全部 队列研究。在接受富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 且 HBV 病毒血症受到抑制的捐赠者中也检测到了 HDV RNA。考虑到已经接受后续护理的捐赠者的方便样本,研究结果是假设生成而不是人口水平的估计。这些结果支持在慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者的随访过程中进行更大规模的代表性研究和系统性丁型肝炎病毒筛查。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染会加剧 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关的肝病。本研究旨在确定 HBsAg 阳性献血者的 HDV RNA 复制阳性率。
设计与对象
2021 年 3 月进行了一项基于非概率便利样本的横断面研究。在 51 名捐赠者中,75% 为男性 (n = 38),平均年龄为 37 ± 9 岁。
核心方法
HDV RNA 通过针对两个基因组区域(核苷酸 888-1122 和 313-485)的巢式逆转录聚合酶链式反应 (RT-PCR) 进行检测。
主要结果
43 名捐献者 (84%) 可获得 HBV DNA 结果。其中,可检测到乙肝病毒血症者的 HDV RNA 阳性率为 88.9% (32/36),而病毒载量未检测到的患者 HDV RNA 阳性率为 100% (7/7),总体阳性率为 90.7% (39/43)。
解释与创新
这些结果支持在慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者的随访过程中进行更大规模的代表性研究和系统性丁型肝炎病毒筛查。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了对慢性 HBV 感染者进行系统 HDV 筛查的重要性,以更好地了解 HDV RNA 复制的 患病率 及其对肝病进展的影响。
局限与证据强度
考虑到已经接受后续护理的捐赠者的方便样本,研究结果是假设生成而不是人口水平的估计。

Prevalence of hepatitis delta virus (HDV) among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) in Cte dIvoire

Authors: Renaud Dechi Jacques, Jeanne Armande Ake Aya, Kabore Saydou, Philippe N'Din Jean-Louis, Moussa Salimata, Oura Doris, Dembélé Bamory, Siransy Kouabla Liliane, Mamadou Sekongo Yassongui, Saydou Kaboré
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis delta virus (HDV) infection exacerbates hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related liver disease. In Côte d’Ivoire, HDV is systematic not systematically screened among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive blood donors, and most regional estimates rely on anti-HDV serology, which indicates exposure but not active replication. This study aimed to determine HDV RNA replication positivity among HBsAg-positive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) of the National Blood Transfusion Centre of Côte d’Ivoire (CNTSCI), using nested reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR). A cross-sectional study based on a non-probability convenience sample was conducted in March 2021. HDV RNA was detected by nested RT-PCR targeting two genomic regions (nucleotides 888–1122 and 313–485). Of 51 donors included, 75% were male (n = 38) and and mean age was 37 ± 9 years. HBV DNA results were available for 43 donors (84%). Among them, HDV RNA positivity was 88.9% (32/36) in those with detectable HBV viraemia and 100% (7/7) in those with undetectable viral load, giving an overall positivity 90.7% (39/43). This figure applies only to donors with available viral load data not the entire cohort of 51 donors. HDV RNA was also detected in donors receiving tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) with suppressed HBV viraemia. Given the convenience sample of donors already in follow-up care, findings are hypothesis-generating rather than population-level estimates. These results support larger representative studies and systematic HDV screening during follow-up of individuals with chronic HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis delta virus (HDV) infection exacerbates hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related liver disease. This study aimed to determine HDV RNA replication positivity among HBsAg-positive blood donors.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study based on a non-probability convenience sample was conducted in March 2021. Of 51 donors included, 75% were male (n = 38) and the mean age was 37 ± 9 years.
Core methods
HDV RNA was detected by nested reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) targeting two genomic regions (nucleotides 888–1122 and 313–485).
Main results
HBV DNA results were available for 43 donors (84%). Among them, HDV RNA positivity was 88.9% (32/36) in those with detectable HBV viraemia and 100% (7/7) in those with undetectable viral load, giving an overall positivity of 90.7% (39/43).
Interpretation and novelty
These results support larger representative studies and systematic HDV screening during follow-up of individuals with chronic HBV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of systematic HDV screening among individuals with chronic HBV infection to better understand the prevalence of HDV RNA replication and its implications for liver disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
Given the convenience sample of donors already in follow-up care, findings are hypothesis-generating rather than population-level estimates.

公众对肝细胞癌危险因素的认识较低。

Low public awareness of hepatocellular carcinoma risk factors.
作者： Wei Yuan, Peyvand Parhizkar, Mohammad Saeid Rezaee-Zavareh, Osama Khattab, Li Li, Hyun-Seok Kim, Alexander Kuo, Walid Ayoub, Yun Wang, Aarshi Vipani
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估公众对与 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和饮酒相关的致癌风险的认识,因为这些因素对于预防 HCC 至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究使用了具有全国代表性的 2024 年健康信息国家趋势调查 (HINTS 7),分别有 6,711、6,661 和 6,793 名受访者了解 HBV、HCV 和酒精意识结果。
核心方法
使用对缺失协变量进行多重插补的调查加权逻辑回归来评估社会人口特征和数字行为与风险意识之间的关联。
主要结果
总体认知度较低:只有18.0%的人认为乙型肝炎病毒是致癌物,16.7%的人认为丙型肝炎病毒是致癌物,37.1%的人认为酒精是致癌物。
解释与创新
作者解释说,公众对乙型肝炎病毒、丙型肝炎病毒和酒精相关癌症风险的认识仍然较低,这凸显了有针对性的沟通策略的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为制定有针对性的、对识字率敏感的沟通策略提供了信息,以提高公众对 HBV、HCV 和酒精相关癌症风险的认识,并支持 HCC 预防工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对自我报告数据的依赖和潜在的无法测量的混杂因素而受到限制,但全国代表性的样本和多变量调整为结果提供了适度的可信度。

Low public awareness of hepatocellular carcinoma risk factors.

Authors: Wei Yuan, Peyvand Parhizkar, Mohammad Saeid Rezaee-Zavareh, Osama Khattab, Li Li, Hyun-Seok Kim, Alexander Kuo, Walid Ayoub, Yun Wang, Aarshi Vipani
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND & AIMS: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) prevention can be mediated through hepatitis B virus (HBV) vaccination, hepatitis C virus (HCV) treatment, and alcohol control. We assessed public awareness of their carcinogenic risks and association with digital information seeking behaviors. METHODS: We analyzed the nationally representative 2024 Health Information National Trends Survey (HINTS 7), including 6,711, 6,661, and 6,793 respondents with observed HBV, HCV, and alcohol awareness outcomes, respectively. Survey-weighted logistic regression with multiple imputation of missing covariates assessed associations with sociodemographic characteristics and digital behaviors. RESULTS: Overall awareness was low: only 18.0%, 16.7%, and 37.1% recognized HBV, HCV, and alcohol as carcinogenic, respectively. Older age was independently associated with lower alcohol-related awareness; compared with adults aged 18-34 years, those aged 35-49 years (aOR 0.67, 95% CI 0.48-0.93), 50-64 years (aOR 0.56, 95% CI 0.40-0.78), and ≥65 years (aOR 0.49, 95% CI 0.36-0.68) had lower awareness. Higher educational attainment was associated with greater alcohol-related awareness (aOR 1.86, 95% CI 1.47-2.34). Online health information seeking was associated with greater awareness of HCV-related (aOR 1.74, 95% CI 1.17-2.59) and alcohol-related cancer risk (aOR 1.95, 95% CI 1.44-2.63), but not HBV-related cancer risk (aOR 1.40, 95% CI 0.95-2.08). However, social media use was not independently associated with risk awareness in multivariable models. CONCLUSIONS: Public awareness of HCC risk factors remains low. General social media use was not independently associated with cancer-risk awareness, whereas online health information seeking was associated with greater HCV- and alcohol-related awareness. These findings highlight opportunities for more targeted, literacy-sensitive HCC-prevention communication. IMPACT AND IMPLICATIONS: Public awareness that hepatitis B virus, hepatitis C virus, and alcohol consumption are associated with cancer risk remains low among U.S. Using nationally representative HINTS 7 data, we identified sociodemographic differences in awareness and found that general social media use was not independently associated with awareness after multivariable adjustment, whereas online health information seeking was associated with greater awareness of HCV- and alcohol-related cancer risk. These findings highlight opportunities for targeted, literacy-sensitive communication strategies that facilitate access to credible health information and strengthen HCC prevention efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess public awareness of the carcinogenic risks associated with hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and alcohol consumption, as these factors are critical for HCC prevention.
Design and population
The study used the nationally representative 2024 Health Information National Trends Survey (HINTS 7) with 6,711, 6,661, and 6,793 respondents for HBV, HCV, and alcohol awareness outcomes, respectively.
Core methods
Survey-weighted logistic regression with multiple imputation of missing covariates was used to assess associations between sociodemographic characteristics and digital behaviors with risk awareness.
Main results
Overall awareness was low: only 18.0% recognized HBV as carcinogenic, 16.7% recognized HCV as carcinogenic, and 37.1% recognized alcohol as carcinogenic.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that public awareness of HBV, HCV, and alcohol-related cancer risks remains low, highlighting the need for targeted communication strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of targeted, literacy-sensitive communication strategies to improve public awareness of HBV, HCV, and alcohol-related cancer risks and support HCC prevention efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and the potential for unmeasured confounding, but the nationally representative sample and multivariable adjustment provide moderate confidence in the results.

聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗慢性乙型肝炎血液学不良反应处理专家共识

Expert consensus on the management of hematologic adverse reactions related to pegylated interferon-alpha therapy for chronic hepatitis B
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译摘要 为帮助临床医生合理决策聚乙二醇化干扰素α(PEG-IFN-α)治疗慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)期间血液学不良反应的处理,国家感染性疾病医学中心组织感染性疾病、肝病学、血液学和临床药学专家组,根据现有证据和临床实践,制定本共识。该共识系统地描述了PEG-IFN-α 发病率治疗慢性乙型肝炎期间中性粒细胞减少、血小板减少和贫血的临床特征和机制;分析血细胞减少症与 抗病毒药物 疗效之间的关联;提出治疗前风险分层、标准化监测策略、分级管理方案(包括口服升白细胞剂、重组人粒细胞集落刺激因子、重组人白细胞介素11、重组人血小板生成素等支持治疗)以及针对肝硬化患者、老年人、儿童、肾功能不全患者等特殊人群的管理建议。它强调,轻至中度血细胞减少不应作为剂量减少或治疗停药的唯一原因,而应与治疗疗效指标结合评估。本共识旨在为PEG-IFN-α治疗CHB相关血液学不良反应的规范化管理提供参考,从而提高治疗的完成率和患者安全性。实践指南注册:透明度实践指南注册 (PREPARE-2026CN2145)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决临床医生在治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 聚乙二醇化干扰素-α (PEG-IFN-α) 治疗期间血液学不良反应时面临的挑战。目的是根据当前证据和临床实践就这些不良反应的管理提供共识。
设计与对象
国家感染病医学中心组织感染病学、肝病学、血液学、临床药学专家组成专家组制定本共识。该研究重点关注接受PEG-IFN-α治疗CHB的患者,包括肝硬化患者、老年人、儿童和肾功能不全患者等特殊人群。
核心方法
该共识是通过对 PEG-IFN-α 发病率 治疗 CHB 期间中性粒细胞减少、血小板减少和贫血的临床特征和机制进行系统分析而制定的。它还评估血细胞减少症与 抗病毒药物 疗效之间的关联,并提出标准化监测策略和管理方案。
主要结果
它还分析了血细胞减少症与 抗病毒药物 疗效之间的关联,并提出治疗前风险分层、标准化监测策略和分级管理方案。
解释与创新
作者解释说,轻度至中度血细胞减少不应是减少剂量或 治疗 停药的唯一原因,而应与 治疗 疗效指标一起进行评估。这为管理接受 PEG-IFN-α 治疗的慢性乙型肝炎患者的血液学不良反应提供了一个新的框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为与 PEG-IFN-α 治疗 CHB 相关的血液学不良反应的标准化管理提供信息,从而有可能提高 治疗 的完成率和患者安全性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于专家共识而不是经验数据,这可能会限制证据的强度。然而,对当前证据和临床实践的系统分析为拟议的管理策略提供了中等程度的信心。

Expert consensus on the management of hematologic adverse reactions related to pegylated interferon-alpha therapy for chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
Abstract To assist clinicians in making rational decisions regarding the management of hematologic adverse reactions during pegylated interferon-alpha (PEG-IFN-α) therapy for chronic hepatitis B (CHB), the National Medical Center for Infectious Diseases organized a panel of experts in infectious diseases, hepatology, hematology, and clinical pharmacy to develop this consensus based on current evidence and clinical practice. The consensus systematically describes the incidence, clinical characteristics, and mechanisms of neutropenia, thrombocytopenia, and anemia during PEG-IFN-α treatment for CHB; analyzes the association between cytopenias and antiviral efficacy; and proposes pretreatment risk stratification, standardized monitoring strategies, graded management protocols (including oral leukocyte-raising agents, recombinant human granulocyte colony-stimulating factor, recombinant human interleukin-11, recombinant human thrombopoietin, and other supportive therapies), as well as management recommendations for special populations, including patients with liver cirrhosis, older adults, children, and patients with renal insufficiency. It emphasizes that mild-to-moderate cytopenias should not be used as the sole reason for dose reduction or treatment discontinuation but should be assessed in conjunction with on-treatment efficacy indicators. This consensus aims to provide a reference for the standardized management of hematologic adverse reactions associated with PEG-IFN-α therapy for CHB, thereby improving treatment completion rates and patient safety. Practice guideline registration: Practice Guideline Registration for Transparency (PREPARE-2026CN2145)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to address the challenges clinicians face in managing hematologic adverse reactions during pegylated interferon-alpha (PEG-IFN-α) therapy for chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The objective is to provide a consensus on the management of these adverse reactions based on current evidence and clinical practice.
Design and population
A panel of experts in infectious diseases, hepatology, hematology, and clinical pharmacy was organized by the National Medical Center for Infectious Diseases to develop this consensus. The study focuses on patients undergoing PEG-IFN-α therapy for CHB, including special populations such as those with liver cirrhosis, older adults, children, and patients with renal insufficiency.
Core methods
The consensus was developed through systematic analysis of the incidence, clinical characteristics, and mechanisms of neutropenia, thrombocytopenia, and anemia during PEG-IFN-α treatment for CHB. It also evaluates the association between cytopenias and antiviral efficacy and proposes standardized monitoring strategies and management protocols.
Main results
It also analyzes the association between cytopenias and antiviral efficacy and proposes pretreatment risk stratification, standardized monitoring strategies, and graded management protocols.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that mild-to-moderate cytopenias should not be the sole reason for dose reduction or treatment discontinuation but should be assessed alongside on-treatment efficacy indicators. This provides a novel framework for managing hematologic adverse reactions in CHB patients receiving PEG-IFN-α therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the standardized management of hematologic adverse reactions associated with PEG-IFN-α therapy for CHB, potentially improving treatment completion rates and patient safety.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on expert consensus rather than empirical data, which may limit the strength of the evidence. However, the systematic analysis of current evidence and clinical practice provides a moderate level of confidence in the proposed management strategies.

乙型肝炎病毒 相关慢性肝衰竭的外周免疫景观:肿瘤坏死因子-α、M1 样巨噬细胞和淋巴细胞耗竭

Peripheral immune landscape in hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure: Tumor necrosis factor-α, M1-like macrophages, and lymphocyte exhaustion
作者： Ying Lin, Hui-Hui Chen, Dong-Yang Zhong, Wei-Zhen Chen, Bing‐Wang Jie, Yu-Wei Shen, Dan Xie, Shi Ouyang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 相关的慢加急性肝衰竭 (HBV-ACLF) 是一种 28 天 死亡率 高的危重疾病。该研究旨在调查 HBV-ACLF 患者的外周免疫状况,重点关注肿瘤坏死因子-α (TNF-α) 水平、M1 巨噬细胞极化和淋巴细胞耗竭之间的关联。
设计与对象
这是一项前瞻性横断面研究,涉及 97 名受试者,包括健康对照、慢性乙型肝炎 患者和 HBV-ACLF 患者。该研究检查了这些群体的免疫特征和细胞因子水平。
核心方法
使用流式细胞术和酶联免疫吸附测定对外周免疫谱和细胞因子进行定量。通过 LASSO 回归和多变量逻辑回归确定独立相关因素。Benjamini-Hochberg 错误发现率 (FDR) 校正应用于所有组间比较和相关分析。
主要结果
HBV-ACLF patients showed profound peripheral immune remodeling, characterized by a pro-inflammatory shift toward M1-like monocyte/macrophage polarization, accompanied by compensatory upregulation of M2-like monocytes/macrophages and a sharp increase in serum TNF-α, interferon-gamma, and interleukin (IL)-6 (all FDR-adjusted P < 0.05). TNF-α and IL-6 exhibited strong collinearity (Spearman rho = 0.834), and sensitivity analysis substituting IL-6 for TNF-α yielded consistent findings (IL-6: OR = 1.187, 95%CI: 1.052-1.339, P = 0.005).
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了 HBV-ACLF 外周免疫环境中促炎性重塑和免疫耗竭的共存,将 TNF-α 确定为关键的免疫病理学相关因素。这项研究为 HBV-ACLF 的免疫机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,针对 TNF-α 并监测多谱系免疫串扰对于 HBV-ACLF 的风险分层和治疗干预可能很有价值。它们可能会为未来治疗这种疾病的研究和临床策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横截面设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制。然而,证据是强有力的,FDR 调整的 P 值和敏感性分析的结果一致。

Peripheral immune landscape in hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure: Tumor necrosis factor-α, M1-like macrophages, and lymphocyte exhaustion

Authors: Ying Lin, Hui-Hui Chen, Dong-Yang Zhong, Wei-Zhen Chen, Bing‐Wang Jie, Yu-Wei Shen, Dan Xie, Shi Ouyang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF) is a critical illness with high 28-day mortality. It is associated with immunometabolic-mediated systemic inflammation leading to multi-organ failure. AIM To investigate the peripheral immune landscape in patients with HBV-ACLF, with particular attention to the associations among tumor necrosis factor-alpha (TNF-α) levels, M1 macrophage polarization, and lymphocyte exhaustion. METHODS In this prospective cross-sectional study of 97 subjects (healthy controls, chronic hepatitis B, and HBV-ACLF), flow cytometry and enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay quantified peripheral immune profiles and cytokines. Independent associated factors were identified via least absolute shrinkage and selection operator (LASSO) and multivariable logistic regression. The Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate (FDR) correction was applied to all intergroup comparisons and correlation analyses. RESULTS HBV-ACLF patients exhibited profound peripheral immune remodeling, featuring a pro-inflammatory shift toward M1-like monocyte/macrophage polarization accompanied by compensatory upregulation of M2-like monocytes/macrophages and a sharp escalation in serum TNF-α, interferon-gamma, and interleukin (IL)-6 ( all FDR-adjusted P < 0.05). LASSO regression with 10-fold cross-validation selected 9 feature variables, and after iterative variance inflation factor refinement, 6 variables were retained. Firth’s bias-reduced logistic regression identified elevated TNF-α as an independent associated factor [odds ratio (OR) = 1.359, 95%CI: 1.056-1.749, P = 0.017]. Notably, TNF-α and IL-6 exhibited strong collinearity (Spearman rho = 0.834); sensitivity analysis substituting IL-6 for TNF-α yielded consistent findings (IL-6: OR = 1.187, 95%CI: 1.052-1.339, P = 0.005). Stratified and correlation analyses revealed that high TNF-α and M1-like monocyte/macrophage polarization positively correlated with clinical severity (total bilirubin, creatinine) and negatively with lymphocyte percentages, while M2-like monocytes/macrophages positively correlated with neutrophil ratio (FDR-adjusted P < 0.05). Staging analysis showed a synchronized TNF-α surge, M1 shift, and deep lymphocyte depletion during mid-to-late stages (prothrombin activity ≤ 30%). CONCLUSION This study systematically characterized the coexistence of pro-inflammatory remodeling and immune exhaustion within the peripheral immune landscape of HBV-ACLF, identifying TNF-α as a key immunopathological associated factor. These findings highlight the potential value of targeting TNF-α and monitoring multi-lineage immune crosstalk for risk stratification and therapeutic intervention in HBV-ACLF.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF) is a critical illness with high 28-day mortality. The study aimed to investigate the peripheral immune landscape in HBV-ACLF patients, focusing on the associations among tumor necrosis factor-alpha (TNF-α) levels, M1 macrophage polarization, and lymphocyte exhaustion.
Design and population
This was a prospective cross-sectional study involving 97 subjects, including healthy controls, chronic hepatitis B patients, and those with HBV-ACLF. The study examined the immune profiles and cytokine levels across these groups.
Core methods
Peripheral immune profiles and cytokines were quantified using flow cytometry and enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay. Independent associated factors were identified through LASSO regression and multivariable logistic regression. The Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate (FDR) correction was applied to all intergroup comparisons and correlation analyses.
Main results
HBV-ACLF patients showed profound peripheral immune remodeling, characterized by a pro-inflammatory shift toward M1-like monocyte/macrophage polarization, accompanied by compensatory upregulation of M2-like monocytes/macrophages and a sharp increase in serum TNF-α, interferon-gamma, and interleukin (IL)-6 (all FDR-adjusted P < 0.05). TNF-α and IL-6 exhibited strong collinearity (Spearman rho = 0.834), and sensitivity analysis substituting IL-6 for TNF-α yielded consistent findings (IL-6: OR = 1.187, 95%CI: 1.052-1.339, P = 0.005).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the coexistence of pro-inflammatory remodeling and immune exhaustion in the peripheral immune landscape of HBV-ACLF, identifying TNF-α as a key immunopathological associated factor. This study provides novel insights into the immune mechanisms underlying HBV-ACLF.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that targeting TNF-α and monitoring multi-lineage immune crosstalk could be valuable for risk stratification and therapeutic intervention in HBV-ACLF. They may inform future research and clinical strategies for managing this condition.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for confounding factors. However, the evidence is strong, with FDR-adjusted P values and consistent findings from sensitivity analysis.
