乌干达布韦亚勒镇议会孕妇中的疟疾 患病率、相关因素以及艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和梅毒的合并感染

Malaria prevalence, associated factors, and coinfections with HIV, hepatitis B, and syphilis among pregnant women in Bweyale Town Council, Uganda
作者： Charles Drichi, Hillary Ngolobe, Peterson David Kamulegeya, Linda Mercy Akello, Daniel S. Ebbs, Felix Bongomin
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管存在有效的预防干预措施,但怀孕期间的疟疾仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区可预防的孕产妇和新生儿发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因。人们对乌干达布韦亚勒镇议会孕妇的疟疾负担、相关因素以及疟疾-艾滋病毒、疟疾-乙型肝炎和疟疾-梅毒合并感染知之甚少。
设计与对象
2026 年 4 月至 5 月期间,在布韦亚勒镇议会的三个卫生中心进行了一项基于设施的横断面研究。参与者是通过便利抽样招募的。共有 422 名孕妇入组。
核心方法
使用泛物种快速诊断测试确认疟疾,而艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和梅毒结果则使用访谈员填写的问卷从产前记录中提取。使用具有稳健标准误差的改良泊松回归评估与疟疾相关的因素,并报告了具有 95% 置信区间的调整后 患病率 比率 (aPR)。
主要结果
The prevalence of laboratory-confirmed malaria was 12.6% (53/422; 95% CI 10.0–16.0%). HIV, HBV, and syphilis prevalence were 4.0%, 2.8%, and 2.6%, respectively. Malaria-HIV and malaria-syphilis coinfections occurred in 0.5% and 1.0% of participants, respectively. Women who did not sleep under long-lasting insecticide-treated nets (LLINs) had more than twice the prevalence of malaria compared with net users (aPR 2.45, 95% CI 1.47–4.09; p = 0.001).
解释与创新
未能在经过杀虫剂处理的蚊帐下睡觉与疟疾独立相关。该研究深入了解乌干达布韦亚勒镇议会孕妇中疟疾的 患病率 及其与艾滋病毒和梅毒的合并感染。
科研与公卫意义
加强 ANC 中使用的疟疾预防策略可能有助于减少妊娠期疟疾及其相关不良后果。研究结果可能为改善该地区孕产妇和新生儿健康的有针对性的干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在一个镇议会中进行的,可能无法推广到其他地区。证据基于便利抽样,这可能会引入偏差。研究结果的可信度为中等。

Malaria prevalence, associated factors, and coinfections with HIV, hepatitis B, and syphilis among pregnant women in Bweyale Town Council, Uganda

Authors: Charles Drichi, Hillary Ngolobe, Peterson David Kamulegeya, Linda Mercy Akello, Daniel S. Ebbs, Felix Bongomin
Original abstract (English)
Malaria during pregnancy remains a major cause of preventable maternal and neonatal morbidity and mortality in sub-Saharan Africa despite the availability of effective preventive interventions. Pregnant women with malaria may also have concurrent infections such as human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), and syphilis, which can further worsen pregnancy outcomes. However, little is known about the burden of malaria, associated factors, and malaria-HIV, malaria-HBV, and malaria-syphilis coinfections among pregnant women in Bweyale Town Council, Uganda. This study determined the malaria prevalence, associated factors, and coinfections with HIV, Hepatitis B, and Syphilis among pregnant women in Bweyale Town Council, Uganda. A facility-based cross-sectional study was conducted between April and May 2026 at three health centres in Bweyale Town Council. Participants were recruited using convenience sampling. Malaria was confirmed using a pan-species rapid diagnostic test, while HIV, hepatitis B, and syphilis results were extracted from antenatal records using interviewer-administered questionnaires. Factors associated with malaria were assessed using modified Poisson regression with robust standard errors, and adjusted prevalence ratios (aPRs) with 95% confidence intervals were reported. Statistical significance was set at p < 0.05. A total of 422 pregnant women were enrolled. The median age was 24 years (IQR: 20–27), and 95.3% (402/422) were married. The prevalence of laboratory-confirmed malaria was 12.6% (53/422; 95% CI 10.0–16.0%). HIV, HBV, and syphilis prevalence were 4.0 , 2.8 , and 2.6% , respectively. Malaria-HIV and malaria-syphilis coinfections occurred in 0.5% and 1.0% of participants, respectively. Women who did not sleep under long-lasting insecticide-treated nets (LLINs) had more than twice the prevalence of malaria compared with net users (aPR 2.45, 95% CI 1.47–4.09; p = 0.001). About one in eight pregnant women attending ANC in Bweyale Town Council had malaria infection. Malaria coinfections with HIV and syphilis were also identified. Failure to sleep under an insecticide-treated net was independently associated with malaria. Strengthening malaria preventive strategies used in ANC could contribute to reducing malaria in pregnancy and its associated adverse outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Malaria during pregnancy remains a major cause of preventable maternal and neonatal morbidity and mortality in sub-Saharan Africa despite the availability of effective preventive interventions. Little is known about the burden of malaria, associated factors, and malaria-HIV, malaria-HBV, and malaria-syphilis coinfections among pregnant women in Bweyale Town Council, Uganda.

A facility-based cross-sectional study was conducted between April and May 2026 at three health centres in Bweyale Town Council. Participants were recruited using convenience sampling. A total of 422 pregnant women were enrolled.
Core methods
Malaria was confirmed using a pan-species rapid diagnostic test, while HIV, hepatitis B, and syphilis results were extracted from antenatal records using interviewer-administered questionnaires. Factors associated with malaria were assessed using modified Poisson regression with robust standard errors, and adjusted prevalence ratios (aPRs) with 95% confidence intervals were reported.
Main results
The prevalence of laboratory-confirmed malaria was 12.6% (53/422; 95% CI 10.0–16.0%). HIV, HBV, and syphilis prevalence were 4.0%, 2.8%, and 2.6%, respectively. Malaria-HIV and malaria-syphilis coinfections occurred in 0.5% and 1.0% of participants, respectively. Women who did not sleep under long-lasting insecticide-treated nets (LLINs) had more than twice the prevalence of malaria compared with net users (aPR 2.45, 95% CI 1.47–4.09; p = 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
Failure to sleep under an insecticide-treated net was independently associated with malaria. The study provides insights into the prevalence of malaria and its coinfections with HIV and syphilis among pregnant women in Bweyale Town Council, Uganda.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening malaria preventive strategies used in ANC could contribute to reducing malaria in pregnancy and its associated adverse outcomes. The findings may inform targeted interventions to improve maternal and neonatal health in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single town council and may not be generalizable to other regions. The evidence is based on convenience sampling, which may introduce bias. The confidence in the findings is moderate.

聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 添加到核苷(酸)类似物可减少肝细胞癌和晚期纤维化的失代偿 慢性乙型肝炎

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B
作者： Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
摘要中文翻译背景目前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。然而,其在改善 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者预后方面的价值以及该联合策略的合适候选者仍有待进一步探索。目的 评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。方法 这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了诊断为 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化的患者。参与者被分为两组:(1)接受联合治疗(Peg-IFNα-2b 附加到正在进行的 NA 治疗);(2) 接受 NA 单一疗法的患者。采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。结果 该分析包括 302 对配对,中位随访时间为 220 周。与 NA 单一疗法相比,联合治疗与发生 HCC(风险比 = 0.22;95% CI:0.11-0.48)和失代偿性肝硬化(风险比 = 0.39;95% CI:0.20-0.77)的风险显着降低相关。这些益处在经验证的 HCC 预测模型分类为中高风险的患者以及持续生化异常持续 > 12 个月的患者中尤其明显。结论 Peg-IFNα-2b 与 NA 的附加治疗与患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者的 HCC 减少和失代偿显着相关,支持其在该人群中的使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。当前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了诊断患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化的患者。参与者被分为两组:(1)接受联合治疗(Peg-IFNα-2b 附加到正在进行的 NA 治疗);(2) 接受 NA 单一疗法的患者。
核心方法
采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。
主要结果
该分析包括 302 对配对,中位随访时间为 220 周。与 NA 单一疗法相比,联合治疗与发生 HCC(风险比 = 0.22;95% CI:0.11-0.48)和失代偿性肝硬化(风险比 = 0.39;95% CI:0.20-0.77)的风险显着降低相关。
解释与创新
这些益处在经验证的 HCC 预测模型分类为中高风险的患者以及持续生化异常持续 > 12 个月的患者中尤其明显。Peg-IFNα-2b 与 NA 的附加治疗与患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者的 HCC 减少和失代偿显着相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持对患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者使用附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 疗法,以减少肝细胞癌和失代偿。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的具体局限性。

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure. However, its value in improving prognosis and the suitable candidates for this combination strategy in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis remain to be further explored. AIM To evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. METHODS This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy. A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores. RESULTS The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy. These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. CONCLUSION Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy.
Core methods
A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores.
Main results
The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy.
Interpretation and novelty
These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis to reduce hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

尽管 慢性乙型肝炎 患者有家人支持,但仍感到耻辱和信息披露问题:来自印度的混合方法证据

Perceived stigma and disclosure concerns despite family support among chronic hepatitis B patients: mixed-methods evidence from India
作者： Kanica Kaushal, Sumridhi Gautam, Shrinkhla Sahai, Guresh Kumar, Siddhesh Mhatre, Smriti Singh, Bharat Bhushan Rewari, Shiv Kumar Sarin
摘要中文翻译这项混合方法横断面研究测量了印度 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的耻辱感和感知耻辱感,并探讨了与乙型肝炎 诊断 相关的生活经历和心理社会挑战。2024 年,从新德里的一家三级医院招募了总共 273 名年龄≥ 18 岁、患有 慢性乙型肝炎(乙型肝炎表面抗原 [HBsAg] 阳性)的参与者。定量数据使用结构化问卷和 Berger HIV 耻辱量表的 11 项乙型肝炎改编,测量感知耻辱的四个维度:披露问题、个性化耻辱、公众态度和消极自我形象,再加上制定了四项耻辱指标,包括社会排斥、工作场所歧视、家庭孤立和伴侣遗弃。二十名参与者接受了定性访谈,探讨 诊断 反应、歧视经历和应对机制。平均年龄为41.57岁;60.1%为男性,72.5%已婚,58.6%拥有研究生或更高学历。大多数参与者不同意四个领域中每个领域的污名化陈述(64.5%–82.4%);总分中位数为 22(IQR 22-28),总分可能为 11-44。家庭内部的耻辱并不常见(98.53% 的人表示没有家庭隔离),而 31.14% 的人表示对披露自己的状况犹豫不决。社会边缘化持续存在,15.75% 的人被排除在外,28.57% 的人报告职业受到影响。在怀孕期间确诊的女性 (n = 68) 中,76.5% 的人表示担心母婴 传播。这些发现为支持《2025 年病毒性肝炎(预防和控制)法案》提供了重要证据,强调迫切需要制定反歧视条款和有针对性的干预措施来解决工作场所歧视和怀孕相关问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解尽管有家庭支持,印度 慢性乙型肝炎 患者所感受到的耻辱和披露担忧的影响,以解决与乙型肝炎 诊断 相关的心理社会挑战的知识差距。
设计与对象
2024 年,新德里一家三级医院进行了一项混合方法横断面研究,涉及 273 名年龄≥18 岁、患有 慢性乙型肝炎(HBsAg 阳性)的参与者。
核心方法
使用改编自伯杰艾滋病毒耻辱量表的结构化问卷收集定量数据,测量四个维度的感知耻辱,并通过社会排斥和家庭隔离等指标制定耻辱。对 20 名参与者进行了定性访谈,探讨他们的生活经历和应对机制。
主要结果
大多数参与者不同意这四个领域中的每一个领域的污名化陈述(64.5%–82.4%);总分中位数为 22(IQR 22-28),总分可能为 11-44。家庭内部的耻辱并不常见(98.53% 的人表示没有家庭隔离),而 31.14% 的人表示对披露自己的状况犹豫不决。
解释与创新
该研究强调了慢乙肝患者中持续存在的社会边缘化问题,其中 15.75% 的患者被排除在外,28.57% 的患者报告了职业影响,并强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来解决工作场所歧视和与怀孕相关的问题。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过强调反歧视条款和解决医疗机构中的耻辱问题的重要性,为公共卫生政策(例如《2025 年病毒性肝炎(预防和控制)法案》)提供了重要证据。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于新德里的一家三级医院,可能会影响普遍性。由于横截面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖,证据的强度适中。

Perceived stigma and disclosure concerns despite family support among chronic hepatitis B patients: mixed-methods evidence from India

Authors: Kanica Kaushal, Sumridhi Gautam, Shrinkhla Sahai, Guresh Kumar, Siddhesh Mhatre, Smriti Singh, Bharat Bhushan Rewari, Shiv Kumar Sarin
Original abstract (English)
This mixed-methods cross-sectional study measured enacted and perceived stigma among chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients in India and explored lived experiences and psychosocial challenges associated with hepatitis B diagnosis. A total of 273 participants aged ≥ 18 years with chronic hepatitis B (hepatitis B surface antigen [HBsAg]-positive) were recruited from a tertiary hospital in New Delhi during 2024. Quantitative data used structured questionnaires and an 11-item hepatitis B adaptation of the Berger HIV Stigma Scale measuring four dimensions of perceived stigma: disclosure concerns, personalized stigma, public attitudes, and negative self-image, plus four enacted stigma indicators including social exclusion, workplace discrimination, family isolation, and partner abandonment. Twenty participants underwent qualitative interviews exploring diagnosis reactions, discrimination experiences, and coping mechanisms. The mean age was 41.57 years; 60.1% were male, 72.5% married, and 58.6% held graduate or higher qualifications. Most participants disagreed with the stigma statements in each of the four domains (64.5%–82.4%); the median total score was 22 (IQR 22–28) of a possible 11–44. Enacted stigma within families was uncommon (98.53% reported no family isolation), whereas 31.14% reported hesitancy about disclosing their status. Social marginalization persisted, with 15.75% excluded and 28.57% reporting career impacts. Among women diagnosed during pregnancy (n = 68), 76.5% reported concern about mother-to-child transmission. These findings provide crucial evidence supporting the Viral Hepatitis (Prevention and Control) Bill, 2025, highlighting urgent need for anti-discrimination provisions and targeted interventions addressing workplace discrimination and pregnancy-related concerns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand the impact of perceived stigma and disclosure concerns on chronic hepatitis B patients in India, despite family support, to address gaps in knowledge about psychosocial challenges associated with hepatitis B diagnosis.
Design and population
A mixed-methods cross-sectional study was conducted in a tertiary hospital in New Delhi during 2024, involving 273 participants aged ≥ 18 years with chronic hepatitis B (HBsAg-positive).
Core methods
Quantitative data were collected using structured questionnaires adapted from the Berger HIV Stigma Scale, measuring perceived stigma across four dimensions and enacted stigma through indicators like social exclusion and family isolation. Qualitative interviews were conducted with 20 participants to explore their lived experiences and coping mechanisms.
Main results
Most participants disagreed with stigma statements in each of the four domains (64.5%–82.4%); the median total score was 22 (IQR 22–28) of a possible 11–44. Enacted stigma within families was uncommon (98.53% reported no family isolation), whereas 31.14% reported hesitancy about disclosing their status.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the persistence of social marginalization among CHB patients, with 15.75% excluded and 28.57% reporting career impacts, and underscores the need for targeted interventions addressing workplace discrimination and pregnancy-related concerns.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide crucial evidence for informing public health policies, such as the Viral Hepatitis (Prevention and Control) Bill, 2025, by emphasizing the importance of anti-discrimination provisions and addressing stigma in healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single tertiary hospital in New Delhi, potentially affecting generalizability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data.

HBV 相关肝硬化患者的抗 HDV 血清阳性及其与肝细胞癌和肝病严重程度的关系:一项回顾性横断面研究

Anti-HDV Seropositivity and Its Association with Hepatocellular Carcinoma and Liver Disease Severity in Patients with HBV-Related Cirrhosis: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Hayrettin Sever, Sami Çifçi, Yağmur Gökseven, Elif Güven Çetin, Celalettin Herek, Kader İrak, Şule Poturoğlu, Yağmur Gökseven Arda
摘要中文翻译丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 是肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的既定危险因素;然而,比较抗 HDV 阳性和抗 HDV 阴性 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝硬化患者的数据,特别是来自流行地区的数据有限。我们评估了抗 HDV 血清阳性与 HCC 以及 HBV 相关肝硬化中肝病严重程度的关联。这项回顾性横断面研究包括 221 名 HBV 相关肝硬化患者和可用的抗 HDV 结果(2020 年 9 月至 2025 年 9 月)。临床数据是在 HCC 诊断 或肝移植时或之前获得的。HCC 通过多变量逻辑回归评估,疾病严重程度通过终末期肝病模型 (MELD) 评分的对数转换模型线性回归评估,移植史通过 Firth 惩罚逻辑回归评估。39 名患者(18%)抗 HDV 呈阳性,其中 14 名(36%)可检测到 HDV RNA;94 名患者 (43%) 患有 HCC。抗 HDV 血清阳性与 HCC 无关(调整优势比 [aOR] 0.86;95% 置信区间 [CI] 0.38–1.95)。调整年龄和性别后,抗 HDV 阳性患者的 MELD 评分高出 24%(几何平均比 1.24;95% CI 1.05–1.45),这种关联在非 HCC 患者中也持续存在。在一项探索性分析中,抗 HDV 血清阳性也与移植史相关(aOR 6.32;95% CI 2.52-15.99)。在 HBV 相关肝硬化中,抗 HDV 血清阳性与更严重的肝脏疾病相关,但与 HCC 无关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝硬化患者中抗 HDV 血清阳性与肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和肝脏疾病严重程度的关系,弥补文献中的知识空白,特别是来自流行地区的文献。
设计与对象
对 2020 年 9 月至 2025 年 9 月期间 221 名 HBV 相关肝硬化患者和可用的抗 HDV 结果进行了回顾性横断面研究。
核心方法
该研究利用多变量逻辑回归进行 HCC 评估、线性回归进行 MELD 评分分析,以及 Firth 的惩罚逻辑回归进行移植史。
主要结果
抗 HDV 血清阳性与 HCC 无关(调整优势比 [aOR] 0.86;95% 置信区间 [CI] 0.38–1.95)。然而,它与更严重的肝脏疾病(抗 HDV 阳性患者的 MELD 评分高出 24%)和移植史相关(aOR 6.32;95% CI 2.52-15.99)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,抗 HDV 血清阳性与更严重的肝脏疾病相关,但与 HBV 相关肝硬化中的 HCC 无关,这为 HDV 感染的临床意义提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
该研究的结果可以为 监测 和 HBV 相关肝硬化患者的管理策略提供信息,特别是在监测肝病严重程度和移植风险方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Anti-HDV Seropositivity and Its Association with Hepatocellular Carcinoma and Liver Disease Severity in Patients with HBV-Related Cirrhosis: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Hayrettin Sever, Sami Çifçi, Yağmur Gökseven, Elif Güven Çetin, Celalettin Herek, Kader İrak, Şule Poturoğlu, Yağmur Gökseven Arda
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis D virus (HDV) is an established risk factor for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC); however, data comparing anti-HDV-positive and anti-HDV-negative patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related cirrhosis, particularly from endemic regions, are limited. We evaluated the association of anti-HDV seropositivity with HCC and liver disease severity in HBV-related cirrhosis. This retrospective cross-sectional study included 221 patients with HBV-related cirrhosis and available anti-HDV results (September 2020–September 2025). Clinical data were obtained at or before HCC diagnosis or liver transplantation. HCC was assessed by multivariable logistic regression, disease severity by linear regression of the log-transformed Model for End-Stage Liver Disease (MELD) score, and transplantation history by Firth’s penalized logistic regression. Anti-HDV was positive in 39 patients (18%), 14 of whom (36%) had detectable HDV RNA; 94 patients (43%) had HCC. Anti-HDV seropositivity was not associated with HCC (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 0.86; 95% confidence interval [CI] 0.38–1.95). After adjustment for age and sex, MELD scores were 24% higher in anti-HDV-positive patients (geometric mean ratio 1.24; 95% CI 1.05–1.45), an association that persisted among patients without HCC. In an exploratory analysis, anti-HDV seropositivity was also associated with transplantation history (aOR 6.32; 95% CI 2.52–15.99). In HBV-related cirrhosis, anti-HDV seropositivity was associated with more severe liver disease but not with HCC.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the association of anti-HDV seropositivity with hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and liver disease severity in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related cirrhosis, addressing a knowledge gap in the literature, particularly from endemic regions.
Design and population
A retrospective cross-sectional study was conducted on 221 patients with HBV-related cirrhosis and available anti-HDV results from September 2020 to September 2025.
Core methods
The study utilized multivariable logistic regression for HCC assessment, linear regression for MELD score analysis, and Firth’s penalized logistic regression for transplantation history.
Main results
Anti-HDV seropositivity was not associated with HCC (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 0.86; 95% confidence interval [CI] 0.38–1.95). However, it was associated with more severe liver disease (MELD scores 24% higher in anti-HDV-positive patients) and transplantation history (aOR 6.32; 95% CI 2.52–15.99).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that anti-HDV seropositivity is associated with more severe liver disease but not with HCC in HBV-related cirrhosis, providing new insights into the clinical implications of HDV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study’s findings can inform surveillance and management strategies for patients with HBV-related cirrhosis, particularly regarding the monitoring of liver disease severity and the risk of transplantation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the potential for confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

探索载脂蛋白在 乙型肝炎病毒 相关慢加急性肝衰竭中的作用

Exploring the role of apolipoproteins in hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure
作者： Li-Min Cao, Lei Liu, Dao-Kuan Zhai, Bin Yang
摘要中文翻译背景 乙型肝炎病毒 相关的慢加急性肝衰竭 (HBV-ACLF) 具有较高的 死亡率,但目前的预后工具缺乏分子精度。目的通过蛋白质组学分析探讨载脂蛋白(APO)在HBV-ACLF中的参与情况。方法 我们通过数据独立采集质谱法使用定量蛋白质组学分析了 10 名健康对照者和 20 名接受以血浆置换为中心的人工肝支持系统治疗的 HBV-ACLF 患者(10 名幸存者,10 名非幸存者)的血清样本。使用基因本体论和蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络工具分析差异蛋白质表达(倍数变化≥1.2或≤0.833,P<0.05)。同时评估肝酶和胆红素等临床参数。结果 200余种差异表达蛋白中,HBV-ACLF组APOL1、APOA1、APOC1、APOC2、APOH的含量显着低于对照组,非幸存者组APOC1、APOC2、APOH的含量显着低于幸存者组。然而,在调整混杂因素后,差异并不具有统计学意义。此外,APOL1 和 APOA1 的组合 诊断 组合显示出 HBV-ACLF 的最高诊断准确性,曲线下面积 (AUC) 为 0.940 (P = 0.0001)。同样,APOC2 和 APOH 的组合对非存活产生了最高的预测性能,达到 0.990(P = 0.0002)的 AUC。蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络将 APO 定位为脂质代谢和炎症途径的枢纽。结论这些APO可能为HBV-ACLF的早期检测和预后风险分层提供新的线索。未来需要大样本研究来验证我们的发现。

问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 相关的慢加急性肝衰竭 (HBV-ACLF) 具有较高的 死亡率,但目前的预后工具缺乏分子精度。

目的通过蛋白质组学分析探讨载脂蛋白(APO)在HBV-ACLF中的参与情况。我们通过数据独立采集质谱法使用定量蛋白质组学分析了 10 名健康对照者和 20 名接受以血浆置换为中心的人工肝支持系统治疗的 HBV-ACLF 患者(10 名幸存者,10 名非幸存者)的血清样本。
核心方法
使用基因本体论和蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络工具分析差异蛋白质表达(倍数变化≥1.2或≤0.833,P<0.05)。同时评估肝酶和胆红素等临床参数。
主要结果
在200多种差异表达蛋白中,HBV-ACLF组中APOL1、APOA1、APOC1、APOC2、APOH的水平显着低于对照组,非幸存者组中APOC1、APOC2、APOH的水平显着低于幸存者组。APOL1 和 APOA1 的组合 诊断 组合显示出 HBV-ACLF 的最高诊断准确性,曲线下面积 (AUC) 为 0.940 (P = 0.0001)。同样,APOC2 和 APOH 的组合对非存活产生了最高的预测性能,达到 0.990(P = 0.0002)的 AUC。

这些APO可能为HBV-ACLF的早期检测和预后风险分层提供新的线索。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为开发更精确的 HBV-ACLF 诊断工具提供信息,并改善患者的风险分层。
局限与证据强度
然而,在调整混杂因素后,差异并不具有统计学意义。

Exploring the role of apolipoproteins in hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure

Authors: Li-Min Cao, Lei Liu, Dao-Kuan Zhai, Bin Yang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF) carries high mortality, yet current prognostic tools lack molecular precision. AIM To investigate the involvement of apolipoproteins (APO) in HBV-ACLF through using proteomics analyses. METHODS We analyzed serum samples from 10 healthy controls and 20 patients with HBV-ACLF treated with a plasma exchange-centered artificial liver support system (10 survivors, 10 non-survivors) using quanititative proteomics via data-independent acquisition mass spectrometry. Differential protein expression (fold-change ≥ 1.2 or ≤ 0.833, P < 0.05) was analyzed using Gene Ontology and protein–protein interaction network tools. Clinical parameters like liver enzymes and bilirubin were assessed concurrently. RESULTS Among more than 200 differentially expressed proteins, he levels of APOL1, APOA1, APOC1, APOC2, and APOH were significantly lower in the HBV-ACLF groups than in the control groups, and the levels of APOC1, APOC2, and APOH were significantly lower in non-survivors than in survivor group. However, after adjusting for confounding factors, the differences were not statistically significant. Furthermore, the combined diagnosis panel of APOL1 and APOA1 showed the highest diagnostic accuracy for HBV-ACLF, with an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.940 (P = 0.0001). Similarly, the combination of APOC2 and APOH yielded the highest predictive performance for non-survival, achieving an AUC of 0.990 (P = 0.0002). The protein-protein interaction network positioned APO as hubs in lipid metabolism and inflammation pathways. CONCLUSION These APO may provide new clues for early detection and prognostic risk stratification of HBV-ACLF. Large sample studies are needed to validate our findings in the future.

Question and background
Hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF) carries high mortality, yet current prognostic tools lack molecular precision.
Design and population
AIM To investigate the involvement of apolipoproteins (APO) in HBV-ACLF through using proteomics analyses. We analyzed serum samples from 10 healthy controls and 20 patients with HBV-ACLF treated with a plasma exchange-centered artificial liver support system (10 survivors, 10 non-survivors) using quantitative proteomics via data-independent acquisition mass spectrometry.
Core methods
Differential protein expression (fold-change ≥ 1.2 or ≤ 0.833, P < 0.05) was analyzed using Gene Ontology and protein–protein interaction network tools. Clinical parameters like liver enzymes and bilirubin were assessed concurrently.
Main results
Among more than 200 differentially expressed proteins, the levels of APOL1, APOA1, APOC1, APOC2, and APOH were significantly lower in the HBV-ACLF groups than in the control groups, and the levels of APOC1, APOC2, and APOH were significantly lower in non-survivors than in survivor group. The combined diagnosis panel of APOL1 and APOA1 showed the highest diagnostic accuracy for HBV-ACLF, with an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.940 (P = 0.0001). Similarly, the combination of APOC2 and APOH yielded the highest predictive performance for non-survival, achieving an AUC of 0.990 (P = 0.0002).
Interpretation and novelty
These APO may provide new clues for early detection and prognostic risk stratification of HBV-ACLF.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more precise diagnostic tools for HBV-ACLF and improve risk stratification for patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
However, after adjusting for confounding factors, the differences were not statistically significant.

慢性乙型肝炎 中 MRN-ATM 通路激活和 CD4+ T 细胞衰老相关表型

MRN-ATM pathway activation and senescence-associated phenotype of CD4+ T cells in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Xiao-Ying Deng, Xiao-Yan Wang, Xu-Yang Zheng, Yun Zhou, Jian-Qi Lian, Chao Fan, Ying Zhang, Yi Li, Fangfang Li, Jun Xiong
摘要中文翻译T 细胞衰老是持续性病毒感染中免疫功能障碍的标志,其特征是 DNA 损伤积累和端粒磨损。然而,慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染期间 DNA 损伤反应途径在 CD4 + T 细胞衰老中的作用仍不清楚。在这项研究中,我们证明,与健康受试者 (HS) 相比,慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者在 CD4 + T 细胞中表现出衰老相关表型,其特征是 KLRG1 升高,同时 DNA 损伤增加和端粒缩短。值得注意的是,MRE11/RAD50/NBS1-共济失调毛细血管扩张突变 (MRN-ATM) 通路的激活在慢性乙型肝炎患者的 CD4 + T 细胞中很显着。重要的是,抑制 MRN 会减弱 ATM 磷酸化及其下游信号分子,并且抑制 ATM 会减少从 CHB 和 HS 个体中分离出的 CD4 + T 淋巴细胞中 IFN-γ 和 IL-2 的产生。我们的研究结果表明,在慢性 HBV 感染期间,CD4 + T 细胞表现出衰老相关表型、端粒缩短和基因组 DNA 损伤。MRN-ATM 通路的同时激活可能代表一种修复 DNA 损伤的机制,并与 CD4 + T 细胞细胞因子的产生相关,尽管它并不能完全预防衰老。阐明 CD4 + T 细胞衰老相关表型与 MRN-ATM 通路之间的关系,有助于了解 HBV 持续存在的机制,并可能为未来的治疗策略提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染期间 DNA 损伤反应途径在 CD4 + T 细胞衰老中的作用,弥补在理解 HBV 持续存在机制方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究涉及比较 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 个体和健康受试者 (HS) 的 CD4 + T 细胞,以检查与衰老相关的表型和 DNA 损伤。
核心方法
从 CHB 和 HS 参与者中分离出 CD4 + T 细胞,并使用特定测定法评估衰老相关表型、DNA 损伤和端粒缩短。
主要结果
与 HS 相比,CHB 患者的 CD4 + T 细胞表现出衰老相关表型,其特征是 KLRG1 升高、DNA 损伤增加和端粒缩短。
解释与创新
研究表明,CHB 患者的 CD4 + T 细胞中 MRN-ATM 通路的激活非常显着,抑制 MRN 会减弱 ATM 磷酸化和下游信号传导,从而减少细胞因子的产生。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在慢性 HBV 感染期间,CD4 + T 细胞表现出衰老相关表型和基因组 DNA 损伤,并且 MRN-ATM 通路的同时激活可能为未来的治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括缺乏随机化和使用单一干预措施,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。

MRN-ATM pathway activation and senescence-associated phenotype of CD4+ T cells in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Xiao-Ying Deng, Xiao-Yan Wang, Xu-Yang Zheng, Yun Zhou, Jian-Qi Lian, Chao Fan, Ying Zhang, Yi Li, Fangfang Li, Jun Xiong
Original abstract (English)
T-cell senescence is a hallmark of immune dysfunction in persistent viral infections, characterized by DNA damage accumulation and telomere attrition. However, the role of DNA damage response pathway in CD4 + T-cell senescence during chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains poorly defined. In this study, we demonstrated that people with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) exhibited senescence-associated phenotype in CD4 + T cells, marked by elevated KLRG1, along with increased DNA damage and telomere shortening, compared to healthy subjects (HS). Notably, activation of the MRE11/RAD50/NBS1-ataxia telangiectasia mutated (MRN-ATM) pathway was prominent in CD4 + T cells from CHB patients. Importantly, suppression of MRN attenuated ATM phosphorylation and its downstream signaling molecules, and inhibition of ATM reduced the production of IFN-γ and IL-2 in CD4 + T lymphocytes isolated from individuals with CHB and HS. Our findings indicate that during chronic HBV infection, CD4 + T cells exhibited senescence-associated phenotype, telomere shortening and genomic DNA damage. Concurrent activation of the MRN-ATM pathway may represent a mechanism that repairs DNA damage and be associated with CD4 + T-cell cytokine production, although it does not fully prevent senescence. Elucidating the relationship between CD4 + T-cell senescence-associated phenotype and the MRN-ATM pathway, helps to understand the mechanisms underlying HBV persistence and may inform future therapeutic strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of DNA damage response pathway in CD4 + T-cell senescence during chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, addressing a knowledge gap in understanding the mechanisms underlying HBV persistence.
Design and population
The study involved comparing CD4 + T cells from individuals with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and healthy subjects (HS) to examine senescence-associated phenotypes and DNA damage.
Core methods
CD4 + T cells were isolated from CHB and HS participants, and senescence-associated phenotypes, DNA damage, and telomere shortening were assessed using specific assays.
Main results
CHB patients exhibited senescence-associated phenotypes in CD4 + T cells, characterized by elevated KLRG1, increased DNA damage, and telomere shortening compared to HS.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that activation of the MRN-ATM pathway was prominent in CD4 + T cells from CHB patients, and suppression of MRN attenuated ATM phosphorylation and downstream signaling, reducing cytokine production.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that during chronic HBV infection, CD4 + T cells exhibit senescence-associated phenotypes and genomic DNA damage, and concurrent activation of the MRN-ATM pathway may inform future therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the absence of randomization and the use of a single intervention, which may affect the generalizability of the findings.

接受附加聚乙二醇干扰素 alfa-2b 的核苷(酸)类似物治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 和低水平病毒血症患者的第 48 周 HBsAg 清除率的早期预测:一项多中心真实世界研究

Early prediction of week-48 HBsAg clearance in nucleos(t)ide analogue-treated patients with chronic hepatitis B and low-level viremia receiving add-on pegylated interferon alfa-2b: a multicenter real-world study
作者： Shuo-Jie Wang, Yuan-Jiao Gao, Wen Deng, Xin Wei, Jun-Liang Fu, Jun-Feng Lu, Ya-Qin Zhang, Wei-Hua Cao, Zi-Yu Zhang, Xin-Xin Li
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在为接受核苷(酸)类似物加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 和低水平病毒血症患者开发第 48 周 HBsAg 清除的早期预测模型。
设计与对象
对 327 例 慢性乙型肝炎 和低水平病毒血症患者开始核苷类似物加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗的多中心前瞻性 队列研究 进行回顾性分析。最终模型是使用 310 名患者的数据开发的,并观察了第 48 周的结果。
核心方法
单变量分析中 P < 0.20 的预测因子以及年龄和性别均被考虑用于 LASSO 选择。该模型通过 Bootstrap 内部验证、158 名患者的外部验证以及 325 名患者的无反应者插补敏感性分析进行了验证。
主要结果
Among 310 patients with observed week-48 outcomes, 47 achieved HBsAg clearance. The final model included baseline HBsAg and its decline by week 12, both of which were associated with clearance (OR = 0.26, 95% CI: 0.18–0.38 and OR = 5.72, 95% CI: 3.15–10.38).
解释与创新
该模型显示出良好的预测性能,并且可以为临床风险评估提供有用的参考,尽管其区分度与更简单的第 12 周 HBsAg 测量结果相当。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为接受核苷(酸)类似物和 Peg-IFNα-2b 联合治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 和低水平病毒血症患者的 HBsAg 清除的临床风险评估提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用回顾性数据和有限的外部验证,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,该模型表现出良好的引导验证和决策曲线结果,表明证据强度中等。

Early prediction of week-48 HBsAg clearance in nucleos(t)ide analogue-treated patients with chronic hepatitis B and low-level viremia receiving add-on pegylated interferon alfa-2b: a multicenter real-world study

Authors: Shuo-Jie Wang, Yuan-Jiao Gao, Wen Deng, Xin Wei, Jun-Liang Fu, Jun-Feng Lu, Ya-Qin Zhang, Wei-Hua Cao, Zi-Yu Zhang, Xin-Xin Li
Original abstract (English)
To develop an early prediction model using LASSO regression for HBsAg clearance at week 48 in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients with low-level viremia (LLV) receiving nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) combined with pegylated interferon alpha-2b (Peg-IFNα-2b), and to evaluate its predictive performance. We retrospectively analyzed a multicenter prospective cohort of 327 patients with chronic hepatitis B and low-level viremia who initiated nucleos(t)ide analogue plus Peg-IFNα-2b therapy. Predictors with P < 0.20 in univariable analyses, together with age and sex, were considered for LASSO selection. The retained predictors were used to develop a nomogram in 310 patients with observed week-48 outcomes. The model underwent bootstrap internal validation (1,000 resamples), external validation in 158 patients using the original coefficients, and non-responder imputation (NRI) sensitivity analysis in 325 patients with available week-12 predictors. Exploratory analyses assessed model discrimination by baseline HBeAg status without refitting; a separate HBeAg-positive model was not developed because of the limited number of clearance events. Among 310 patients with observed week-48 outcomes, 47 achieved HBsAg clearance. The final model included baseline HBsAg and its decline by week 12, both were associated with clearance (OR = 0.26, 95% CI: 0.18–0.38 and OR = 5.72, 95% CI: 3.15–10.38). The apparent AUC was 0.889 (95% CI: 0.818–0.961), with favorable bootstrap validation, calibration, and decision curve results. The AUCs for week-12 HBsAg alone and the week-12 HBsAg < 100 IU/mL threshold were 0.886 and 0.870, respectively, indicating discrimination comparable to that of the final model. In the external validation cohort ( n = 158), the AUC was 0.845 (95% CI: 0.753–0.937). The NRI sensitivity analysis yielded a similar AUC of 0.887 (95% CI: 0.815–0.959) among 325 patients with week-12 predictor data. Based on LASSO regression, we developed a nomogram integrating baseline HBsAg level and its decline from baseline to week 12 to estimate week-48 HBsAg clearance in patients with CHB and low-level viremia receiving combined NAs and Peg-IFNα-2b therapy. The model showed good predictive performance and may provide a useful reference for clinical risk assessment, although its discrimination was comparable to that of simpler week-12 HBsAg measures.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop an early prediction model for HBsAg clearance at week 48 in patients with chronic hepatitis B and low-level viremia undergoing nucleos(t)ide analogue plus Peg-IFNα-2b therapy.
Design and population
A multicenter prospective cohort of 327 patients with chronic hepatitis B and low-level viremia who initiated nucleos(t)ide analogue plus Peg-IFNα-2b therapy was retrospectively analyzed. The final model was developed using data from 310 patients with observed week-48 outcomes.
Core methods
Predictors with P < 0.20 in univariable analyses, along with age and sex, were considered for LASSO selection. The model was validated through bootstrap internal validation, external validation in 158 patients, and non-responder imputation sensitivity analysis in 325 patients.
Main results
Among 310 patients with observed week-48 outcomes, 47 achieved HBsAg clearance. The final model included baseline HBsAg and its decline by week 12, both of which were associated with clearance (OR = 0.26, 95% CI: 0.18–0.38 and OR = 5.72, 95% CI: 3.15–10.38).
Interpretation and novelty
The model showed good predictive performance and may provide a useful reference for clinical risk assessment, although its discrimination was comparable to that of simpler week-12 HBsAg measures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform clinical risk assessment for HBsAg clearance in patients with chronic hepatitis B and low-level viremia receiving combined nucleos(t)ide analogue and Peg-IFNα-2b therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used retrospective data and limited external validation, which may affect generalizability. However, the model demonstrated favorable bootstrap validation and decision curve results, suggesting moderate evidence strength.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

“开发和验证一种新的预后模型来预测 死亡率 移植后 1 年急性 慢性乙型肝炎 肝衰竭:一项全国性、多中心 队列研究 研究”的勘误

Corrigendum to “Development and validation of a novel prognostic model to predict 1-year post-transplant mortality for acute-on-chronic hepatitis B liver failure: a nationwide, multicentre, cohort study”
作者： Li Zhuang, Yimou Lin, Yu Jia, Jun Fang, Yujian Zheng, Taishi Fang, Meiching Ong, Aibo Mu, Jiaxing Zhu, Mengchao Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发和验证一种预后模型,用于预测 慢性乙型肝炎 急性肝衰竭患者移植后 1 年的情况。
设计与对象
这是一项全国性多中心 队列研究 研究的勘误,该研究纳入了 668 名 乙型肝炎病毒 感染患者,数据收集时间为 2015 年 1 月 1 日至 2021 年 12 月 31 日。
核心方法
该研究涉及使用 队列研究 感染患者的 队列研究 数据开发和验证预后模型,并纠正原始出版物日期范围中的拼写错误。
主要结果
该研究纠正了原始出版物中的印刷错误,将日期范围从“2015年1月1日至2021年12月1日之间”更改为“2015年1月1日至2021年12月31日之间”。
解释与创新
更正不会改变研究结果,但确保了 队列研究 研究报告时间范围的准确性。
科研与公卫意义
该修正保持了研究数据和结果的完整性,这与了解 乙型肝炎病毒 感染患者的移植后 死亡率 相关。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于勘误表,仅解决印刷错误,不提供新的数据或发现。由于修正的性质,证据的强度中等。

Corrigendum to “Development and validation of a novel prognostic model to predict 1-year post-transplant mortality for acute-on-chronic hepatitis B liver failure: a nationwide, multicentre, cohort study”

Authors: Li Zhuang, Yimou Lin, Yu Jia, Jun Fang, Yujian Zheng, Taishi Fang, Meiching Ong, Aibo Mu, Jiaxing Zhu, Mengchao Wang
Original abstract (English)
In the “Findings” section of the Summary, due to a typo, the original text said “Between Jan 1, 2015, and Dec 1, 2021, 668 patients ...” but should have said “Between Jan 1, 2015, and Dec 31, 2021, 668 patients ...”.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop and validate a prognostic model for predicting 1-year post-transplant mortality in patients with acute-on-chronic hepatitis B liver failure.
Design and population
This is a corrigendum to a nationwide, multicentre cohort study that included 668 patients with hepatitis B virus infection, with data collected between Jan 1, 2015, and Dec 31, 2021.
Core methods
The study involved the development and validation of a prognostic model using data from a cohort of patients with hepatitis B virus infection, with the correction of a typo in the original publication's date range.
Main results
The study corrected a typographical error in the original publication, changing the date range from "Between Jan 1, 2015, and Dec 1, 2021" to "Between Jan 1, 2015, and Dec 31, 2021".
Interpretation and novelty
The correction does not alter the study's findings but ensures the accuracy of the reported time frame for the cohort study.
Scientific and public-health significance
The correction maintains the integrity of the study's data and findings, which are relevant for understanding post-transplant mortality in patients with hepatitis B virus infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a corrigendum, which only addresses a typographical error and does not provide new data or findings. The evidence is of moderate strength due to the nature of the correction.

慢性乙型肝炎 患者纤维化非侵入性标志物与肝活检的比较

Comparison of Non-Invasive Markers with Liver Biopsy for Detecting Fibrosis in Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B
作者： Hande İdil Tüz, Nihal Pişkin, Güven Çelebi, Hande Aydemir, Emre HORUZ, Çağatay Büyükuysal, Mehmet Ali Tüz, Mustafa Çağatay Büyükuysal
摘要中文翻译目的 肝活检是确定 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 肝纤维化的金标准;然而,正在开发替代方法,例如非侵入性间接血清标记物,因为它是一种侵入性程序。本研究旨在将慢性乙型肝炎患者肝活检的纤维化值与非侵入性血清标志物进行比较,以确定这些标志物的功效。材料和方法 回顾性纳入接受肝活检的 诊断 慢性乙型肝炎患者。根据Ishak纤维化分期系统,将患者分为两组:轻度纤维化(F0-2)和晚期纤维化(F3-6)。计算年龄-血小板指数、天冬氨酸转氨酶(AST)与血小板比值指数(APRI)、AST/丙氨酸转氨酶(ALT)比值(AAR)、纤维化-4指数、Fibro-Q和King’s评分。比较了标记物区分晚期纤维化的能力。结果共纳入183例患者。其中,101例患者检测到轻度纤维化(≤2),82例检测到晚期纤维化(>2)。在治疗治疗开始时,晚期纤维化组的ALT、AST、组织学活动指数和HBV-DNA水平显着升高(p<0.05)。APRI、King’s 评分和 AAR(反比)对于检测晚期纤维化具有统计学意义 (p<0.05)。APRI、AAR 和 King’s 评分的接受者操作特征值下面积分别为 0.611、0.637 和 0.632。结论 APRI 和 King’s 评分在区分晚期和轻度纤维化方面具有统计学意义,但其功效有限。AAR 显示出反比关系,可能反映了炎症活动而不是实际的纤维化。肝活检似乎是诊断慢性乙型肝炎患者纤维化的金标准。需要新的研究来开发非侵入性标记物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较无创血清标志物与肝活检用于检测 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者纤维化的情况,满足对微创方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究回顾性地纳入了接受肝活检的慢性乙型肝炎患者,分为轻度和晚期纤维化组。
核心方法
该研究使用了血清标志物,包括年龄-血小板指数、APRI、AAR、纤维化-4指数、Fibro-Q和King’s评分,并将它们与肝活检结果进行比较。
主要结果
总共纳入 183 名患者,其中轻度纤维化组 101 名,晚期纤维化组 82 名。APRI、AAR 和 King’s 评分在检测晚期纤维化方面显示出统计学显着性。
解释与创新
APRI 和 King’s 评分在区分晚期纤维化和轻度纤维化方面具有统计学意义,但其功效有限。AAR 可能反映了炎症活动而不是实际的纤维化。
科研与公卫意义
肝活检仍然是诊断慢性乙型肝炎纤维化的金标准,需要新的研究来开发更有效的非侵入性标志物。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和有限的样本量可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。标记物的功效有限,需要进一步研究。

Comparison of Non-Invasive Markers with Liver Biopsy for Detecting Fibrosis in Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B

Authors: Hande İdil Tüz, Nihal Pişkin, Güven Çelebi, Hande Aydemir, Emre HORUZ, Çağatay Büyükuysal, Mehmet Ali Tüz, Mustafa Çağatay Büyükuysal
Original abstract (English)
Objectives Liver biopsy is the gold standard for determining liver fibrosis in chronic hepatitis B (CHB); however, alternative methods, such as non-invasive indirect serum markers, are being developed because it is an invasive procedure. This study aimed to compare fibrosis values from liver biopsies of patients with CHB to non-invasive serum markers to determine the efficacy of these markers. Materials and Methods Patients with a diagnosis of CHB who underwent a liver biopsy were retrospectively included. According to the Ishak fibrosis staging system, patients were divided into two groups: mild fibrosis (F0-2) and advanced fibrosis (F3-6). Age-platelet index, aspartate aminotransferase (AST)-to-platelet ratio index (APRI), AST/alanine aminotransferase (ALT) ratio (AAR), fibrosis-4 index, Fibro-Q, and King’s score were calculated. The markers were compared with respect to their ability to distinguish advanced fibrosis. Results A total of 183 patients were included. Of these, mild fibrosis (≤2) was detected in 101 patients and advanced fibrosis (>2) in 82. At the beginning of treatment, ALT, AST, the histological activity index, and HBV-DNA levels were significantly higher in the advanced fibrosis group (p<0.05). APRI, King’s score, and AAR (inversely) were statistically significant for detecting advanced fibrosis (p<0.05). The area under the receiver operating characteristic values for APRI, AAR, and King’s score were 0.611, 0.637, and 0.632, respectively. Conclusion APRI and King’s scores were statistically significant in differentiating advanced from mild fibrosis, but their efficacy was limited. AAR, displaying an inverse relationship, likely reflected inflammatory activity rather than actual fibrosis. Liver biopsy appears to be the gold standard for diagnosing fibrosis in patients with CHB. New studies are needed to develop non-invasive markers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare non-invasive serum markers with liver biopsy for detecting fibrosis in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB), addressing the need for less invasive methods.
Design and population
The study retrospectively included patients with CHB who underwent liver biopsies, divided into mild and advanced fibrosis groups.
Core methods
The study used serum markers including age-platelet index, APRI, AAR, fibrosis-4 index, Fibro-Q, and King’s score, comparing them with liver biopsy results.
Main results
A total of 183 patients were included, with 101 in the mild fibrosis group and 82 in the advanced fibrosis group. APRI, AAR, and King’s score showed statistical significance in detecting advanced fibrosis.
Interpretation and novelty
APRI and King’s scores were statistically significant in differentiating advanced from mild fibrosis, but their efficacy was limited. AAR likely reflected inflammatory activity rather than actual fibrosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
Liver biopsy remains the gold standard for diagnosing fibrosis in CHB, and new studies are needed to develop more effective non-invasive markers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective design and limited sample size may affect the generalizability of the findings. The markers' efficacy was limited, and further research is needed.

在巴基斯坦拉合尔和拉卡纳的综合一站式服务模式中,跨性别女性和男男性行为者的乙型和丙型肝炎负担以及丙型肝炎护理级联

Hepatitis B and C burden and HCV care cascade among transgender women and men who have sex with men in an integrated one-stop shop model in Lahore and Larkana, Pakistan
作者： Hassan Mahmood, Alina Rashid, Quaid Saeed, Zain-ul Abdeen, Syed Raza Haider Tirmizi, Sarwat Farooq, Wajid Ali, Sadam Hussain Dero, Mahnoor Chaudhary, Nayab Shahid
摘要中文翻译巴基斯坦是全球病毒性肝炎负担最重的国家之一,但跨性别女性和男男性行为者 (MSM) 中的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 数据仍然有限。这项研究评估了参加 CBO 的 MSM 和跨性别女性的乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和抗 HCV 阳性情况,并描述了综合一站式模式中的 HCV 护理级联。2019 年 10 月至 2021 年 10 月在旁遮普邦拉合尔和信德省拉卡纳进行了一项前瞻性实施研究。通过三个社区组织 (CBO) 总共招募了 2,241 名参与者。一站式模式将 HBV/HCV 筛查与 CBO 协调的 HCV RNA 确认、治疗 以及现有人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)/性传播感染 (STI) 服务中的后续工作整合在一起。使用 Abbott Diagnostics 制造的Determine HBsAg 快速诊断测试和 SD BIOLINE HCV 抗体快速诊断测试对参与者进行筛查。抗 HCV 反应性参与者在 CBO 协调的指定实验室使用 GeneXpert 进行了确认性 HCV RNA 检测。HBsAg 阳性参与者与公共部门肝炎设施有关。治疗 完成被定义为完成规定的 抗病毒药物 疗程,而持续病毒学应答 (SVR) 用于评估 治疗 后的 HCV 治愈。对 MSM 和变性女性之间的筛查和护理结果进行了比较。在 2,241 名参与者中,1,160 名(51.9%)是男男性行为者,1,081 名(48.1%)是跨性别女性。总体 HBsAg 阳性率为 3.1%,抗 HCV 阳性率为 8.9%。MSM 中的 HBsAg 阳性率高于变性女性(4.9% 比 1.1%),抗 HCV 阳性率也更高(13.4% 比 4.3%;p < 0.001)。在多变量分析中,共用剃须刀/刀片和牙刷与抗 HCV 阳性几率较高独立相关。在 201 名抗 HCV 反应性参与者中,159 名(79.1%)接受了确认性 HCV RNA 检测,其中 99 名可检测到 HCV RNA。其中,92 例启动 HCV 治疗,74 例完成 治疗,67 例实现 SVR。MSM 和变性女性之间的 治疗 完成度没有显着差异 (p = 0.264)。所有 69 名 HBsAg 阳性参与者均已与公共部门肝炎设施联系起来以进行进一步管理。基于 CBO 的 HIV/STI 计划可以为 MSM 和跨性别女性的肝炎护理提供重要的切入点,并提供将筛查、诊断 和 治疗 扩展到巴基斯坦重点人群的实用方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
巴基斯坦是全球病毒性肝炎负担最重的国家之一,但跨性别女性和男男性行为者 (MSM) 中的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
2019 年 10 月至 2021 年 10 月在旁遮普邦拉合尔和信德省拉卡纳进行了一项前瞻性实施研究。通过三个社区组织 (CBO) 总共招募了 2,241 名参与者。在 2,241 名参与者中,1,160 名(51.9%)是男男性行为者,1,081 名(48.1%)是跨性别女性。
核心方法
使用 Abbott Diagnostics 制造的Determine HBsAg 快速诊断测试和 SD BIOLINE HCV 抗体快速诊断测试对参与者进行筛查。抗 HCV 反应性参与者在 CBO 协调的指定实验室使用 GeneXpert 进行了确认性 HCV RNA 检测。HBsAg 阳性参与者与公共部门肝炎设施有关。
主要结果
总体 HBsAg 阳性率为 3.1%,抗 HCV 阳性率为 8.9%。MSM 中的 HBsAg 阳性率高于变性女性(4.9% 比 1.1%),抗 HCV 阳性率也更高(13.4% 比 4.3%;p < 0.001)。在多变量分析中,共用剃须刀/刀片和牙刷与抗 HCV 阳性几率较高独立相关。
解释与创新
一站式模式将 HBV/HCV 筛查与 CBO 协调的 HCV RNA 确认、治疗 以及现有人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)/性传播感染 (STI) 服务中的后续工作整合在一起。
科研与公卫意义
基于 CBO 的 HIV/STI 计划可以为 MSM 和跨性别女性的肝炎护理提供重要的切入点,并提供将筛查、诊断 和 治疗 扩展到巴基斯坦重点人群的实用方法。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告局限性或证据强度。

Hepatitis B and C burden and HCV care cascade among transgender women and men who have sex with men in an integrated one-stop shop model in Lahore and Larkana, Pakistan

Authors: Hassan Mahmood, Alina Rashid, Quaid Saeed, Zain-ul Abdeen, Syed Raza Haider Tirmizi, Sarwat Farooq, Wajid Ali, Sadam Hussain Dero, Mahnoor Chaudhary, Nayab Shahid
Original abstract (English)
Pakistan bears one of the highest global burdens of viral hepatitis, yet data on hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) among transgender women and men who have sex with men (MSM) remains limited. This study assessed Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and anti-HCV positivity among MSM and transgender women attending CBOs and described the HCV care cascade within an integrated one-stop-shop model. A prospective implementation study was conducted from October 2019 to October 2021 in Lahore, Punjab, and Larkana, Sindh. A total of 2,241 participants were enrolled through three community-based organizations (CBOs). The one-stop-shop model integrated HBV/HCV screening with CBO-coordinated HCV RNA confirmation, treatment, and follow-up within existing Human immunodeficiency virus (HIV)/ Sexually transmitted infection (STI) services. Participants were screened using the Determine HBsAg rapid diagnostic test and the SD BIOLINE HCV antibody rapid diagnostic test, manufactured by Abbott Diagnostics. Anti-HCV-reactive participants underwent confirmatory HCV RNA testing using GeneXpert at designated laboratories coordinated through CBOs. HBsAg-positive participants were linked to public-sector hepatitis facilities. Treatment completion was defined as completion of the prescribed antiviral course, while sustained virological response (SVR) was used to assess HCV cure following treatment. Screening and care outcomes were compared between MSM and transgender women. Among 2,241 participants, 1,160 (51.9%) were MSM and 1,081 (48.1%) were transgender women. Overall HBsAg positivity was 3.1% and anti-HCV positivity was 8.9%. HBsAg positivity was higher among MSM than transgender women (4.9% versus 1.1%), as was anti-HCV positivity (13.4% versus 4.3%; p < 0.001). In multivariable analysis, sharing razors/blades and toothbrushes were independently associated with higher odds of anti-HCV positivity. Of 201 anti-HCV-reactive participants, 159 (79.1%) underwent confirmatory HCV RNA testing, of whom 99 had detectable HCV RNA. Among these, 92 initiated HCV treatment, 74 completed treatment and 67 achieved SVR. Treatment completion did not differ significantly between MSM and transgender women (p = 0.264). All 69 HBsAg-positive participants were linked to public-sector hepatitis facilities for further management. CBO-based HIV/STI programmes can provide an important entry point for hepatitis care among MSM and transgender women and offer a practical approach to extending screening, diagnosis and treatment to key populations in Pakistan.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Pakistan bears one of the highest global burdens of viral hepatitis, yet data on hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) among transgender women and men who have sex with men (MSM) remains limited.
Design and population
A prospective implementation study was conducted from October 2019 to October 2021 in Lahore, Punjab, and Larkana, Sindh. A total of 2,241 participants were enrolled through three community-based organizations (CBOs). Among 2,241 participants, 1,160 (51.9%) were MSM and 1,081 (48.1%) were transgender women.
Core methods
Participants were screened using the Determine HBsAg rapid diagnostic test and the SD BIOLINE HCV antibody rapid diagnostic test, manufactured by Abbott Diagnostics. Anti-HCV-reactive participants underwent confirmatory HCV RNA testing using GeneXpert at designated laboratories coordinated through CBOs. HBsAg-positive participants were linked to public-sector hepatitis facilities.
Main results
Overall HBsAg positivity was 3.1% and anti-HCV positivity was 8.9%. HBsAg positivity was higher among MSM than transgender women (4.9% versus 1.1%), as was anti-HCV positivity (13.4% versus 4.3%; p < 0.001). In multivariable analysis, sharing razors/blades and toothbrushes were independently associated with higher odds of anti-HCV positivity.
Interpretation and novelty
The one-stop-shop model integrated HBV/HCV screening with CBO-coordinated HCV RNA confirmation, treatment, and follow-up within existing Human immunodeficiency virus (HIV)/ Sexually transmitted infection (STI) services.

CBO-based HIV/STI programmes can provide an important entry point for hepatitis care among MSM and transgender women and offer a practical approach to extending screening, diagnosis and treatment to key populations in Pakistan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

华支睾吸虫共感染增强 慢性乙型肝炎 患者多形核骨髓源性抑制细胞的扩增和代谢重编程

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients
作者： Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
摘要中文翻译背景已知华支睾吸虫(C. sinensis)合并感染会加速乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在两种感染流行的地区。代谢重编程已成为免疫抑制的关键驱动因素。这些代谢变化通常是由骨髓源性抑制细胞(MDSC)精心策划的,这些细胞在慢性感染期间会扩张以促进免疫耐受。中华丝虫合并感染会重新编程代谢以有利于 MDSC 相关的抑制,而这一过程与正常肝酶水平的关系仍不清楚。目的 探讨中华支原体合并感染是否会诱导肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者发生 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。方法 在这项横断面研究中,总共招募了 117 名参与者,并分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、单一感染中华支原体的参与者(n = 29)、单一感染 HBV 的参与者(n = 28)和混合感染的参与者(n = 30)。使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。进行 Spearman 相关性和中介分析来探讨炎症、代谢和免疫之间的关系。结果 HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,与HBV单一感染组相比,合并感染组的炎症细胞因子白细胞介素6(IL-6)和肿瘤坏死因子-α(TNF-α)水平较高,同时干扰素-γ(IFN-γ)水平明显较低。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。在代谢方面,共同感染组的鸟氨酸与精氨酸的比率显着较高。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。中华线虫合并感染通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高与免疫抑制相关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨华支睾吸虫合并感染是否会在肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者中诱导 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。背景强调,已知中华支原体合并感染会加速 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在流行地区。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究招募了 117 名参与者,并将他们分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、单一感染中华支原体的参与者(n = 29)、单一感染 HBV 的参与者(n = 28)和同时感染的参与者(n = 30)。
核心方法
使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。
主要结果
HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,与HBV单一感染组相比,合并感染组的炎症细胞因子白细胞介素6(IL-6)和肿瘤坏死因子-α(TNF-α)水平较高,同时干扰素-γ(IFN-γ)水平明显较低。在代谢方面,共同感染组的鸟氨酸与精氨酸的比率显着较高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,中华支线虫合并感染通过 IL-6/TNF-α 相关的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高与免疫抑制相关,从而导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为了解中华支原体合并感染的乙型肝炎患者的免疫抑制机制提供了见解,这可以为未来的免疫调节研究和靶向治疗的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,这限制了建立因果关系的能力。证据是中等的,因为它基于观察数据和统计相关性,没有纵向随访。

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients

Authors: Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Clonorchis sinensis (C. sinensis ) co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV), particularly in regions where both infections are endemic. Metabolic reprogramming has emerged as a key driver of immunosuppression. These metabolic shifts are often orchestrated by myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs), which expand during chronic infections to promote immune tolerance. sinensis co-infection reprograms metabolism to favor MDSC-linked suppression and how this process relates to normal liver enzyme levels remains unclear. AIM To investigate whether C. sinensis co-infection induces MDSC- linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. METHODS In this cross-sectional study, a total of 117 participants were enrolled and assigned to four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30). Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Spearman correlation and mediation analyses were performed to explore the relationships among inflammation, metabolism, and immunity. RESULTS HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, compared with the HBV mono-infected group, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels. Metabolically, the co-infected group had a significantly higher ornithine-to-arginine ratio. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether Clonorchis sinensis co-infection induces MDSC-linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. The background highlights that C. sinensis co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV), particularly in endemic regions.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study enrolled 117 participants and assigned them to four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30).
Core methods
Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels.
Main results
HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, compared with the HBV mono-infected group, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Metabolically, the co-infected group had a significantly higher ornithine-to-arginine ratio.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that C. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the mechanisms of immunosuppression in HBV patients co-infected with C. sinensis, which could inform future studies on immune modulation and the development of targeted therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, which limits the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data and statistical correlations without longitudinal follow-up.

集成重组酶辅助扩增-入侵系统:用于 乙型肝炎病毒 检测的闭管视觉平台

Integrated recombinase-aided amplification–invader system: a closed-tube visual platform for hepatitis B virus detection
作者： Shan Chen, Zhaohui Li, Xiu Han, Shuyun Pang
摘要中文翻译背景 快速、准确的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 检测对于 HBV 感染的早期管理至关重要。实时定量PCR(qPCR)依赖于复杂的热循环仪器,这极大地限制了其在即时检测(POCT)中的应用。方法 本研究开发了一种集成重组酶辅助扩增入侵系统 (IRIS),用于视觉和灵敏的 HBV DNA 检测。通过将集成的扩增-入侵策略应用于RAA并优化FEN1酶剂量,我们提高了反应兼容性,同时有效降低了总体时间成本。在 490 nm 处激发的荧光信号可以用肉眼观察到,并可以通过带有 520 nm 滤光片的智能手机成像进行记录,从而消除了对定制检测设备的依赖。结果 IRIS 表现出良好的特异性,每个反应的检测限为 10 个拷贝。此外,对临床 HBV 样本的检测显示,诊断与商业 qPCR 检测完全一致。结论 这种简化的、独立于设备的检测方法为资源有限环境下的现场 HBV 检测提供了可行的 POCT 解决方案。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
快速、准确的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 检测对于 HBV 感染的早期治疗至关重要。
设计与对象
实时定量PCR(qPCR)依赖于复杂的热循环仪器,这极大地限制了其在即时检测(POCT)中的应用。
核心方法
方法 本研究开发了一种集成重组酶辅助扩增入侵系统 (IRIS),用于视觉和灵敏的 HBV DNA 检测。
主要结果
IRIS 表现出良好的特异性,每个反应的检测限为 10 个拷贝。此外,对临床 HBV 样本的检测显示,诊断与商业 qPCR 检测完全一致。
解释与创新
这种简化的、独立于设备的检测方法为资源有限环境下的现场 HBV 检测提供了可行的 POCT 解决方案。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为在先进实验室设备有限的环境中改善 HBV 监测 和 诊断 提供了一种有前景的工具。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告局限性或证据强度。

Integrated recombinase-aided amplification–invader system: a closed-tube visual platform for hepatitis B virus detection

Authors: Shan Chen, Zhaohui Li, Xiu Han, Shuyun Pang
Original abstract (English)
Background Rapid and accurate hepatitis B virus (HBV) detection is essential for the early management of HBV infection. Quantitative real-time PCR (qPCR) depends on sophisticated thermal cycling instruments, which greatly restricts its application in point-of-care testing (POCT). Methods Herein, this study developed an Integrated Recombinase-Aided Amplification–invader System (IRIS) for visual and sensitive HBV DNA detection. By adapting the integrated amplification-invader strategy to RAA and optimizing the FEN1 enzyme dosage, we achieved improved reaction compatibility while effectively reducing the overall time cost. Fluorescent signals excited at 490 nm are observable by naked eye and recordable via smartphone imaging with a 520 nm filter, removing reliance on customized detection devices. Results IRIS exhibited favorable specificity and a detection limit of 10 copies per reaction. Furthermore, testing of clinical HBV specimens revealed full diagnostic concordance with commercial qPCR assays. Conclusion This simplified, equipment-independent detection method provides a feasible POCT solution for on-site HBV detection in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rapid and accurate hepatitis B virus (HBV) detection is essential for the early management of HBV infection.
Design and population
Quantitative real-time PCR (qPCR) depends on sophisticated thermal cycling instruments, which greatly restricts its application in point-of-care testing (POCT).
Core methods
Methods Herein, this study developed an Integrated Recombinase-Aided Amplification–invader System (IRIS) for visual and sensitive HBV DNA detection.
Main results
IRIS exhibited favorable specificity and a detection limit of 10 copies per reaction. Furthermore, testing of clinical HBV specimens revealed full diagnostic concordance with commercial qPCR assays.
Interpretation and novelty
This simplified, equipment-independent detection method provides a feasible POCT solution for on-site HBV detection in resource-limited settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a promising tool for improving HBV surveillance and diagnosis in settings where access to advanced laboratory equipment is limited.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

芬太尼、海洛因和四氢大麻酚改变 乙型肝炎病毒 抗原产生、NTCP 表达和肝细胞转录谱

Fentanyl, Heroin, and Tetrahydrocannabinol Alter Hepatitis B Virus Antigen Production, NTCP Expression, and Hepatocyte Transcriptional Profiles
作者： Janani Madhuravasal Krishnan, Susan D. Rouster, Heidi L. Meeds, Krishna M. Roskin, Mario Medvedovic, Lufuno Ratshisusu, Omphile E. Simani, Selokela G. Selabe, Jason T. Blackard
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:物质使用可能通过对肝细胞的影响而影响 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 发病机制,其影响超出了 传播 风险。本研究探讨了芬太尼、海洛因和四氢大麻酚 (THC) 如何影响体外 HBV 表面抗原水平和 宿主 细胞反应。方法:将产生 HBV 的 HepG2.2.15 细胞和原代人肝细胞 (PHH) 暴露于芬太尼、海洛因或 THC。通过 ELISA 测定乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和牛磺胆酸钠共转运多肽 (NTCP) 蛋白水平,并通过 RT-qPCR 定量 SLC10A1 mRNA 表达。使用 M30 CytoDeath ELISA 测量细胞相关的 caspase 裂解的 cytokeratin-18。分别通过RNA测序和microRNA测序来分析宿主基因和microRNA表达。结果:所有三种药物均增加了 HepG2.2.15 细胞中的 HBsAg 和 NTCP 蛋白水平。在 PHH 中,所有三种药物均增加了 NTCP 蛋白丰度,芬太尼和 THC 显着增加了 SLC10A1 mRNA 表达。M30 信号在 治疗 条件下没有显着差异。芬太尼暴露与最大数量的差异表达基因相关,而 THC 暴露与最大数量的满足探索性显着性阈值的 microRNA 相关。结论:芬太尼、海洛因和 THC 增加了 HBV 产生细胞中的 HBsAg 水平以及两种肝细胞模型中的 NTCP 蛋白丰度。这些药物对 宿主 基因和 microRNA 表达的影响不同。需要进一步的研究来确定这些变化是否影响乙肝病毒的进入、复制或生产性感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
物质使用可能通过对肝细胞的影响而影响 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 发病机制,其影响超出了 传播 风险。
设计与对象
本研究使用产生 HBV 的 HepG2.2.15 细胞和原代人肝细胞 (PHH),在体外研究了芬太尼、海洛因和四氢大麻酚 (THC) 如何影响 HBV 表面抗原水平和 宿主 细胞反应。
核心方法
产生 HBV 的 HepG2.2.15 细胞和原代人肝细胞 (PHH) 暴露于芬太尼、海洛因或 THC。通过 ELISA 测定乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和牛磺胆酸钠共转运多肽 (NTCP) 蛋白水平,并通过 RT-qPCR 定量 SLC10A1 mRNA 表达。使用 M30 CytoDeath ELISA 测量细胞相关的 caspase 裂解的 cytokeratin-18。分别通过RNA测序和microRNA测序来分析宿主基因和microRNA表达。
主要结果
所有三种药物均增加 HepG2.2.15 细胞中的 HBsAg 和 NTCP 蛋白水平。在 PHH 中,所有三种药物均增加了 NTCP 蛋白丰度,芬太尼和 THC 显着增加了 SLC10A1 mRNA 表达。芬太尼暴露与最大数量的差异表达基因相关,而 THC 暴露与最大数量的满足探索性显着性阈值的 microRNA 相关。
解释与创新
芬太尼、海洛因和 THC 增加了 HBV 产生细胞中的 HBsAg 水平以及两种肝细胞模型中的 NTCP 蛋白丰度。这些药物对 宿主 基因和 microRNA 表达的影响不同。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来关于药物使用如何影响 HBV 发病机制 和 宿主 反应的研究提供信息,从而有可能指导有针对性的干预措施的制定。
局限与证据强度
需要进一步的研究来确定这些变化是否影响乙肝病毒的进入、复制或生产性感染。由于该研究仅限于体外模型并且没有解决体内效应,因此证据是中等的。

Fentanyl, Heroin, and Tetrahydrocannabinol Alter Hepatitis B Virus Antigen Production, NTCP Expression, and Hepatocyte Transcriptional Profiles

Authors: Janani Madhuravasal Krishnan, Susan D. Rouster, Heidi L. Meeds, Krishna M. Roskin, Mario Medvedovic, Lufuno Ratshisusu, Omphile E. Simani, Selokela G. Selabe, Jason T. Blackard
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Substance use may influence hepatitis B virus (HBV) pathogenesis through effects on hepatocytes that extend beyond transmission risk. This study examined how fentanyl, heroin, and tetrahydrocannabinol (THC) affect HBV surface antigen levels and host cellular responses in vitro. Methods: HBV-producing HepG2.2.15 cells and primary human hepatocytes (PHHs) were exposed to fentanyl, heroin, or THC. Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and sodium taurocholate cotransporting polypeptide (NTCP) protein levels were measured by ELISA, and SLC10A1 mRNA expression was quantified by RT-qPCR. Cell-associated caspase-cleaved cytokeratin-18 was measured using the M30 CytoDeath ELISA. Host gene and microRNA expression were profiled by RNA sequencing and microRNA sequencing, respectively. Results: All three drugs increased HBsAg and NTCP protein levels in HepG2.2.15 cells. In PHHs, all three drugs increased NTCP protein abundance, and fentanyl and THC significantly increased SLC10A1 mRNA expression. M30 signals did not differ significantly across treatment conditions. Fentanyl exposure was associated with the largest number of differentially expressed genes, whereas THC exposure was associated with the largest number of microRNAs meeting the exploratory significance threshold. Conclusions: Fentanyl, heroin, and THC increased HBsAg levels in HBV-producing cells and NTCP protein abundance in both hepatocyte models. The drugs differed in their effects on host gene and microRNA expression. Further studies are needed to determine whether these changes affect HBV entry, replication, or productive infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Substance use may influence hepatitis B virus (HBV) pathogenesis through effects on hepatocytes that extend beyond transmission risk.
Design and population
This study examined how fentanyl, heroin, and tetrahydrocannabinol (THC) affect HBV surface antigen levels and host cellular responses in vitro using HBV-producing HepG2.2.15 cells and primary human hepatocytes (PHHs).
Core methods
HBV-producing HepG2.2.15 cells and primary human hepatocytes (PHHs) were exposed to fentanyl, heroin, or THC. Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and sodium taurocholate cotransporting polypeptide (NTCP) protein levels were measured by ELISA, and SLC10A1 mRNA expression was quantified by RT-qPCR. Cell-associated caspase-cleaved cytokeratin-18 was measured using the M30 CytoDeath ELISA. Host gene and microRNA expression were profiled by RNA sequencing and microRNA sequencing, respectively.
Main results
All three drugs increased HBsAg and NTCP protein levels in HepG2.2.15 cells. In PHHs, all three drugs increased NTCP protein abundance, and fentanyl and THC significantly increased SLC10A1 mRNA expression. Fentanyl exposure was associated with the largest number of differentially expressed genes, whereas THC exposure was associated with the largest number of microRNAs meeting the exploratory significance threshold.
Interpretation and novelty
Fentanyl, heroin, and THC increased HBsAg levels in HBV-producing cells and NTCP protein abundance in both hepatocyte models. The drugs differed in their effects on host gene and microRNA expression.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future studies on how substance use impacts HBV pathogenesis and host responses, potentially guiding the development of targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further studies are needed to determine whether these changes affect HBV entry, replication, or productive infection. The evidence is moderate as the study is limited to in vitro models and does not address in vivo effects.

塞内加尔圣路易斯中学生乙型肝炎知识、血清保护状况和 HBsAg 患病率:一项横断面研究

Hepatitis B Knowledge, Seroprotection Status, and HBsAg Prevalence among Middle School Students in Saint-Louis, Senegal: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Alassane Dièye, E. B. Diakhaby, Sidy Diallo, Rose Dibor Faye, Papa Latyr Junior Diouf, Ndiogou Seck, Mamadou Moustapha Diop, Maimouna Sow, Djiby Niang, Nguaisiry Mbaye
摘要中文翻译简介:尽管有有效的 疫苗,乙型病毒性肝炎 (VHB) 仍然是塞内加尔的一个主要公共卫生问题。目的:本研究的目的是评估塞内加尔圣路易斯中学生 疫苗接种 后的免疫状况和 VHB 知识水平。材料和方法:我们于 2024 年 6 月 5 日至 25 日在圣路易斯的中学生中进行了一项横断面、描述性和分析性研究,并征得其父母或法定监护人的自由和知情同意。使用谷歌表单调查问卷收集数据。使用一站式 HBsAg 测试来检测 HBsAg,并使用 MAGLUMI 800 系统来检测抗 HBs 抗体,对血液样本进行 乙型肝炎病毒 血清学 分析。抗 HBs 抗体水平≥10 IU/L 的中学生被认为对 VHB 具有血清保护。结果:研究期间,共纳入280名学生。只有 11 名学生 (4%) 表示听说过 VHB。两名学生(0.7%)知道病原体是病毒。没有学生表示知道自己接种了 VHB 疫苗。几乎所有学生(99.6%)都没有提供有关 VHB 传播 模式的任何答案。他们报告不了解 VHB 的临床症状、并发症或预防措施。所有中学生的 HBsAg 检测均为阴性。平均抗 HBs 抗体水平为 21.2 IU/L (±73.2)。72名中学生抗HBs抗体水平≥10 IU/L,血清保护率为25.7%。结论:我们的结果表明,圣路易斯中学生对 VHB 的了解非常有限。没有发现 HBsAg 阳性学生。略多于四分之一的人的抗 HBs 抗体水平达到血清保护阈值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管有有效的 疫苗,乙型病毒性肝炎 (VHB) 仍然是塞内加尔的一个主要公共卫生问题。本研究的目的是评估塞内加尔圣路易斯中学生 疫苗接种 后的免疫状况和 VHB 知识水平。
设计与对象
在征得家长或法定监护人的自由和知情同意后,我们于 2024 年 6 月 5 日至 25 日对圣路易斯的中学生进行了一项横断面、描述性和分析性研究。研究期间,共有280名学生。
核心方法
使用谷歌表单调查问卷收集数据。使用一站式 HBsAg 测试来检测 HBsAg,并使用 MAGLUMI 800 系统来检测抗 HBs 抗体,对血液样本进行 乙型肝炎病毒 血清学 分析。
主要结果
只有 11 名学生 (4%) 表示听说过 VHB。两名学生(0.7%)知道病原体是病毒。没有学生表示知道自己接种了 VHB 疫苗。几乎所有学生(99.6%)都没有提供有关 VHB 传播 模式的任何答案。他们报告不了解 VHB 的临床症状、并发症或预防措施。所有中学生的 HBsAg 检测均为阴性。平均抗 HBs 抗体水平为 21.2 IU/L (±73.2)。72名中学生抗HBs抗体水平≥10 IU/L,血清保护率为25.7%。
解释与创新
我们的结果表明,圣路易斯中学生对 VHB 的了解非常有限。略多于四分之一的人的抗 HBs 抗体水平达到血清保护阈值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要改进学校的乙型肝炎教育,以提高认识和预防措施。他们还表明,很大一部分学生在遵循 疫苗接种 后可能没有足够的免疫力。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在一个地点进行的,可能无法推广到其他地区。证据基于自我报告的数据和血清学检测,这在获取综合知识或免疫状态方面可能存在局限性。信心是中等的。

Hepatitis B Knowledge, Seroprotection Status, and HBsAg Prevalence among Middle School Students in Saint-Louis, Senegal: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Alassane Dièye, E. B. Diakhaby, Sidy Diallo, Rose Dibor Faye, Papa Latyr Junior Diouf, Ndiogou Seck, Mamadou Moustapha Diop, Maimouna Sow, Djiby Niang, Nguaisiry Mbaye
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Despite the availability of an effective vaccine, viral hepatitis B (VHB) is a major public health problem in Senegal. Aim: The objective of this study was to assess the post-vaccination immunisation status and the level of knowledge about VHB among middle school students in Saint-Louis, Senegal. Materials and Methods: We conducted a cross-sectional, descriptive and analytical study among middle school students in Saint-Louis from 5 to 25 June 2024, following the free and informed consent of their parents or legal guardians. Data were collected using a Google Forms questionnaire. Blood samples were analysed for hepatitis B virus serology using the ONE STEP HBsAg TEST to detect HBsAg and the MAGLUMI 800 system to detect anti-HBs antibodies. Middle school students with an anti-HBs antibody level ≥10 IU/L were considered to be seroprotected against VHB. Results: During the study period, 280 students were included. Only 11 students (4%) reported having heard of VHB. Two students (0.7%) knew that the causative agent is a virus. None of the students reported knowing that they had been vaccinated against VHB. Nearly all students (99.6%) did not provide any answer about the modes of VHB transmission. They reported no knowledge of the clinical signs, complications or preventive measures of VHB. The HBsAg test was negative in all middle school students. The mean anti-HBs antibody level was 21.2 IU/L (±73.2). An anti-HBs antibody level ≥10 IU/L was found in 72 middle school students, representing a seroprotection rate of 25.7%. Conclusion: Our results suggest that knowledge of VHB among middle school students in Saint-Louis was very limited. No HBsAg-positive students were identified. Slightly more than one-quarter had anti-HBs antibody levels meeting the seroprotection threshold.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite the availability of an effective vaccine, viral hepatitis B (VHB) is a major public health problem in Senegal. The objective of this study was to assess the post-vaccination immunisation status and the level of knowledge about VHB among middle school students in Saint-Louis, Senegal.
Design and population
We conducted a cross-sectional, descriptive and analytical study among middle school students in Saint-Louis from 5 to 25 June 2024, following the free and informed consent of their parents or legal guardians. During the study period, 280 students were included.
Core methods
Data were collected using a Google Forms questionnaire. Blood samples were analysed for hepatitis B virus serology using the ONE STEP HBsAg TEST to detect HBsAg and the MAGLUMI 800 system to detect anti-HBs antibodies.
Main results
Only 11 students (4%) reported having heard of VHB. Two students (0.7%) knew that the causative agent is a virus. None of the students reported knowing that they had been vaccinated against VHB. Nearly all students (99.6%) did not provide any answer about the modes of VHB transmission. They reported no knowledge of the clinical signs, complications or preventive measures of VHB. The HBsAg test was negative in all middle school students. The mean anti-HBs antibody level was 21.2 IU/L (±73.2). An anti-HBs antibody level ≥10 IU/L was found in 72 middle school students, representing a seroprotection rate of 25.7%.
Interpretation and novelty
Our results suggest that knowledge of VHB among middle school students in Saint-Louis was very limited. Slightly more than one-quarter had anti-HBs antibody levels meeting the seroprotection threshold.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for improved hepatitis B education in schools to enhance awareness and preventive measures. They also suggest that a significant proportion of students may not have adequate immunity following vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single location and may not be generalizable to other regions. The evidence is based on self-reported data and serological testing, which may have limitations in capturing comprehensive knowledge or immunity status. The confidence is moderate.
