A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

HBVCare 智能手机应用程序在 慢性乙型肝炎 中改善短期 治疗 依从性的有效性和患者体验:随机对照试验(预印本)

Effectiveness and Patient Experience of the HBVCare Smartphone App for Improving Short-Term Treatment Adherence in Chronic Hepatitis B: Randomized Controlled Trial (Preprint)
作者： Hang Viet Dao, Trung Quoc Vu, Hoa Ngoc Lam, Thuong Nguyen Thu, Uyen Vu Thi Thu, Thao Dang Thi, Long Bao Hoang, Long Van Dao, Thuong Thu Nguyen, Uyen Thi Thu Vu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 需要长期坚持用药以预防严重的肝脏并发症。本研究旨在评估将 HBVCare 智能手机应用程序添加到标准护理中是否可以提高 治疗 患者 3 个月时的依从性,并确定与依从性相关的因素并描述用户体验。
设计与对象
我们在越南河内的两个地点进行了一项多中心、单盲、平行组随机对照试验。符合 抗病毒药物 治疗 资格的 慢性乙型肝炎 成人按 1:1 随机分配至干预组(HBVCare 和标准护理;n=51)或对照组(标准护理;n=52)。
核心方法
使用 UMUX-Lite 量表评估用户体验。对参与者进行随机分析,仅限于具有 3 个月结果数据的参与者(完整病例分析)。
主要结果
Of 103 randomized participants, 76 (73.8%) had outcome data (intervention group: n=40; control group: n=36). Good medication adherence was higher in the intervention group than in the control group (34/40, 85% vs 20/36, 55.6%; risk difference 29.4%, 95% CI 9.8 to 49.1; P=.01). On-schedule follow-up did not differ significantly (20/40, 50% vs 14/36, 38.9%; risk difference 11.1%, 95% CI –11.1 to 33.3; P=.46).
解释与创新
在这项试验中,将 HBVCare 添加到标准护理中与有结果数据的参与者在 3 个月时自我报告的药物依从性较高相关,但与按计划随访无关,并且一半的干预参与者停止使用该应用程序。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,移动健康应用程序可能是提高 慢性乙型肝炎 患者短期药物依从性的有用工具,但需要采用客观依从性措施进行更长期的试验来证实这些结果。
局限与证据强度
解释受到失访、自我报告结果和短期随访的限制。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

Effectiveness and Patient Experience of the HBVCare Smartphone App for Improving Short-Term Treatment Adherence in Chronic Hepatitis B: Randomized Controlled Trial (Preprint)

Authors: Hang Viet Dao, Trung Quoc Vu, Hoa Ngoc Lam, Thuong Nguyen Thu, Uyen Vu Thi Thu, Thao Dang Thi, Long Bao Hoang, Long Van Dao, Thuong Thu Nguyen, Uyen Thi Thu Vu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Chronic hepatitis B requires long-term medication adherence to prevent severe liver complications. Randomized evidence on mobile health apps for this purpose is limited, particularly in Vietnam. OBJECTIVE This study aimed to evaluate whether adding the HBVCare smartphone app to standard care improves treatment adherence at 3 months among patients with chronic hepatitis B, and to identify factors associated with adherence and describe user experience. METHODS We conducted a multicenter, single-blind, parallel-group randomized controlled trial at two sites in Hanoi, Vietnam. Adults with chronic hepatitis B who were eligible for antiviral treatment were randomized 1:1 to the intervention group (HBVCare and standard care; n=51) or the control group (standard care; n=52). The co-primary outcomes were medication adherence at 3 months (a perfect score of 25 on the Medication Adherence Report Scale-5 [MARS-5]) and follow-up adherence (a clinic visit within ±7 days of the scheduled date, verified from clinic records). Participants were analyzed as randomized, restricted to those with 3-month outcome data (complete-case analysis). User experience was assessed with the UMUX-Lite scale. RESULTS Of 103 randomized participants, 76 (73.8%) had outcome data (intervention group: n=40; control group: n=36). Good medication adherence was higher in the intervention group than in the control group (34/40, 85% vs 20/36, 55.6%; risk difference 29.4%, 95% CI 9.8 to 49.1; P=.01). On-schedule follow-up did not differ significantly (20/40, 50% vs 14/36, 38.9%; risk difference 11.1%, 95% CI –11.1 to 33.3; P=.46). At 3 months, 20/40 (50%) of the intervention participants were still using the app. Continuers were not significantly younger than discontinuers (mean age 37.0, SD 6.9 years vs 43.1, SD 13.7 years; P=.09), and 28/40 (70%) participants rated usability as acceptable. CONCLUSIONS In this trial, adding HBVCare to standard care was associated with higher self-reported medication adherence at 3 months among participants with outcome data, but not with on-schedule follow-up, and half of the intervention participants stopped using the app. Interpretation is limited by loss to follow-up, a self-reported outcome, and short follow-up. Longer trials with objective adherence measures are needed. CLINICALTRIAL ClinicalTrials.gov NCT07540637
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B requires long-term medication adherence to prevent severe liver complications. This study aimed to evaluate whether adding the HBVCare smartphone app to standard care improves treatment adherence at 3 months among patients with chronic hepatitis B, and to identify factors associated with adherence and describe user experience.
Design and population
We conducted a multicenter, single-blind, parallel-group randomized controlled trial at two sites in Hanoi, Vietnam. Adults with chronic hepatitis B who were eligible for antiviral treatment were randomized 1:1 to the intervention group (HBVCare and standard care; n=51) or the control group (standard care; n=52).
Core methods
User experience was assessed with the UMUX-Lite scale. Participants were analyzed as randomized, restricted to those with 3-month outcome data (complete-case analysis).
Main results
Of 103 randomized participants, 76 (73.8%) had outcome data (intervention group: n=40; control group: n=36). The findings suggest that mobile health apps may be a useful tool for improving short-term medication adherence in patients with chronic hepatitis B, but longer trials with objective adherence measures are needed to confirm these results.
Limitations and evidence strength
Interpretation is limited by loss to follow-up, a self-reported outcome, and short follow-up. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

定义噻唑化物 抗病毒药物 的结构-活性关系:平衡效力、代谢稳定性和水溶性。

Defining the Structure-Activity Relationships of Thiazolide Antivirals: Balancing Potency, Metabolic Stability, and Aqueous Solubility.
作者： Joshua Taujanskas, Sophie Pate, Andrew V Stachulski, Sara Piacentini, Simone La Frazia, M Gabriella Santoro, Paul M O'Neill
摘要中文翻译最近对噻唑化物 抗病毒药物 的研究已确定化合物 RM5038 和 RM5021 是 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和甲型流感病毒的有效抑制剂,尽管它们的开发受到水溶性差和快速代谢清除的限制。在这项工作中,使用更新的聚合合成方法和灵活的保护基策略合成了一系列新型卤素取代和4'-烷基砜噻唑化物类似物。DMPK 评估表明,3-X 卤素取代显着提高了水溶性,同时增强了代谢稳定性。在氯噻唑系列中,化合物 10g 和 10l 显示出大大改善的代谢稳定性、优异的水溶性(比母体药物提高了 10 倍)、低个位数微摩尔 抗病毒药物(甲型流感)活性和可接受的体外安全指数。对 4'-烷基砜噻唑烷的评估还揭示了体外 DMPK 特性的改善,同时保留了微摩尔 抗病毒药物 效力。SAR 分析表明,含有 2 至 3 个碳的短烷基砜取代基对于 抗病毒药物 活性是最佳的,而支链烷基可能提供额外的代谢优势。总之,化合物 10g、10l 和 15d 代表了进行体内分析和其他临床前评估的有希望的早期先导化合物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决开发噻唑化物 抗病毒药物 的挑战,该噻唑化物 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的 治疗 具有更高的效力、代谢稳定性和水溶性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及新型噻唑烷类似物的合成及其 抗病毒药物 抗 HBV 活性的评估,重点是优化其理化性质。
核心方法
研究人员使用更新的聚合合成方法和灵活的保护基策略合成了一系列卤素取代的4'-烷基砜噻唑化物类似物。然后他们评估了药物的 DMPK 特征和 抗病毒药物 的体外效力。
主要结果
DMPK评价表明,3-X卤素取代显着提高了水溶性和代谢稳定性。化合物 10g 和 10l 表现出大大改善的代谢稳定性、优异的水溶性(比母体药物提高 10 倍)以及针对甲型流感病毒的低个位数微摩尔 抗病毒药物 活性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,特定的结构修饰可以增强噻唑烷类化合物的理化性质,而不影响其 抗病毒药物 效力,为 HBV 药物设计提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为开发更有效的抗 HBV 的 抗病毒药物 药物奠定了基础,可能改善 治疗 的选择并减少当前噻唑类药物的局限性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外评估,证据仅基于摘要数据,可能无法完全反映体内疗效或安全性。这些发现的可信度是中等的。

Defining the Structure-Activity Relationships of Thiazolide Antivirals: Balancing Potency, Metabolic Stability, and Aqueous Solubility.

Authors: Joshua Taujanskas, Sophie Pate, Andrew V Stachulski, Sara Piacentini, Simone La Frazia, M Gabriella Santoro, Paul M O'Neill
Original abstract (English)
Recent studies on thiazolide antivirals have identified compounds RM5038 and RM5021 as potent inhibitors of hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and influenza A, although their development has been limited by poor aqueous solubility and rapid metabolic clearance. In this work, a series of novel halogen-substituted and 4'-alkylsulfone thiazolide analogs were synthesized using updated convergent synthetic approaches and flexible protecting group strategies. DMPK evaluation demonstrated that 3-X halogen substitution significantly improved aqueous solubility in parallel to enhancing metabolic stability. Within the chlorothiazolide series, compounds 10gand 10l displayed much improved metabolic stability profiles, excellent aqueous solubility representing a 10-fold improvement over the parent drug, together with low single-digit micromolar antiviral (influenza A) activity and acceptable in vitro safety indices. Evaluation of the 4'-alkylsulfone thiazolides also revealed improved in vitro DMPK profiles while retaining micromolar antiviral potency. SAR analysis indicated that short alkyl sulfone substituents containing two to three carbons are optimal for antiviral activity, while branched alkyl groups may provide additional metabolic advantages. In summary, compounds 10g, 10l, and 15d represent promising early leads for progression to in vivo profiling and additional preclinical evaluation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of developing thiazolide antivirals with improved potency, metabolic stability, and aqueous solubility for the treatment of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infections.
Design and population
The study involved the synthesis of novel thiazolide analogs and their evaluation for antiviral activity against HBV, with a focus on optimizing their physicochemical properties.
Core methods
The researchers synthesized a series of halogen-substituted and 4'-alkylsulfone thiazolide analogs using updated convergent synthetic approaches and flexible protecting group strategies. They then evaluated the drugs' DMPK profiles and antiviral potency in vitro.
Main results
DMPK evaluation showed that 3-X halogen substitution significantly improved aqueous solubility and metabolic stability. Compounds 10g and 10l demonstrated much improved metabolic stability profiles, excellent aqueous solubility (10-fold improvement over the parent drug), and low single-digit micromolar antiviral activity against influenza A.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that specific structural modifications can enhance the physicochemical properties of thiazolides without compromising their antiviral potency, offering a novel approach to drug design for HBV.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a foundation for the development of more effective antiviral agents against HBV, potentially improving treatment options and reducing the limitations of current thiazolide drugs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro evaluations, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, which may not fully reflect in vivo efficacy or safety. The confidence in these findings is moderate.

一项基于横断面问卷的研究,旨在评估三级护理教学医院医护人员对针刺伤和暴露后预防的知识、态度和实践

A Cross-Sectional Questionnaire-Based Study to Assess Knowledge, Attitude and Practice Regarding Needle Stick Injury and PostExposure Prophylaxis among Healthcare Workers in a Tertiary Care Teaching Hospital
作者： ANISHA FIRTHOUZ. A, SOUNDARYA PRIYADHARSINI, Maduram Annamalai, Kumaresan Kumaresan, Vivek Valliappan
摘要中文翻译背景:针刺伤(NSI)是医护人员常见的职业危害,可能暴露于血液传播病原体,如人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)。尽管有标准预防措施、乙型肝炎疫苗和暴露后预防(PEP),由于不安全操作、报告不足以及暴露后管理的漏洞,针刺伤仍持续发生。因此,评估医护人员对针刺伤和暴露后预防的知识、态度和行为对于识别可预防的漏洞并加强职业安全措施至关重要。目的:评估某三级教学医院医护人员对针刺伤和暴露后预防(PEP)的知识、态度和行为(KAP)。方法:在医院内进行了一项基于横断面的问卷调查,调查了166名医护人员。使用结构化问卷收集数据,评估人口统计学特征、对针刺伤和PEP的知识、对职业安全的态度以及预防和暴露后的行为。知识评估包含7个问题,态度评估包含5个李克特量表陈述,行为评估包含8个问题。数据使用描述性统计和适当的关联性检验进行分析。结果:研究人群的平均年龄为28.73 ± 6.88岁,女性占67.5%。总体上对针刺伤和PEP的知识良好。94.6%的人认识到通过针刺伤传播HIV、HBV和HCV的风险,97.6%的人认为用肥皂和水清洗是适当的即时急救措施,97.0%的人认识到针尖回套是不安全的。态度总体积极,93.4%的人认为针刺伤是严重的职业危害,95.8%的人支持报告职业暴露。然而,33.7%的人报告曾经历针刺伤。在暴露人群中,只有60.7%的人报告了受伤,57.1%的人接受了PEP。尽管98.8%的人报告了适当的锐器处理,但46.4%的人报告总是或有时回套针头。乙型肝炎疫苗接种率是92.8%。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
针刺伤 (NSI) 是医护人员常见的职业危害,可能会接触人体免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 等血源性病原体。尽管有标准预防措施、乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 和暴露后预防 (PEP),但由于不安全的做法、报告不充分以及暴露后管理方面的差距,NSI 仍然发生。
设计与对象
对 166 名医护人员进行了一项以医院为基础的横断面问卷研究。研究人群的平均年龄为28.73±6.88岁,其中女性占67.5%。
核心方法
使用结构化调查问卷收集数据,评估人口特征、有关 NSI 和 PEP 的知识、对职业安全的态度以及预防和暴露后做法。知识使用七个项目进行评估,态度使用五个李克特量表陈述进行评估,实践使用八个项目进行评估。使用描述性统计和适当的关联测试来分析数据。
主要结果
总体上对针刺伤和PEP的知识良好。94.6%的人认识到通过针刺伤传播HIV、HBV和HCV的风险,97.6%的人认为用肥皂和水清洗是适当的即时急救措施,97.0%的人认识到针尖回套是不安全的。然而,33.7%的人报告曾经历针刺伤。在暴露人群中,只有60.7%的人报告了受伤,57.1%的人接受了PEP。尽管98.8%的人报告了适当的锐器处理,但46.4%的人报告总是或有时回套针头。
解释与创新
该研究强调需要改进对安全协议的遵守和更好的报告机制,以减少 NSI 的 发病率 并确保及时的暴露后预防。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,以改善职业安全实践并加强医护人员的暴露后管理方案。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一家三级护理教学医院,可能无法推广到其他环境。证据是中等的,因为它基于自我报告的数据,不包括直接的生物学或临床结果。

A Cross-Sectional Questionnaire-Based Study to Assess Knowledge, Attitude and Practice Regarding Needle Stick Injury and PostExposure Prophylaxis among Healthcare Workers in a Tertiary Care Teaching Hospital

Authors: ANISHA FIRTHOUZ. A, SOUNDARYA PRIYADHARSINI, Maduram Annamalai, Kumaresan Kumaresan, Vivek Valliappan
Original abstract (English)
Background: Needle stick injuries (NSIs) are a common occupational hazard among healthcare workers, with potential exposure to blood-borne pathogens such as human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), and hepatitis C virus (HCV). Despite the availability of standard precautions, hepatitis B vaccination, and postexposure prophylaxis (PEP), NSIs continue to occur due to unsafe practices, inadequate reporting, and gaps in postexposure management. Assessing healthcare workers’ knowledge, attitudes, and practices is therefore essential for identifying preventable gaps and strengthening occupational safety measures. Objective: To assess the knowledge, attitude and practices (KAP) regarding needle stick injuries and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) among healthcare workers in a tertiary-care teaching hospital. Methods: A hospital-based cross-sectional questionnaire study was conducted among 166 healthcare workers. Data were collected using a structured questionnaire assessing demographic characteristics, knowledge regarding NSIs and PEP, attitudes towards occupational safety, and preventive and post-exposure practices. Knowledge was assessed using seven items, attitude using five Likert-scale statements, and practices using eight items. Data were analysed using descriptive statistics and appropriate tests of association. Results: The study population had a mean age of 28.73 ± 6.88 years, with females comprising 67.5%. Overall knowledge regarding NSIs and PEP was good. 94.6% recognized transmission of HIV, HBV and HCV through NSIs, 97.6% identified washing with soap and water as the appropriate immediate first-aid measure, and 97.0% recognized needle recapping as unsafe. Attitudes were predominantly positive, with 93.4% considering NSI a serious occupational hazard and 95.8% supporting reporting of occupational exposures. However, 33.7% reported having experienced an NSI. Among those exposed, only 60.7% reported the injury and 57.1% received PEP. Although 98.8% reported appropriate sharps disposal, 46.4% reported always or sometimes recapping needles. Hepatitis B vaccination coverage was 92.8%.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Needle stick injuries (NSIs) are a common occupational hazard among healthcare workers, with potential exposure to blood-borne pathogens such as human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), and hepatitis C virus (HCV). Despite the availability of standard precautions, hepatitis B vaccination, and postexposure prophylaxis (PEP), NSIs continue to occur due to unsafe practices, inadequate reporting, and gaps in postexposure management.

Core methods
Data were collected using a structured questionnaire assessing demographic characteristics, knowledge regarding NSIs and PEP, attitudes towards occupational safety, and preventive and post-exposure practices. Knowledge was assessed using seven items, attitude using five Likert-scale statements, and practices using eight items. Data were analysed using descriptive statistics and appropriate tests of association.
Main results
Overall knowledge regarding NSIs and PEP was good. 94.6% recognized transmission of HIV, HBV and HCV through NSIs, 97.6% identified washing with soap and water as the appropriate immediate first-aid measure, and 97.0% recognized needle recapping as unsafe. However, 33.7% reported having experienced an NSI. Among those exposed, only 60.7% reported the injury and 57.1% received PEP. Although 98.8% reported appropriate sharps disposal, 46.4% reported always or sometimes recapping needles.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the need for improved adherence to safety protocols and better reporting mechanisms to reduce the incidence of NSIs and ensure timely postexposure prophylaxis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted interventions to improve occupational safety practices and enhance postexposure management protocols for healthcare workers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single tertiary-care teaching hospital and may not be generalizable to other settings. The evidence is moderate as it is based on self-reported data and does not include direct biological or clinical outcomes.

2020年至2024年博茨瓦纳献血者输血传播感染反应性:一项回顾性研究

Transfusion transmitted infection reactivity among blood donors in Botswana from 2020 to 2024: a retrospective study
作者： Gorata G. A. Mpebe, Chanana D. Tsayang, Wonderful Tatenda Choga, Bonolo Bonita Phinius, Chandapiwa Kefilwe, Lebogang Tembo, Lone Senanhele, Tsholofelo Sethibe, Florence G. Gaongalelwe, Tony Chebani
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
输血传播感染 (TTI) 仍然是血液安全的一个重要威胁,需要献血者持续进行 监测。
设计与对象
博茨瓦纳国家输血服务中心 (NBTS) 对所有捐献的人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和梅毒进行筛查。我们估计了 TTI 反应性并评估了博茨瓦纳献血者之间的时间和人口统计学关联。
核心方法
我们对 2020 年 4 月至 2025 年 3 月期间哈博罗内和弗朗西斯敦 NBTS 站点的捐赠记录进行了回顾性分析。主要分析使用每个捐赠年的第一次捐赠。估计了年度反应比例和 95% 威尔逊置信区间。使用具有捐赠者聚类稳健标准误差的人口平均逻辑回归来估计平均年度变化,并使用分类年模型正式评估线性偏离。使用 Stata 版本 18 进行分析。
主要结果
Among 102,102 source records from 61,446 donors, 34 same-day duplicate records and 1,138 later records from the 549 restored first-result-only donors were excluded. The final dataset contained 100,930 records from 61,439 donors and 84,303 donor-years. In continuous-year models, the average annual odds decreased for HCV (aOR 0.95, 95% CI 0.914–0.99), syphilis (aOR 0.92, 95% CI 0.88–0.95), and any TTI (aOR 0.97, 95% CI 0.95–0.99). Categorical-year analyses identified significant non-linearity for HBV, HCV, HIV and any TTI (all p ≤ 0.003), whereas the syphilis pattern was compatible with a linear decline (p = 0.61).
解释与创新
作者解释说,博茨瓦纳献血者的 TTI 反应性仍然很大,而且时间模式因病原体而异。
科研与公卫意义
持续筛查、确认性随访、捐赠者咨询和 监测 对于维持血液安全仍然至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的数据缺失而受到限制,但证据是强有力的,因为它基于全面的捐赠记录和稳健的统计分析。

Transfusion transmitted infection reactivity among blood donors in Botswana from 2020 to 2024: a retrospective study

Authors: Gorata G. A. Mpebe, Chanana D. Tsayang, Wonderful Tatenda Choga, Bonolo Bonita Phinius, Chandapiwa Kefilwe, Lebogang Tembo, Lone Senanhele, Tsholofelo Sethibe, Florence G. Gaongalelwe, Tony Chebani
Original abstract (English)
Background Transfusion-transmissible infections (TTIs) remain an important threat to blood safety and require continuous surveillance among blood donors. Botswana National Blood Transfusion Services (NBTS) screens all donations for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and syphilis. We estimated TTI reactivity and assessed temporal and demographic associations among blood donors in Botswana. Methods We conducted a retrospective analysis of donation records from the Gaborone and Francistown NBTS sites between April 2020 and March 2025. The primary analysis used the first donation per donor-year. Annual reactive proportions and 95% Wilson confidence intervals were estimated. Population-averaged logistic regression with donor-clustered robust standard errors was used to estimate average annual change, and categorical-year models formally assessed departure from linearity. Analyses were conducted using Stata version 18. Results Among 102,102 source records from 61,446 donors, 34 same-day duplicate records and 1,138 later records from the 549 restored first-result-only donors were excluded. The final dataset contained 100,930 records from 61,439 donors and 84,303 donor-years. In continuous-year models, the average annual odds decreased for HCV (aOR 0.95, 95% CI 0.914–0.99), syphilis (aOR 0.92, 95% CI 0.88–0.95), and any TTI (aOR 0.97, 95% CI 0.95–0.99). Categorical-year analyses identified significant non-linearity for HBV, HCV, HIV and any TTI (all p ≤ 0.003), whereas the syphilis pattern was compatible with a linear decline (p = 0.61). Conclusion TTI reactivity among Botswana blood donors remained substantial and temporal patterns differed by pathogen. Syphilis declined consistently, whereas HBV, HCV, HIV and overall TTI reactivity showed non-linear year-to-year variation. Continued screening, confirmatory follow-up, donor counseling and surveillance remain essential to sustain blood safety.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Transfusion-transmissible infections (TTIs) remain an important threat to blood safety and require continuous surveillance among blood donors.
Design and population
Botswana National Blood Transfusion Services (NBTS) screens all donations for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and syphilis. We estimated TTI reactivity and assessed temporal and demographic associations among blood donors in Botswana.
Core methods
We conducted a retrospective analysis of donation records from the Gaborone and Francistown NBTS sites between April 2020 and March 2025. The primary analysis used the first donation per donor-year. Annual reactive proportions and 95% Wilson confidence intervals were estimated. Population-averaged logistic regression with donor-clustered robust standard errors was used to estimate average annual change, and categorical-year models formally assessed departure from linearity. Analyses were conducted using Stata version 18.
Main results
Among 102,102 source records from 61,446 donors, 34 same-day duplicate records and 1,138 later records from the 549 restored first-result-only donors were excluded. The final dataset contained 100,930 records from 61,439 donors and 84,303 donor-years. In continuous-year models, the average annual odds decreased for HCV (aOR 0.95, 95% CI 0.914–0.99), syphilis (aOR 0.92, 95% CI 0.88–0.95), and any TTI (aOR 0.97, 95% CI 0.95–0.99). Categorical-year analyses identified significant non-linearity for HBV, HCV, HIV and any TTI (all p ≤ 0.003), whereas the syphilis pattern was compatible with a linear decline (p = 0.61).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that TTI reactivity among Botswana blood donors remained substantial and temporal patterns differed by pathogen.
Scientific and public-health significance
Continued screening, confirmatory follow-up, donor counseling and surveillance remain essential to sustain blood safety.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for missing data, but the evidence is strong as it is based on comprehensive donation records and robust statistical analyses.

慢性乙型肝炎 的 RNA 干扰和反义策略:机制、临床进展和通往 功能性治愈 的途径。

RNA interference and antisense strategies for chronic hepatitis B: mechanisms, clinical advances, and pathways toward functional cure.
作者： Ali M. Atoom, Jasur Rizaev, Amir Abdul Kadhim, K. V. Jamuna, Laxmidhar Maharana, Neeraj Bainsal, Divya Singhal, Djamila Polatova
摘要中文翻译目前的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 疗法、核苷(酸)类似物和干扰素 (IFN) 可抑制病毒复制,但无法消除共价闭合环状 DNA (共价闭合环状DNA) 或整合的 HBV DNA,从而使 CHB 成为全球健康威胁。高抗原水平会导致严重的免疫衰竭,因此药剂必须沉默病毒转录本,同时恢复 抗病毒药物 免疫力。这篇综述将 RNA 干扰 (RNAi) 平台总结为新兴疗法。临床前和 I-II 期数据显示,GalNAc 缀合的 siRNA(xalnesiran、imdusiran、elebsiran、daplusiran/tomligisiran、RBD-1016)可将 HBsAg 降低 1-2 log IU/mL,通常在 治疗 后持续数月。通过靶向 共价闭合环状DNA 和整合的 HBV DNA 的转录本,它们比当前的 抗病毒药物 具有机械优势。与衣壳组装调节剂、聚乙二醇化 IFN、治疗性 疫苗 或反义药物的组合可产生抗原的累加或协同下降,支持 RNAi 作为治疗性多药方案的支柱。尽管安全性和有效性有希望,但障碍仍然存在:HBsAg 平台期、免疫恢复不完全、晚期肝病的递送挑战以及缺乏用于患者选择的生物标志物。尽管如此,RNAi 疗法代表了一种范式转变策略,可以将 CHB 护理推向有限的、免疫驱动的 功能性治愈。使用 Google Scholar 对有关 CHB 的 RNAi 和反义策略(1985-2026)的文章进行了文献检索。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
目前的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 疗法、核苷(酸)类似物和干扰素 (IFN) 可抑制病毒复制,但无法消除共价闭合环状 DNA (共价闭合环状DNA) 或整合的 HBV DNA,从而使 CHB 成为全球健康威胁。
证据基础与方法
使用 Google Scholar 对有关 CHB 的 RNAi 和反义策略(1985-2026)的文章进行了文献检索。
共识与结论
这篇综述将 RNA 干扰 (RNAi) 平台总结为新兴疗法。通过靶向 共价闭合环状DNA 和整合的 HBV DNA 的转录本,它们比当前的 抗病毒药物 具有机械优势。与衣壳组装调节剂、聚乙二醇化 IFN、治疗性 疫苗 或反义药物的组合可产生抗原的累加或协同下降,支持 RNAi 作为治疗性多药方案的支柱。
争议与缺口
尽管安全性和有效性有希望,但障碍仍然存在:HBsAg 平台期、免疫恢复不完全、晚期肝病的递送挑战以及缺乏用于患者选择的生物标志物。
科研与实践启示
临床前和 I-II 期数据显示,GalNAc 缀合的 siRNA(xalnesiran、imdusiran、elebsiran、daplusiran/tomligisiran、RBD-1016)可将 HBsAg 降低 1-2 log IU/mL,通常在 治疗 后持续数月。

RNA interference and antisense strategies for chronic hepatitis B: mechanisms, clinical advances, and pathways toward functional cure.

Authors: Ali M. Atoom, Jasur Rizaev, Amir Abdul Kadhim, K. V. Jamuna, Laxmidhar Maharana, Neeraj Bainsal, Divya Singhal, Djamila Polatova
Original abstract (English)
Current Chronic Hepatitis B (CHB) therapies, nucleos(t)ide analogs and interferon (IFN), suppress viral replication but cannot eliminate covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA) or integrated HBV DNA, leaving CHB a global health threat. High antigen levels cause profound immune exhaustion, so agents must silence viral transcripts while restoring antiviral immunity. This review summarizes RNA interference (RNAi) platforms as emerging therapies. Preclinical and phase I-II data show that GalNAc-conjugated siRNAs (xalnesiran, imdusiran, elebsiran, daplusiran/tomligisiran, RBD-1016) reduce HBsAg by 1-2 log IU/mL, often for months after treatment. By targeting transcripts from both cccDNA and integrated HBV DNA, they offer a mechanistic advantage over current antivirals. Combinations with capsid assembly modulators, pegylated IFN, therapeutic vaccines, or antisense drugs yield additive or synergistic declines in antigen, supporting RNAi as a backbone for curative multidrug regimens. Despite promising safety and efficacy, obstacles remain: HBsAg plateaus, incomplete immune restoration, delivery challenges in advanced liver disease, and lack of biomarkers for patient selection. Nevertheless, RNAi therapies represent a paradigm-shifting strategy that could move CHB care toward a finite, immune-driven functional cure. A literature search was conducted using Google Scholar for articles on RNAi and antisense strategies for CHB (1985-2026).
View review five-element analysis

Current Chronic Hepatitis B (CHB) therapies, nucleos(t)ide analogs and interferon (IFN), suppress viral replication but cannot eliminate covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA) or integrated HBV DNA, leaving CHB a global health threat.
Evidence base and method
A literature search was conducted using Google Scholar for articles on RNAi and antisense strategies for CHB (1985-2026).
Consensus and conclusions
This review summarizes RNA interference (RNAi) platforms as emerging therapies. By targeting transcripts from both cccDNA and integrated HBV DNA, they offer a mechanistic advantage over current antivirals. Combinations with capsid assembly modulators, pegylated IFN, therapeutic vaccines, or antisense drugs yield additive or synergistic declines in antigen, supporting RNAi as a backbone for curative multidrug regimens.
Controversies and gaps
Despite promising safety and efficacy, obstacles remain: HBsAg plateaus, incomplete immune restoration, delivery challenges in advanced liver disease, and lack of biomarkers for patient selection.
Research and practice implications
Preclinical and phase I-II data show that GalNAc-conjugated siRNAs (xalnesiran, imdusiran, elebsiran, daplusiran/tomligisiran, RBD-1016) reduce HBsAg by 1-2 log IU/mL, often for months after treatment.

乙型和丁型肝炎:基于干扰素的治疗 - 用于临床试验或标准护理 - 回到未来?

Hepatitis B and D: Interferon-Based Therapy-For Clinical Trials or Standard of Care-Back to the Future?
作者： Zillah Cargill, Harry L A Janssen, Tarik Asselah, Kosh Agarwal
摘要中文翻译过去三十年来,干扰素 (IFN) 在慢性病毒性乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的治疗中发挥了重要作用。核苷/核苷酸类似物(NUC)在抑制乙型肝炎病毒方面的广泛应用(其副作用较小),加上最近获得的用于丁型肝炎 治疗 的 bulevirtide 许可,意味着其临床使用进一步下降。聚乙二醇化干扰素α (peg-IFNα) 治疗 效果欠佳,耐受性差,仅在有限数量的患者中实现了乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的持久清除。尽管如此,与单独使用 NUC 的患者相比,其在乙型肝炎中的使用显着增加了实现乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 消失的患者数量。有证据表明,peg-IFNα 还与慢性丁型肝炎的长期临床结果的整体改善相关,即使对于许多丁型肝炎病毒载量在停药后反弹的人来说也是如此。最近的临床试验重点关注 peg-IFNα 与新型 抗病毒药物 疗法的联合治疗,以实现乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的有限治愈。本文重点介绍最新的研究进展,探讨 peg-IFNα 治疗 是否仍可能在乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎 治疗 中发挥重要作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
过去三十年来,干扰素 (IFN) 在慢性病毒性乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的治疗中发挥了重要作用。
证据基础与方法
核苷/核苷酸类似物(NUC)在抑制乙型肝炎病毒方面的广泛应用(其副作用较小),加上最近获得的用于丁型肝炎 治疗 的 bulevirtide 许可,意味着其临床使用进一步下降。
共识与结论
聚乙二醇化干扰素α (peg-IFNα) 治疗 效果欠佳,耐受性差,仅在有限数量的患者中实现了乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的持久清除。
争议与缺口
尽管如此,与单独使用 NUC 的患者相比,其在乙型肝炎中的使用显着增加了实现乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 消失的患者数量。
科研与实践启示
有证据表明,peg-IFNα 还与慢性丁型肝炎的长期临床结果的整体改善相关,即使对于许多丁型肝炎病毒载量在停药后反弹的人来说也是如此。

Hepatitis B and D: Interferon-Based Therapy-For Clinical Trials or Standard of Care-Back to the Future?

Authors: Zillah Cargill, Harry L A Janssen, Tarik Asselah, Kosh Agarwal
Original abstract (English)
Interferon (IFN) has historically played a major role in chronic viral hepatitis B and D management over the last three decades. Widening utilisation of nucleoside/nucleotide analogues (NUCs) for hepatitis B viral suppression, which have a better side-effect profile, alongside the recent licensing of bulevirtide for treatment of hepatitis D, means that its clinical use has further declined. Pegylated-interferon alpha (peg-IFNα) treatment is suboptimal with poor tolerability and achieves durable clearance of hepatitis B and D in only a limited number of patients. Nevertheless, its use in hepatitis B has significantly increased the number of patients achieving hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss compared to those on NUCs alone. Evidence has shown that peg-IFNα is also associated with overall improved long-term clinical outcomes in chronic hepatitis D, even for the many whose hepatitis D viral load rebounds after discontinuation. Clinical trials have recently focused on peg-IFNα in combination with novel antiviral therapies in the goal for finite cure in hepatitis B and D. This article focuses on recent research advances, examining if peg-IFNα treatment may still play an important role in hepatitis B and D treatment.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Interferon (IFN) has historically played a major role in chronic viral hepatitis B and D management over the last three decades.
Evidence base and method
Widening utilisation of nucleoside/nucleotide analogues (NUCs) for hepatitis B viral suppression, which have a better side-effect profile, alongside the recent licensing of bulevirtide for treatment of hepatitis D, means that its clinical use has further declined.
Consensus and conclusions
Pegylated-interferon alpha (peg-IFNα) treatment is suboptimal with poor tolerability and achieves durable clearance of hepatitis B and D in only a limited number of patients.
Controversies and gaps
Nevertheless, its use in hepatitis B has significantly increased the number of patients achieving hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss compared to those on NUCs alone.
Research and practice implications
Evidence has shown that peg-IFNα is also associated with overall improved long-term clinical outcomes in chronic hepatitis D, even for the many whose hepatitis D viral load rebounds after discontinuation.

美国的 慢性乙型肝炎:当前形势、未满足的需求和未来方向。

Chronic Hepatitis B in the United States: Current Landscape, Unmet Needs and Future Directions.
作者： Chari Cohen, James Philip Esteban, Shravani Bobde, Robert J Wong
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染是美国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,与及时 诊断、治疗、监测和疾病管理相关的大量需求未得到满足。这篇叙述性综述探讨了美国慢性乙型肝炎的情况,包括患者护理方面的差距、不断发展的治疗前景、血清学生物标志物在改善乙型肝炎患者 (PLWHB) 患者的治疗结果和经历方面的临床效用。在美国,乙型肝炎的诊断和治疗仍然不足,目前的努力不足以实现世界卫生组织相关的 2030 年消除目标。美国肝病研究协会和美国传染病学会的最新指南代表了北美的观点,标志着向扩大 治疗 资格标准和共同决策的转变。核苷(酸)类似物 (NA) 是美国目前的护理标准,但单独使用 NA 很少能实现 功能性治愈。针对 功能性治愈 的新型有限疗法,例如反义寡核苷酸、小干扰 RNA、免疫调节剂以及组合或序贯疗法,正在研究中。由于慢性乙型肝炎的不可预测性和复杂性,定量乙型肝炎表面抗原等生物标志物可为 治疗 决策提供预后价值,并有助于指导个体化慢性乙型肝炎管理。PLWHB 经历心理影响和健康相关问题,包括对 传播 和 乙型肝炎病毒 相关并发症的恐惧。改善 CHB 管理的机会包括增加筛查和与护理的联系、开发更多针对 功能性治愈 的 治疗、采用血清学生物标志物来告知 治疗 决策以及优先考虑 PLWHB 的生活经历和偏好。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染是美国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,与及时 诊断、治疗、监测和疾病管理相关的大量需求未得到满足。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述探讨了美国慢性乙型肝炎的情况,包括患者护理方面的差距、不断发展的治疗前景、血清学生物标志物在改善乙型肝炎患者 (PLWHB) 患者的治疗结果和经历方面的临床效用。
共识与结论
在美国,乙型肝炎的诊断和治疗仍然不足,目前的努力不足以实现世界卫生组织相关的 2030 年消除目标。
争议与缺口
改善 CHB 管理的机会包括增加筛查和与护理的联系、开发更多针对 功能性治愈 的 治疗、采用血清学生物标志物来告知 治疗 决策以及优先考虑 PLWHB 的生活经历和偏好。
科研与实践启示
由于慢性乙型肝炎的不可预测性和复杂性,定量乙型肝炎表面抗原等生物标志物可为 治疗 决策提供预后价值,并有助于指导个体化慢性乙型肝炎管理。

Chronic Hepatitis B in the United States: Current Landscape, Unmet Needs and Future Directions.

Authors: Chari Cohen, James Philip Esteban, Shravani Bobde, Robert J Wong
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection represents a major public health challenge in the US, with substantial unmet needs related to timely diagnosis, treatment, monitoring and disease management. This narrative review explores the CHB landscape in the US, including gaps in patient care, the evolving therapeutic landscape, the clinical utility of serological biomarkers to improve outcomes and the experiences of people living with hepatitis B (PLWHB). Hepatitis B remains underdiagnosed and undertreated in the US, and current efforts are insufficient to meet the associated World Health Organization 2030 elimination targets. Latest guidelines from the American Association for the Study of Liver Diseases and the Infectious Diseases Society of America, representing a North American perspective, signify a shift towards expanded treatment eligibility criteria and shared decision-making. Nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) are the current standard of care in the US, but functional cure is rarely achieved with NAs alone. Novel finite therapies targeting functional cure, such as antisense oligonucleotides, small interfering RNAs, immune modulators and combination or sequential regimens, are under investigation. Because of the unpredictable, complex nature of CHB, biomarkers such as quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen offer prognostic value for treatment decisions and could help guide individualised CHB management. PLWHB experience psychological impacts and health-related concerns, including fear of transmission and hepatitis B virus-related complications. Opportunities for improvement of CHB management include increased screening and linkage to care, development of more treatments targeting functional cure, adoption of serological biomarkers to inform treatment decisions and prioritising the lived experiences and preferences of PLWHB.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection represents a major public health challenge in the US, with substantial unmet needs related to timely diagnosis, treatment, monitoring and disease management.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review explores the CHB landscape in the US, including gaps in patient care, the evolving therapeutic landscape, the clinical utility of serological biomarkers to improve outcomes and the experiences of people living with hepatitis B (PLWHB).
Consensus and conclusions
Hepatitis B remains underdiagnosed and undertreated in the US, and current efforts are insufficient to meet the associated World Health Organization 2030 elimination targets.
Controversies and gaps
Opportunities for improvement of CHB management include increased screening and linkage to care, development of more treatments targeting functional cure, adoption of serological biomarkers to inform treatment decisions and prioritising the lived experiences and preferences of PLWHB.
Research and practice implications
Because of the unpredictable, complex nature of CHB, biomarkers such as quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen offer prognostic value for treatment decisions and could help guide individualised CHB management.

评估贝匹罗韦森:慢性乙型肝炎 的一流 治疗 选项。

Evaluating Bepirovirsen: a first-in-class treatment option for chronic hepatitis B.
作者： Mariantonietta Pisaturo, Pierantonio Grimaldi, Antonio Russo, Alessandro Cornelli, Nicola Coppola
摘要中文翻译简介:在过去的十年中,慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的治疗模式已逐渐从终生病毒抑制转向 功能性治愈 的宏伟目标,其定义是在 治疗 停药后持续 HBsAg 清除并持久控制病毒复制。Bepirovirsen 是一种针对所有 HBV 信使 RNA 的反义寡核苷酸,是 功能性治愈 最有前途的药物之一。3 期 B-Well 报告的 功能性治愈 率接近 20%,在基线 HBsAg 水平较低的患者中上升至 27%。涵盖领域:在这篇综述中,我们总结了贝匹罗韦森的生物学原理、临床证据和未来前景,强调新兴的有限持续时间疗法如何重新定义 治疗 终点,并使该领域超越病毒抑制,走向 功能性治愈 对慢性乙型肝炎患者的现实前景。专家意见:CHB 的治疗格局正在从终生病毒抑制转向 功能性治愈 作为现实且具有临床意义的终点。贝匹罗韦森可以减少对无限期 治疗 和监测的需求,降低医疗成本,改善患者的生活质量,并减轻与慢性 HBV 感染相关的社会和心理负担。

Evaluating Bepirovirsen: a first-in-class treatment option for chronic hepatitis B.

Authors: Mariantonietta Pisaturo, Pierantonio Grimaldi, Antonio Russo, Alessandro Cornelli, Nicola Coppola
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Over the last decade, the therapeutic paradigm of Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) has progressively shifted from lifelong viral suppression toward the ambitious goal of a functional cure, defined by sustained HBsAg clearance with durable control of viral replication after treatment discontinuation. Bepirovirsen, an antisense oligonucleotide directed against all HBV messenger RNAs, represents one of the most promising agents for functional cure. The phase 3 B-Well reported functional cure rates approaching 20%, rising to 27% in patients with lower baseline HBsAg levels. AREA COVERED: In this review, we summarize the biological rationale, clinical evidence, and future perspectives of bepirovirsen, highlighting how emerging finite duration therapies could redefine treatment endpoints and move the field beyond viral suppression toward a realistic prospect of functional cure for patients living with CHB. EXPERT OPINION: the therapeutic landscape of CHB is shifting from lifelong viral suppression toward functional cure as a realistic and clinically meaningful endpoint. Bepirovirsen may reduce the need for indefinite treatment and monitoring, lower healthcare costs, improve patients' quality of life, and decrease the social and psychological burden associated with chronic HBV infection.

治疗儿童乙型肝炎的药物治疗选择:更新。

Pharmacotherapy options for managing hepatitis B in children: an update.
作者： Chun-Hsun Liao, Jia-Horng Kao
摘要中文翻译简介:患有 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的儿童在发展为晚期肝病之前可能会面临数十年的感染。由于年龄相关的 治疗 资格、有限的长期证据以及 功能性治愈 的不确定性,儿科管理仍然具有挑战性。涵盖领域:这篇叙述性综述探讨了 治疗 的适应症、国际指南以及基于干扰素的治疗和核苷(酸)类似物 (NA) 在儿童和青少年中的疗效和安全性。国际指南被优先考虑,并通过 2026 年 9 月之前的 PubMed 和 Cochrane 图书馆检索进行补充,没有较低的出版日期限制。我们讨论 治疗 戒断、发育安全、免疫抑制、特殊人群和向成人护理的过渡。评估新兴疗法时特别注意将成人结果应用于儿童的局限性。专家意见:我们认为,早期的治疗值得进一步研究,但仅凭年龄较小不足以进行干预。选定的儿童 队列研究 中的良好反应并不能证明治疗所有高病毒血症儿童都有益。贝匹罗韦森通过减少 HBV 转录本提供了有限治疗的潜力,但成人疗效无法确定儿科获益。在接下来的五年中,青少年试验应评估 治疗 停药后 HBsAg 持续消失以及可接受的安全性。在此之前,即使无法治愈,有效的 NA 抑制仍然是一种具有临床价值的选择。

Pharmacotherapy options for managing hepatitis B in children: an update.

Authors: Chun-Hsun Liao, Jia-Horng Kao
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Children with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) may face decades of infection before developing advanced liver disease. Pediatric management remains challenging because of age-dependent treatment eligibility, limited long-term evidence, and uncertainty regarding functional cure. AREAS COVERED: This narrative review examines treatment indications, international guidelines, and the efficacy and safety of interferon-based therapy and nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) in children and adolescents. International guidelines were prioritized, supplemented by PubMed and Cochrane Library searches through September 2026 without a lower publication-date limit. We discuss treatment withdrawal, developmental safety, immunosuppression, special populations and transition to adult care. Emerging therapies are assessed with particular attention to the limits of applying adult results to children. EXPERT OPINION: In our view, earlier treatment deserves further studies, but younger age alone is insufficient grounds for intervention. Favorable responses in selected childhood cohorts do not establish benefits from treating all children with high viremia. Bepirovirsen offers potential for finite therapy by reducing HBV transcripts, yet adult efficacy cannot establish pediatric benefit. Over the next five years, adolescent trials should assess sustained HBsAg loss after treatment withdrawal and acceptable safety. Until then, effective NA suppression remains a clinically valuable option, even when cure is not achieved.

Controversies and gaps

宿主 限制网络控制 乙型肝炎病毒 共价闭合环状DNA 转录和 HBx 介导的反作用。

Host restriction networks governing hepatitis B virus cccDNA transcription and HBx-mediated counteraction.
作者： Yu-Chao Wang, Xing-Long Zhang, Kai Chen, Hui Li
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 共价闭合环状 DNA (共价闭合环状DNA) 是维持 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 中病毒持续存在的转录模板。共价闭合环状DNA 作为稳定的微型染色体持续存在于受感染的肝细胞核中,逃避了 宿主 免疫清除和当前的 抗病毒药物,仍然是 功能性治愈 的主要障碍。最近的进展阐明了 共价闭合环状DNA 转录的调控机制。宿主细胞不是被动的病毒复制工厂;它们协调 宿主 限制因子 (HRF),在多个水平上抑制 共价闭合环状DNA 转录,包括核区室化、表观遗传修饰、启动子水平控制、干扰素诱导的效应反应和早期 共价闭合环状DNA 染色质组装。与此同时,HBV 也有共同进化的对策,主要通过 HBx 来解除这些限制并维持病毒转录,HBx 劫持 宿主 泛素化和染色质调节系统。值得注意的是,随着关于 Smc5/6 介导的限制、HBx 反作用途径和 共价闭合环状DNA“核小体开关”的新证据的出现,之前被归类为限制因子的分子(例如 Spindlin1)的功能特性正在被重新评估。这些发现表明,控制 共价闭合环状DNA 转录的调控网络不是静态的因子集合,而是分层的、动态的、有时有争议的 宿主 病毒竞争。本综述重点关注 HRF 介导的 共价闭合环状DNA 转录的多层抑制以及相应的病毒对策。我们总结了围绕关键限制因素的机制、最新进展和未解决的争议,并讨论了它们在新兴的 宿主 靶向干预措施中的转化潜力,旨在为基于持续 共价闭合环状DNA 沉默或消除的 CHB 功能性治愈 策略提供理论基础。

提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。

这些发现表明,控制 共价闭合环状DNA 转录的调控网络不是静态的因子集合,而是分层的、动态的、有时有争议的 宿主 病毒竞争。

Host restriction networks governing hepatitis B virus cccDNA transcription and HBx-mediated counteraction.

Authors: Yu-Chao Wang, Xing-Long Zhang, Kai Chen, Hui Li
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA) is the transcriptional template sustaining viral persistence in chronic hepatitis B (CHB). Persisting in infected hepatocyte nuclei as a stable minichromosome, cccDNA evades host immune clearance and current antivirals, remaining a central obstacle to a functional cure. Recent progress has clarified the regulatory mechanisms governing cccDNA transcription. Host cells are not passive viral replication factories; they coordinate host restriction factors (HRFs) that suppress cccDNA transcription at multiple levels, including nuclear compartmentalization, epigenetic modification, promoter-level control, interferon-induced effector responses, and early cccDNA chromatin assembly. In parallel, HBV has co-evolved countermeasures that relieve these restrictions and maintain viral transcription, mainly through HBx, which hijacks host ubiquitination and chromatin-regulatory systems. Notably, as new evidence emerges on Smc5/6-mediated restriction, HBx counteraction pathways, and the cccDNA "nucleosome switch", the functional identity of molecules previously classified as restriction factors, such as Spindlin1, is being reassessed. These findings indicate that the regulatory network controlling cccDNA transcription is not a static collection of factors, but a hierarchical, dynamic, and sometimes controversial host-virus contest. This review focuses on multilayered HRF-mediated suppression of cccDNA transcription and corresponding viral countermeasures. We summarize mechanisms, recent advances, and unresolved controversies surrounding key restriction factors, and discuss their translational potential amid emerging host-targeted interventions, aiming to provide a theoretical basis for functional cure strategies for CHB based on sustained cccDNA silencing or elimination.

Scope and question
This review focuses on multilayered HRF-mediated suppression of cccDNA transcription and corresponding viral countermeasures.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
We summarize mechanisms, recent advances, and unresolved controversies surrounding key restriction factors, and discuss their translational potential amid emerging host-targeted interventions, aiming to provide a theoretical basis for functional cure strategies for CHB based on sustained cccDNA silencing or elimination.
Research and practice implications
Notably, as new evidence emerges on Smc5/6-mediated restriction, HBx counteraction pathways, and the cccDNA "nucleosome switch", the functional identity of molecules previously classified as restriction factors, such as Spindlin1, is being reassessed.

欧盟/欧洲经济区、瑞士和英国乙型肝炎患者连续护理的障碍和促进因素:范围界定审查

Barriers and facilitators along the continuum of care among people living with hepatitis B in EU/EEA, Switzerland and the UK: A scoping review
作者： Rigmor C. Berg, Miroslav Kojić, Asgeir Johannessen, Vegard Skogen, Miroslav Kojic
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 是一个重大的全球健康负担,欧盟/欧洲经济区国家约有 1400 万感染者。正如世界卫生组织新的乙型肝炎指南所强调的那样,诊断、参与、参与和保留护理的比例低得惊人,这突显了需要进一步证据证明乙型肝炎护理连续动态的必要性。我们进行了范围界定审查,以确定和描述针对欧盟/欧洲经济区国家、瑞士和英国乙型肝炎病毒感染者连续护理相关的可改变障碍和促进因素的研究。我们在方案中预先指定了我们的方法,根据方法指南进行了审查,检索了六个数据库,一式两份地选择了合格的研究,并对数据进行了描述性总结。我们纳入了 24 项研究,其中大多数在 2016 年之后发表,并且是观察性定量研究。这些研究包括来自 11 个国家的约 25,000 名参与者,主要是感染 HBV 的移民,其中 90% 患有慢性 HBV 感染。六项研究涵盖了医疗保健专业人员的观点。对于每项研究,我们确定了研究参与连续护理的哪个阶段,发现九项提供了两个或三个护理阶段的结果数据。虽然 14 项研究涉及与护理的联系(第 3 阶段)和 10 项研究涉及护理保留(第 5 阶段),但很少有研究调查测试(第 1 阶段)、转诊(第 2 阶段)或参与护理(第 4 阶段)。我们根据五个护理连续阶段对研究结果进行分组,发现在多个连续阶段中始终出现一些障碍:系统性医疗保健限制(包括资源限制、乙肝病毒的优先级降低)、提供者知识缺乏以及患者对乙肝病毒病情的有限了解和对乙肝病毒的负面看法。需要进行更多的研究,并且必须涵盖更广泛的高危人群和收入环境,以更好地符合整个欧洲乙肝病毒负担的流行病学分布。

Barriers and facilitators along the continuum of care among people living with hepatitis B in EU/EEA, Switzerland and the UK: A scoping review

Authors: Rigmor C. Berg, Miroslav Kojić, Asgeir Johannessen, Vegard Skogen, Miroslav Kojic
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) represents a significant global health burden, with approximately 14 million infected individuals in EU/EEA countries. The alarmingly low proportions diagnosed, linked to, engaged and retained in care underscore the necessity for further evidence on HBV care continuum dynamics, as emphasized in the new World Health Organisation guidelines for HBV. We conducted a scoping review to identify and describe research addressing modifiable barriers and facilitators related to the care continuum for people living with HBV infection in EU/EEA countries, Switzerland and the United Kingdom. We pre-specified our methods in a protocol, conducted the review in line with a methodological guide, searched six databases, selected eligible studies in duplicate, and summarized data descriptively. We included 24 studies, of which most were published after 2016 and were observational quantitative studies. The studies included about 25,000 participants from 11 countries, primarily immigrants living with HBV infection, of which 90% had chronic HBV infection. Six studies covered the perspectives of healthcare professionals. For each study, we determined which stage(s) of the continuum of care the study attended to, finding that nine provided outcome data on two or three stages of care. While 14 studies concerned linkage to care (stage 3) and ten studies retention in care (stage 5), few investigated testing (stage 1), referral (stage 2) or engagement in care (stage 4). We grouped the studies’ results according to the five care continuum stages and found that a handful of barriers consistently emerged across multiple continuum stages: systemic healthcare constraints (including resource constraints, deprioritization of HBV), provider knowledge deficiencies, and patients’ limited understanding of the condition and negative perceptions of HBV. More research is necessary and must encompass a broader spectrum of at-risk populations and income settings to better align with the epidemiological distribution of HBV burden across Europe.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) represents a significant global health burden, with approximately 14 million infected individuals in EU/EEA countries. The alarmingly low proportions diagnosed, linked to, engaged and retained in care underscore the necessity for further evidence on HBV care continuum dynamics, as emphasized in the new World Health Organisation guidelines for HBV.
Evidence base and method
We conducted a scoping review to identify and describe research addressing modifiable barriers and facilitators related to the care continuum for people living with HBV infection in EU/EEA countries, Switzerland and the United Kingdom. We pre-specified our methods in a protocol, conducted the review in line with a methodological guide, searched six databases, selected eligible studies in duplicate, and summarized data descriptively.
Consensus and conclusions
We grouped the studies’ results according to the five care continuum stages and found that a handful of barriers consistently emerged across multiple continuum stages: systemic healthcare constraints (including resource constraints, deprioritization of HBV), provider knowledge deficiencies, and patients’ limited understanding of the condition and negative perceptions of HBV.
Controversies and gaps
While 14 studies concerned linkage to care (stage 3) and ten studies retention in care (stage 5), few investigated testing (stage 1), referral (stage 2) or engagement in care (stage 4). More research is necessary and must encompass a broader spectrum of at-risk populations and income settings to better align with the epidemiological distribution of HBV burden across Europe.
Research and practice implications
We included 24 studies, of which most were published after 2016 and were observational quantitative studies. The studies included about 25,000 participants from 11 countries, primarily immigrants living with HBV infection, of which 90% had chronic HBV infection. Six studies covered the perspectives of healthcare professionals.

感觉运动轴突多发性神经病揭示产后 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染:病例报告 和文献回顾

Sensorimotor Axonal Polyneuropathy Revealing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in the Postpartum Period: A Case Report and Review of the Literature
作者： Mehdi Zouaoui, Ilyass Lachgar, Ibtissam Bamaarouf, Youssef Hnach, Mbarek Azouaoui, Nourdin Aqodad
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染的肝外表现已有详细描述;然而,周围神经系统受累是一种罕见且可能未被充分诊断的表现。我们报告了一例感觉运动轴突多发性神经病,该病导致产后期间先前未被识别的慢性 HBV 感染 诊断。一名32岁女性,2月2日妊娠,因先兆子痫在家中阴道分娩,产后出现双下肢部分功能障碍,伴有静动小脑综合征、双侧水平眼球震颤和累及四肢的周围神经源性综合征,无消化道症状或肝病临床体征。尽管肝功能检查正常,但检查显示乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性、总抗 HBc 抗体阳性、HBe 抗原阴性以及 HBV 病毒载量升高。抗中性粒细胞胞浆抗体(ANCA)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)血清学 均为阴性。未进行补体水平、冷球蛋白血症检测和神经/肌肉活检。脑脊液(CSF)分析正常,没有白蛋白细胞解离的证据。发现维生素 B1 缺乏,而维生素 B12 水平正常,冷凝集素呈阴性。脑CT血管造影和MRI均正常。神经传导研究与感觉运动轴突多发性神经病一致,主要影响下肢。开始使用替诺福韦进行 抗病毒药物 治疗,并计划进行临床和临床旁随访。这种表现属于乙型肝炎病毒感染的肝外神经系统表现范围。相关的维生素 B1 缺乏是一个重要的混杂因素,在诊断推理中必须考虑该因素以及产后期的免疫学特殊性。治疗 决定以当前国际建议为指导,该建议涉及在存在症状性肝外表现的情况下对 HBV 感染进行早期 抗病毒药物 治疗。对于出现不明原因周围神经病变的患者,特别是在提示性临床背景下,即使没有肝脏异常,也应该系统地筛查慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染。然而,在乙型肝炎病毒感染被认为是唯一的根本原因之前,全面的病因学检查对于排除相关的鉴别诊断至关重要。

Sensorimotor Axonal Polyneuropathy Revealing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in the Postpartum Period: A Case Report and Review of the Literature

Authors: Mehdi Zouaoui, Ilyass Lachgar, Ibtissam Bamaarouf, Youssef Hnach, Mbarek Azouaoui, Nourdin Aqodad
Original abstract (English)
Extrahepatic manifestations of chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection are well described; however, peripheral nervous system involvement is a rare and probably underdiagnosed manifestation. We report a case of sensorimotor axonal polyneuropathy that led to the diagnosis of a previously unrecognized chronic HBV infection during the postpartum period. A 32-year-old woman, gravida 2 para 2, who delivered vaginally at home in the context of preeclampsia, developed partial functional impairment of both lower limbs during the postpartum period, accompanied by a static-kinetic cerebellar syndrome, bilateral horizontal nystagmus, and a peripheral neurogenic syndrome involving all four limbs, without digestive symptoms or clinical signs of liver disease. Workup revealed a positive hepatitis B surface antigen, positive total anti-HBc antibodies, negative HBe antigen, and an elevated HBV viral load, despite normal liver function tests. Antineutrophil cytoplasmic antibodies (ANCA) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) serology were negative. Complement levels, cryoglobulinemia testing, and nerve/muscle biopsy were not performed. Cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) analysis was normal, with no evidence of albuminocytologic dissociation. Vitamin B1 deficiency was identified, whereas vitamin B12 levels were normal and cold agglutinins were negative. Brain CT angiography and MRI were normal. Nerve conduction studies were consistent with sensorimotor axonal polyneuropathy, predominantly affecting the lower limbs. Antiviral therapy with tenofovir was initiated, and clinical and paraclinical follow-up was planned. This presentation falls within the spectrum of extrahepatic neurological manifestations of HBV infection. The associated vitamin B1 deficiency represents an important confounding factor that must be considered in the diagnostic reasoning, together with the immunological particularities of the postpartum period. The treatment decision was guided by current international recommendations regarding early antiviral therapy for HBV infection in the presence of symptomatic extrahepatic manifestations. In patients presenting with unexplained peripheral neuropathy, particularly in a suggestive clinical context, screening for chronic HBV infection should be systematic, even in the absence of hepatic abnormalities. Nevertheless, a comprehensive etiological workup is essential to exclude relevant differential diagnoses before HBV infection is considered the sole underlying cause.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Extrahepatic manifestations of chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection are well described; however, peripheral nervous system involvement is a rare and probably underdiagnosed manifestation.
Evidence base and method
We report a case of sensorimotor axonal polyneuropathy that led to the diagnosis of a previously unrecognized chronic HBV infection during the postpartum period.
Consensus and conclusions
A 32-year-old woman, gravida 2 para 2, who delivered vaginally at home in the context of preeclampsia, developed partial functional impairment of both lower limbs during the postpartum period, accompanied by a static-kinetic cerebellar syndrome, bilateral horizontal nystagmus, and a peripheral neurogenic syndrome involving all four limbs, without digestive symptoms or clinical signs of liver disease.
Controversies and gaps
Workup revealed a positive hepatitis B surface antigen, positive total anti-HBc antibodies, negative HBe antigen, and an elevated HBV viral load, despite normal liver function tests.
Research and practice implications
In patients presenting with unexplained peripheral neuropathy, particularly in a suggestive clinical context, screening for chronic HBV infection should be systematic, even in the absence of hepatic abnormalities.

慢性乙型肝炎 中代谢功能障碍相关的脂肪肝和 抗病毒药物 反应:系统评价和荟萃分析

Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver and antiviral response in chronic hepatitis B: A systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Jia Liu, Peng Zhang, Ke-Zhen Hu, Zhi-Hui Zhao, Yong-Ning Xin, Yan-Zhen Bi
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述旨在评估代谢功能障碍相关脂肪肝病 (MAFLD) 对 抗病毒药物 治疗 反应和 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者长期临床结果的影响。
证据基础与方法
本系统评价和荟萃分析遵循系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南。对 PubMed、Cochrane 图书馆、EMBASE 和 Web of Science 检索了截至 2025 年 11 月 30 日发表的研究。进行荟萃分析以评估 MAFLD 和 抗病毒药物 治疗 反应或临床结果之间的关联。根据抗病毒药物方案和MAFLD诊断方法进行亚组分析,并进行留一敏感性分析以评估稳健性。
共识与结论
MAFLD 与较低的病毒学和生化反应率、第 48 周时乙型肝炎表面抗原血清清除的几率较高(特别是在接受核苷(酸)类似物治疗的患者中)以及 CHB 患者发生肝细胞癌(HCC)的几率较高相关。在任何时间点均未观察到 MAFLD 与乙型肝炎 e 抗原血清清除之间存在显着关联。
争议与缺口
亚组分析表明,抗病毒药物 方案和 MAFLD 诊断方法部分解释了异质性,特别是对于 乙型肝炎病毒 DNA 清除率和 HCC。MAFLD 和 抗病毒药物 治疗 反应之间的关联在 96 周时减弱。
科研与实践启示
这些发现支持将代谢风险评估纳入 CHB 管理,并强调需要对 MAFLD 和代谢功能障碍相关的脂肪性肝病进行标准化定义。

Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver and antiviral response in chronic hepatitis B: A systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Jia Liu, Peng Zhang, Ke-Zhen Hu, Zhi-Hui Zhao, Yong-Ning Xin, Yan-Zhen Bi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver disease (MAFLD) frequently coexists with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). However, its association with antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with CHB remains unclear. AIM To systematically evaluate the impact of MAFLD on antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with CHB. METHODS This systematic review and meta-analysis was conducted in accordance with the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses guidelines. PubMed, the Cochrane Library, EMBASE, and Web of Science were searched for studies published up to November 30, 2025. Meta-analyses were performed to assess associations between MAFLD and antiviral treatment responses or clinical outcomes. Subgroup analyses were conducted according to antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach, and leave-one-out sensitivity analyses were performed to evaluate robustness. RESULTS Thirty-two studies were included, of which 20 evaluated antiviral treatment efficacy and 13 assessed clinical outcomes in patients with CHB and concomitant MAFLD (one study reported both treatment responses and clinical outcomes). MAFLD was associated with a higher rate of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at 48 weeks [odds ratio (OR) = 1.41, 95%CI: 1.28-1.55], although this association was attenuated at 96 weeks (OR = 1.44, 95%CI: 0.96-2.16). No significant association was observed between MAFLD and hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance at any time-point. MAFLD was consistently associated with lower rates of alanine aminotransferase normalization and hepatitis B virus DNA clearance throughout the treatment duration. MAFLD was associated with a significantly increased risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) despite antiviral therapy (OR = 1.50, 95%CI: 1.01-2.24). Subgroup analyses according to the antiviral regimen revealed distinct patterns, partly explaining the heterogeneity in the overall analyses. Subgroup analyses suggested that the antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach partly explained the heterogeneity, particularly for hepatitis B virus DNA clearance and HCC. CONCLUSION MAFLD was associated with lower virological and biochemical response rates, higher odds of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at week 48, particularly among patients receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, and higher odds of HCC in patients with CHB, but not with hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance. These findings support metabolic risk assessment in CHB management and highlight the need for standardized MAFLD/metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease definitions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review aims to evaluate the impact of metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver disease (MAFLD) on antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB).
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis followed the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines. PubMed, the Cochrane Library, EMBASE, and Web of Science were searched for studies published up to November 30, 2025. Meta-analyses were performed to assess associations between MAFLD and antiviral treatment responses or clinical outcomes. Subgroup analyses were conducted according to antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach, and leave-one-out sensitivity analyses were performed to evaluate robustness.
Consensus and conclusions
MAFLD was associated with lower virological and biochemical response rates, higher odds of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at week 48, particularly among patients receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, and higher odds of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in patients with CHB. No significant association was observed between MAFLD and hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance at any time-point.
Controversies and gaps
Subgroup analyses suggested that the antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach partly explained the heterogeneity, particularly for hepatitis B virus DNA clearance and HCC. The association between MAFLD and antiviral treatment responses was attenuated at 96 weeks.
Research and practice implications
These findings support the integration of metabolic risk assessment in CHB management and highlight the need for standardized definitions of MAFLD and metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease.

献血者筛查中不一致的 血清学 和核酸检测结果:原因、解决途径和操作影响 - 叙述性回顾。

Discordant serology and nucleic acid testing results in blood donor screening: Causes, resolution pathways and operational implications-A narrative review.
作者： Mohamed Ali, R. Sepetiene, Maha A. Badawi, Ramunė Šepetienė
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:血清学和核酸联合检测(NAT)缩小了人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)的传染窗口期,但两者在一小部分捐赠方面仍存在分歧。这篇叙述性综述旨在确定 血清学-NAT 不一致的原因,描述用于调查它的实验室方法,强调异构实践并提出证据分级的解决途径。材料和方法:在美国、欧洲、英国、世界卫生组织 (WHO) 和国际输血协会 (ISBT) 监管和专业指导下对 2000 年 1 月至 2026 年 8 月期间发表的 PubMed/MEDLINE 文献进行了审查。不一致的原因被分类为时间标记不一致、生物学不一致、分析性假反应性、分析性假阴性或分析前错误,并按病毒进行映射,对于乙型肝炎,按反应性标记进行调查和供者管理步骤。结果:血清学 反应/NAT 非反应结果通常反映分析错误反应或已解决的感染。HBV 是主要的例外:其含义取决于乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg)、乙型肝炎核心抗原总抗体 (抗 HBc) 或免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 抗 HBc 是否具有反应性,低水平或间歇性病毒血症可能会逃避小型池 NAT。NAT 反应/血清学-非反应结果主要代表窗口期感染,其解释取决于 NAT 格式、歧视性测试、可重复性和验证性测试的获取。不同司法管辖区的捐赠者重新进入规则存在很大差异。结论:我们提供通用和病毒特异性算法,其中 HBV 是由反应性标记构建的,其中每个步骤都被标记为监管要求、专业指导、常见做法或作者建议。企业可以根据当地监管和确认能力调整这些途径,以减少漏诊和不必要的捐赠者损失。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染,探讨 血清学 与献血者筛查中核酸检测 (NAT) 不一致的原因。核心问题是确定这种不一致的原因,描述调查它的实验室方法,强调异构实践,并提出解决途径。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 2000 年 1 月至 2026 年 8 月期间发表的 PubMed/MEDLINE 文献的叙述性综合,其中纳入了美国、欧洲、英国、世界卫生组织 (WHO) 和国际输血协会 (ISBT) 的监管和专业指导。证据分为时间标记不一致、生物不一致、分析假反应、分析假阴性和分析前错误等类别。
共识与结论
血清学-反应/NAT-非反应结果通常表明分析错误反应或已解决的感染。HBV 是一个例外,其解释取决于反应性标志物(HBsAg、抗 HBc 或 IgM 抗 HBc)以及低水平或间歇性病毒血症逃避小型池 NAT 的可能性。NAT 反应性/血清学-非反应性结果主要是由于窗口期感染所致,其解释取决于 NAT 格式、歧视性测试、可重复性和验证性测试的获取。不同司法管辖区的捐赠者重新进入规则差异很大。
争议与缺口
不同司法管辖区的做法和解释存在差异。该综述强调,乙型肝炎相关的不一致是复杂的,并且取决于反应性标记物,关于低水平病毒血症频率的证据有限。此外,NAT 反应性/血清学 非反应性结果的解释受到 NAT 格式和验证性测试可用性等因素的影响,这些因素可能不会统一应用。
科研与实践启示
审查建议血液机构应根据当地法规和确证检测能力调整解决途径,以减少漏检和不必要的献血者损失。需要进一步研究以更好地了解 HBV 特异性不一致机制并标准化跨司法管辖区的做法。

Discordant serology and nucleic acid testing results in blood donor screening: Causes, resolution pathways and operational implications-A narrative review.

Authors: Mohamed Ali, R. Sepetiene, Maha A. Badawi, Ramunė Šepetienė
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Combined serological and nucleic acid testing (NAT) has narrowed the infectious window period for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV), yet the two still disagree on a small fraction of donations. This narrative review aimed to identify the causes of serology-NAT discordance, describe the laboratory approaches used to investigate it, highlight heterogeneous practice and propose evidence-graded resolution pathways. MATERIALS AND METHODS: PubMed/MEDLINE literature published between January 2000 and August 2026 was reviewed with US, European, UK, World Health Organization (WHO) and International Society of Blood Transfusion (ISBT) regulatory and professional guidance. Causes of discordance were classified as temporal marker discordance, biological discordance, analytical false reactivity, analytical false negativity or pre-analytical error and mapped by virus and, for HBV, by reactive marker to investigation and donor-management steps. RESULTS: Serology-reactive/NAT-nonreactive results usually reflect analytical false reactivity or resolved infection. HBV is the main exception: the meaning depends on whether hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), total antibody to hepatitis B core antigen (anti-HBc) or immunoglobulin M (IgM) anti-HBc is reactive, and low-level or intermittent viraemia may escape minipool NAT. NAT-reactive/serology-nonreactive results mostly represent window-period infection, and their interpretation depends on NAT format, discriminatory testing, repeatability and access to confirmatory testing. Donor re-entry rules differ substantially between jurisdictions. CONCLUSION: We provide general and virus-specific algorithms, with HBV structured by reactive marker, in which each step is labelled as a regulatory requirement, professional guidance, common practice or the authors' proposal. Establishments can adapt these pathways to local regulation and confirmatory capacity to reduce missed infections and unnecessary donor loss.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, examining the causes of discordance between serology and nucleic acid testing (NAT) in blood donor screening. The central question is to identify the reasons for such discordance, describe laboratory approaches to investigate it, highlight heterogeneous practices, and propose resolution pathways.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of PubMed/MEDLINE literature published between January 2000 and August 2026, incorporating US, European, UK, World Health Organization (WHO), and International Society of Blood Transfusion (ISBT) regulatory and professional guidance. The evidence is classified into categories such as temporal marker discordance, biological discordance, analytical false reactivity, analytical false negativity, and pre-analytical error.
Consensus and conclusions
Serology-reactive/NAT-nonreactive results typically indicate analytical false reactivity or resolved infection. HBV is an exception, where the interpretation depends on the reactive marker (HBsAg, anti-HBc, or IgM anti-HBc) and the possibility of low-level or intermittent viraemia escaping minipool NAT. NAT-reactive/serology-nonreactive results are mostly due to window-period infections, and their interpretation depends on NAT format, discriminatory testing, repeatability, and access to confirmatory testing. Donor re-entry rules vary significantly across jurisdictions.
Controversies and gaps
There is heterogeneity in practices and interpretations across different jurisdictions. The review highlights that HBV-related discordance is complex and depends on the reactive marker, with limited evidence on the frequency of low-level viraemia. Additionally, the interpretation of NAT-reactive/serology-nonreactive results is influenced by factors such as NAT format and confirmatory testing availability, which may not be uniformly applied.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that blood establishments should adapt resolution pathways to local regulations and confirmatory testing capacities to reduce missed infections and unnecessary donor loss. Further research is needed to better understand HBV-specific discordance mechanisms and to standardize practices across jurisdictions.

乙型肝炎病毒和乙型肝炎病毒/丁型肝炎病毒检测:意大利当前状况与展望

Testing for hepatitis C virus and hepatitis B virus/hepatitis D virus: Current scenarios and perspectives in Italy
作者： Francesca Colapietro, Maurizia Rossana Brunetto, Nicola Coppola, Stefano FAGIUOLI, Pietro Lampertico
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people.
证据基础与方法
本专家意见审查总结了意大利三级中心积极从事 HCV、HBV 和 HDV 医院检测的经验,并确定了改进检测和与护理联系的实际途径。
共识与结论
在意大利,丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 筛查受益于专门的政府资助,而 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 检测则得到科学协会建议的支持,但并未受益于专门的国家公共卫生计划。
争议与缺口
世界卫生组织的目标是通过 疫苗接种 和筛查计划,到 2030 年将新发肝炎感染率减少 90%,死亡人数减少 65%。
科研与实践启示
本文提出了通过以下方式提高意大利病毒性肝炎管理有效性的建议:(1)加强医院单位之间的协调,整合全科医生和医院之间的信息系统;(2)实施集中报告制度和结构化转诊网络,确保检测结果呈阳性的患者及时得到适当的治疗;(3) 提供所需资源,以增加公共成瘾服务 治疗(Servizi per le Dipendenze)和监狱的检测;(4) 促进对高危人群,包括弱势群体和老年人群的筛查;(5) 开展全国病毒性肝炎宣传活动。

Testing for hepatitis C virus and hepatitis B virus/hepatitis D virus: Current scenarios and perspectives in Italy

Authors: Francesca Colapietro, Maurizia Rossana Brunetto, Nicola Coppola, Stefano FAGIUOLI, Pietro Lampertico
Original abstract (English)
Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people. The World Health Organization aims to reduce new hepatitis infections by 90% and deaths by 65% by 2030 through vaccination and screening programs. In Italy, screening for hepatitis C virus (HCV) benefits from dedicated government funding, whereas testing for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) is supported by recommendations from scientific societies but does not benefit from dedicated national public health programmes. This expert-opinion review summarises the experience of tertiary-level Italian centres actively engaged in hospital-based testing for HCV, HBV, and HDV and identifies practical pathways to improve testing and linkage to care. This article presents proposals to improve the effectiveness of viral hepatitis management in Italy by: (1) Strengthening coordination between hospital units and integrating information systems between general practitioners and hospitals; (2) Implementing a centralised reporting system and a structured referral network to ensure that patients with positive test results receive appropriate therapies in a timely manner; (3) Providing the resources needed to increase testing in public addiction treatment services (Servizi per le Dipendenze) and prisons; (4) Promoting screening in high-risk populations, including vulnerable and older populations; and (5) Implementing a national awareness campaign on viral hepatitis. These measures could help Italy make concrete progress towards eliminating HCV infection and controlling HBV/HDV infection within the next decade.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people.
Evidence base and method
This expert-opinion review summarises the experience of tertiary-level Italian centres actively engaged in hospital-based testing for HCV, HBV, and HDV and identifies practical pathways to improve testing and linkage to care.
Consensus and conclusions
In Italy, screening for hepatitis C virus (HCV) benefits from dedicated government funding, whereas testing for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) is supported by recommendations from scientific societies but does not benefit from dedicated national public health programmes.
Controversies and gaps
The World Health Organization aims to reduce new hepatitis infections by 90% and deaths by 65% by 2030 through vaccination and screening programs.
Research and practice implications
This article presents proposals to improve the effectiveness of viral hepatitis management in Italy by: (1) Strengthening coordination between hospital units and integrating information systems between general practitioners and hospitals; (2) Implementing a centralised reporting system and a structured referral network to ensure that patients with positive test results receive appropriate therapies in a timely manner; (3) Providing the resources needed to increase testing in public addiction treatment services (Servizi per le Dipendenze) and prisons; (4) Promoting screening in high-risk populations, including vulnerable and older populations; and (5) Implementing a national awareness campaign on viral hepatitis.

乙型肝炎相关性肝硬化患者并发慢性门静脉血栓形成的感染性门静脉炎:病例报告 和文献综述

Infective pylephlebitis complicating chronic portal vein thrombosis in a patient with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis: a case report and review of the literature
作者： Yao Jia-han, Jun-Wei Wang, Li Yu, 陈恔, Ming-Ming Tan, Lu-Cou Chen, Jia-Han Yao, Jiao Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 感染性门静脉炎是门静脉系统的化脓性血栓性静脉炎。当识别延迟时,这种情况很少见,是致命的,并且几乎总是在没有预先存在门静脉血栓 (PVT) 的患者中发生。对于伴有慢性门静脉血栓的肝硬化,抗凝治疗受到静脉曲张和凝血病的限制,并且没有针对感染血栓的指导。病例介绍:一名 68 岁汉族男性,患有乙型肝炎相关失代偿性肝硬化,既往因脾切除治疗静脉曲张出血,慢性 PVT 维持低分子肝素 (LMWH) 5 年,出现发热、腹痛和背胀。增强计算机断层扫描 (CT) 显示门静脉主干和肠系膜上静脉闭塞、门静脉周围循环和右结肠壁水肿;血培养培养出非 O1/非 O139 霍乱弧菌。连续静脉注射普通肝素 (UFH) 与头孢哌酮/舒巴坦一起开始,目标活化部分凝血活酶时间 (APTT) 为 50-70 秒。第 3 天,患者拒绝使用输液泵,改用皮下 LMWH。第5天疼痛加剧,APTT降至31.3秒,D-二聚体从2.07毫克/升升至18.74毫克/升,CT显示血栓延伸至侧支血管。经过多学科审查后,恢复持续和睦家医疗。在接下来的两周内,发烧和腹痛消失,腹泻也得到改善。C反应蛋白从20.14毫克/升下降到0.79毫克/升,门静脉在第12天通畅,到第31天完全通畅。持续多年的腹水和持续5年的腹泻完全解决。结论 慢性PVT不能预防叠加感染性门静脉炎,血流感染可以在几天内将稳定的血栓转变为传播的血栓。高烧、腹部或背部疼痛加剧以及 D-二聚体突然升高应立即进行对比增强 CT 检查。一旦 诊断 建立,培养导向的抗菌药物与持续静脉内肝素化相结合,滴定至 APTT 并在出血风险上升时中断,尽管既往有静脉曲张出血,仍可实现血管再通。临床改善滞后于血栓消退,第一周的高 D-二聚体并不是停止使用肝素的理由。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是乙型肝炎相关肝硬化患者并发慢性门静脉血栓形成的感染性门静脉炎。核心问题是慢性门静脉血栓(PVT)是否可以预防叠加感染性门静脉炎,以及血流感染如何影响血栓稳定性。
证据基础与方法
该综述是 病例报告 和文献综述,没有明确提及系统综述方法或荟萃分析。证据来自单个病例研究和关于肝硬化感染性门静脉炎和慢性 PVT 的现有文献。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,慢性 PVT 不能预防叠加的感染性门静脉炎,并且血流感染可以在几天内将稳定的血栓转化为传播的血栓。它还强调临床改善可能落后于血栓消退,并且第一周的高 D-二聚体并不是停止使用肝素的理由。
争议与缺口
证据仅限于单个 病例报告 和现有文献,可能无法提供足够的数据来得出广泛的结论。没有提及汇总估计或共识过程,并且该综述没有解决文献中潜在的争议或相互矛盾的发现。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,一旦 诊断 建立,培养导向的抗菌药物与持续静脉内肝素化相结合,滴定至 APTT 并在出血风险上升时中断,尽管既往有静脉曲张出血,也可以实现血管再通。它还强调,在出现高烧、腹部或背部疼痛恶化以及 D-二聚体突然升高的情况下,需要立即进行增强 CT 检查。

Infective pylephlebitis complicating chronic portal vein thrombosis in a patient with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis: a case report and review of the literature

Authors: Yao Jia-han, Jun-Wei Wang, Li Yu, 陈恔, Ming-Ming Tan, Lu-Cou Chen, Jia-Han Yao, Jiao Chen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Infective pylephlebitis is suppurative thrombophlebitis of the portal venous system. It is rare, lethal when recognition is delayed, and almost always described in patients without pre-existing portal vein thrombosis (PVT). In cirrhosis with chronic PVT, anticoagulation is constrained by varices and coagulopathy, and no guidance covers the infected thrombus. Case presentation: A 68-year-old Han Chinese man with hepatitis B-related decompensated cirrhosis, previous variceal bleeding treated by splenectomy, and chronic PVT maintained on low-molecular-weight heparin (LMWH) for 5 years presented with fever, abdominal pain and back distension. Contrast-enhanced computed tomography (CT) showed occlusion of the main portal vein and superior mesenteric vein, periportal collaterals and right colonic mural oedema; blood culture grew non-O1/non-O139 Vibrio cholerae . Continuous intravenous unfractionated heparin (UFH) targeting an activated partial thromboplastin time (APTT) of 50–70 s was started together with cefoperazone/sulbactam. On day 3 the patient declined the infusion pump and was changed to subcutaneous LMWH. On day 5 the pain worsened, the APTT fell to 31.3 s, the D-dimer rose from 2.07 to 18.74 mg/L and CT showed thrombus extending into the collateral vessels. Continuous UFH was resumed after multidisciplinary review. Over the next two weeks the fever and abdominal pain resolved and the diarrhoea improved; C-reactive protein fell from 20.14 to 0.79 mg/L, and the portal vein was patent on day 12 and fully patent by day 31. Ascites that had persisted for years and diarrhoea present for 5 years resolved completely. Conclusions Chronic PVT does not protect against superimposed infective pylephlebitis, and bloodstream infection can convert a stable thrombus into a propagating one within days. High fever, worsening abdominal or back pain and an abrupt rise in D-dimer should prompt immediate contrast-enhanced CT. Once the diagnosis is established, culture-directed antimicrobials combined with continuous intravenous heparinization, titrated to APTT and interrupted when bleeding risk rises, can achieve recanalization despite previous variceal haemorrhage. Clinical improvement lags behind thrombus resolution, and a high D-dimer in the first week is not a reason to stop heparin.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on infective pylephlebitis complicating chronic portal vein thrombosis in patients with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis. The central question is whether chronic portal vein thrombosis (PVT) provides protection against superimposed infective pylephlebitis and how bloodstream infections may affect thrombus stability.
Evidence base and method
The review is a case report and literature review, with no explicit mention of systematic review methodology or meta-analysis. The evidence is derived from a single case study and existing literature on infective pylephlebitis and chronic PVT in cirrhosis.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that chronic PVT does not protect against superimposed infective pylephlebitis, and that bloodstream infection can convert a stable thrombus into a propagating one within days. It also highlights that clinical improvement may lag behind thrombus resolution, and that a high D-dimer in the first week is not a reason to stop heparin.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited to a single case report and existing literature, which may not provide sufficient data to draw broad conclusions. There is no mention of pooled estimates or consensus processes, and the review does not address potential controversies or conflicting findings in the literature.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that once the diagnosis is established, culture-directed antimicrobials combined with continuous intravenous heparinization, titrated to APTT and interrupted when bleeding risk rises, can achieve recanalization despite previous variceal haemorrhage. It also emphasizes the need for immediate contrast-enhanced CT in cases of high fever, worsening abdominal or back pain, and an abrupt rise in D-dimer.
