And about 360,000 infants each year are born to mothers who aren’t screen for the virus. Public Health Alerts, a collaboration between NEJM Evidence and CIDRAP, fills a gap in reliable data, offering expert-reviewed reports that translate frontline observations into actionable public health evidence. An NEJM Evidence editorial explains the initiative further. Read all Public Health Alerts reports here. 360,000 unscreened pregnant women a year Almost a year ago, the Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices voted to replace a universal recommendation for the hepatitis B vaccine at birth—which had been in place since 1991—with individual decision-making for infants of mothers who test negative for HBV without presenting data to support the decision. The decision came on the heels of a report by the Vaccine Integrity Project (VIP) demonstrating the safety, effectiveness, and public health benefits of the birth dose. The VIP is led by the University of Minnesota’s Center for Infectious Disease Research and Policy, publisher of CIDRAP News. In the following month, January 2026, the Department of Health and Human Services announced a revised childhood immunization schedule that didn’t go through the usual channels for federal vaccine guidance. That schedule reduced the number of recommended routine vaccines from 17 to 11, though a federal judge in March blocked the changes. In today’s Public Health Alerts report, researchers with Truveta, a healthcare data analytics company based in Bellevue, Washington, and with the Callen-Lorde Community Health Center in New York City, assessed Truveta data from 2018 through June 2026. They analyzed data on 1.3 million infants born to 1.1 million women. Of the pregnancies, 90.5% had evidence of an HBV test, of which 0.18% were positive for HBV antibodies (HBsAg). The proportion of mothers with no test recorded during pregnancy was 15.6% in 2018 and 7.6% in 2026. Hepatitis B birth dose vaccination serves as a critical safety net against gaps in protection against perinatal HBV infection. Over the study period, 1,016,149 newborns (75.9%) received hepatitis B vaccination within a day of birth. Newborn vaccination rates were 76.4% in 2018 and 79.8% in 2022 (with a median of 80.0% from 2020 to 2022), then began to decline in mid-2023, falling to 65.6% in June 2026. Newborn vaccination rates were 96.9% over the study period in babies whose mothers tested positive for HBV infection, compared with 75.9% among mothers who tested negative, 76.8% among mothers who had an unknown status, and 75.4% among those with no evidence of a test. Hepatitis B vaccination rates were 97% to 98% for infants born to mothers who tested positive for HBsAg during pregnancy from 2018 through 2025, but that rate dropped to 93.9% from January through June 2026. The authors write, “Applying the 90% screening rate found in this study to the approximately 3.6 million births per year, mothers without evidence of HBV screening during pregnancy may give birth to an estimated 360,000 infants per year. In addition, applying the 0.18% HBsAg positivity rate in this study could translate to more than 640 infants a year born to mothers with HBV infection who are untested.” They conclude, “Hepatitis B birth dose vaccination serves as a critical safety net against gaps in protection against perinatal HBV infection.” Related news Previous Next The return of endemic measles is a likely harbinger of future crises Sarah Boden CIDRAP Op-Ed: Measles didn't come back overnight; here are 5 things we need now Jess Steier, DrPh Pennsylvania logs another measles death as survey finds few Americans fully understand the disease’s risks Liz Szabo New Public Health Alerts report spotlights how pregnant women with measles and their babies fare Jim Wappes Measles elimination at risk—and what a Florida study truly shows about Pfizer’s COVID vaccine: The State of US Vaccine Policy Izzy Brandstetter Figueroa, PhD, MPH, Riley Mulholland, MPH, Jess Steier, DrPH Almost 1 in 4 Americans believe misinformation about cause of autism Meghan Holohan A cancer vaccine milestone, a vaccine injury center, and what a flu shot study actually found: The State of US Vaccine Policy Izzy Brandstetter Figueroa, PhD, MPH, Riley Mulholland, MPH, Jess Steier, DrPH Switching rotavirus vaccination to shared clinical decision may increase serious illness, costs Meghan Holohan This week's top reads Two studies in US veterans find updated COVID vaccines protect against severe illness One study shows that vaccination cut COVID hospitalizations 44%, while another concludes that last season’s Moderna shot outperformed Pfizer’s in older adults. STAT3 基因变异与伊拉克乙型肝炎患者的肝癌有关 - Bioengineer.org

STAT3 Gene Variant Linked to Liver Cancer in Iraqi Hepatitis B Patients - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报伊拉克的一项医院病例对照研究调查了 STAT3 基因中的特定遗传变异与 慢性乙型肝炎 感染患者患肝细胞癌风险之间的联系。该研究于 2026 年 11 月 23 日发表,发现 rs1053004 变异与癌症风险增加之间存在很强的统计关联。该研究招募了 240 名参与者,分为三组:80 名患有肝细胞癌和 慢性乙型肝炎 的患者,80 名患有 慢性乙型肝炎 且无恶性肿瘤的患者,以及 80 名健康对照者。主要发现包括癌症组的平均年龄、男女比例和肝硬化 患病率 较高。与其他 队列研究 相比,癌症组的中位血清甲胎蛋白水平显着升高,并且恶性病例中的病毒载量更高。该研究使用 TaqMan 等位基因辨别和 ARMS-PCR 进行基因分型,确保分析准确性。然而,研究人员指出,由于癌症组和肝炎组之间肝硬化 患病率 的差异,残留的混杂因素可能会持续存在。这项研究强调了 宿主 遗传因素在乙型肝炎相关肝癌进展中的潜在作用。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2024年12月至2025年6月期间进行,新闻报道于2026年11月23日发布(N57)。
地点与对象
该调查在伊拉克卡尔巴拉的 Al-Hussaini 教学医院进行,涉及 240 名参与者,分为三组 队列研究:80 名肝细胞癌和 慢性乙型肝炎,80 名无恶性肿瘤的 慢性乙型肝炎,以及 80 名健康对照(N6、N7、N8、N9、N10)。
事件
伊拉克的一项医院病例对照调查发现,STAT3 基因 (rs1053004) 的特定遗传变异与 慢性乙型肝炎 感染 (N1) 患者患肝细胞癌的风险增加之间存在很强的统计关联。
规模、影响与风险
该研究发现,队列研究 人群在人口统计学和临床​​特征方面存在显着差异,包括癌症组中较高的平均年龄、男女比例和肝硬化 患病率 人群。与慢性携带者(6.2 ng/mL)和对照组(3.4 ng/mL)相比,癌症组的中位血清甲胎蛋白水平显着升高(328.5 ng/mL)。恶性病例的病毒载量(4.7×105 IU/mL)也高于慢性携带者(2.3×104 IU/mL)(N16、N17、N19、N20、N21)。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究采用了严格的排除标准以消除混杂因素,并使用标准化方法进行了基因分型。然而,研究人员承认,由于癌症组和肝炎组在肝硬化患病率方面存在显著差异,可能存在残留的混杂因素(N11,N23,N24,N51)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

STAT3 Gene Variant Linked to Liver Cancer in Iraqi Hepatitis B Patients - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A hospital-based case-control study in Iraq investigated the link between a specific genetic variant in the STAT3 gene and the risk of hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with chronic hepatitis B infection. The research, published on November 23, 2026, found a strong statistical association between the rs1053004 variant and increased cancer risk. The study enrolled 240 participants, divided into three groups: 80 with hepatocellular carcinoma and chronic hepatitis B, 80 with chronic hepatitis B without malignancy, and 80 healthy controls. Key findings included higher mean age, male-to-female ratio, and prevalence of liver cirrhosis in the cancer group. Median serum alpha-fetoprotein levels were significantly elevated in the cancer group compared to the other cohorts, and viral load was higher in malignant cases. The study used TaqMan allelic discrimination and ARMS-PCR for genotyping, ensuring analytical accuracy. However, the researchers noted that residual confounding might persist due to the disparity in cirrhosis prevalence between the cancer and hepatitis groups. This research highlights the potential role of host genetic factors in the progression of hepatitis B-related liver cancer.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Friday, November 27, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health STAT3 Gene Variant Linked to Liver Cancer in Iraqi Hepatitis B Patients by November 23, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 6 mins read 0 STAT3 Gene Variant Linked to Liver Cancer in Iraqi Hepatitis B Patients Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A hospital-based case-control investigation conducted in Iraq has demonstrated a strong statistical association between a specific genetic variation in the STAT3 gene and an increased risk of developing hepatocellular carcinoma among patients infected with the hepatitis B virus. Hepatocellular carcinoma, recognized globally as the sixth most common cancer and the third leading cause of cancer-related mortality, frequently arises in the context of persistent viral hepatitis infections. While global vaccination efforts have targeted the spread of hepatitis B virus, chronic infection remains a notable public health concern in Iraq. Because progression from chronic hepatitis B to primary liver malignancy involves a complex interplay between viral factors, environmental influences, and host genetic susceptibility, researchers sought to clarify the specific contribution of host genomic architecture within this population. The investigation specifically targeted the rs1053004 single-nucleotide polymorphism located within the 3-prime untranslated region of the STAT3 gene, evaluating its potential utility as a predictive molecular marker for liver cancer among Iraqi patients suffering from chronic hepatitis B infection. The study was designed and conducted at Al-Hussaini Teaching Hospital situated in Karbala, Iraq, enrolling participants between December 2024 and June 2025 following formal ethical clearance from the Research Management Unit of the Karbala Health Directorate. To evaluate disease susceptibility systematically, the clinical investigation enrolled 240 individuals categorized into three equal, mutually exclusive cohorts of 80 participants each. The primary clinical group consisted of 80 patients diagnosed with hepatocellular carcinoma arising on a background of chronic hepatitis B infection. The second cohort comprised 80 patients diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B without clinical evidence of malignancy, defined by persistent hepatitis B surface antigen positivity exceeding six months alongside elevated alanine aminotransferase concentrations. The third cohort served as a normal control group consisting of 80 healthy individuals exhibiting normal liver function profiles and confirmed negative serology for both hepatitis B and hepatitis C viruses. Stringent exclusion criteria were applied across all cohorts to exclude confounding liver etiologies, eliminating individuals presenting with autoimmune liver disease, alcoholic liver disease, nonalcoholic steatohepatitis, hemochromatosis, Wilson’s disease, human immunodeficiency virus or hepatitis C virus coinfections, or any prior history of other malignancies. Diagnostic confirmation of hepatocellular carcinoma relied primarily on dynamic radiological imaging adhering strictly to the American Association for the Study of Liver Diseases criteria. All suspected patients underwent multiphasic contrast-enhanced computed tomography, dynamic magnetic resonance imaging, or both modalities, with diagnosis established by the presence of pathognomonic vascular hallmarks consisting of arterial phase hyperenhancement followed by washout during subsequent portal venous or delayed phases. Through this rigorous radiological assessment, 71 patients had hepatocellular carcinoma confirmed exclusively by noninvasive vascular imaging findings, while nine individuals presenting with atypical imaging characteristics required formal histopathological verification. Serum alpha-fetoprotein concentrations were collected as supportive clinical data rather than an isolated diagnostic standard, ensuring regenerative and dysplastic nodules were accurately distinguished from true neoplastic lesions. Discover more Cancer Find Cancer Care cancer Study Biology Order DNA Kits Baseline demographic and clinical profiling revealed notable divergences among the three study cohorts, underscoring typical patterns of chronic liver disease progression. The mean age was significantly higher in the liver cancer cohort at 59.7 years compared to 48.2 years in the chronic hepatitis B group and 45.6 years in the healthy control group. Male participants constituted a majority across all categories, but the male-to-female ratio peaked markedly within the hepatocellular carcinoma cohort at 2.8 to 1, contrasting with 1.9 to 1 in the chronic hepatitis group and 1.5 to 1 in the healthy controls. Liver cirrhosis was substantially more prevalent among cancer patients, identified in 78.8 percent of the malignant cohort compared to 32.5 percent of the chronic hepatitis group. Furthermore, median serum alpha-fetoprotein reached 328.5 nanograms per milliliter in the cancer group, compared with 6.2 nanograms per milliliter in chronic carriers and 3.4 nanograms per milliliter in controls. Circulating median viral load was also distinctly elevated in malignant cases at 4.7 times 10 to the fifth international units per milliliter compared to 2.3 times 10 to the fourth in chronic carriers, alongside significant alterations in bilirubin, albumin, aspartate aminotransferase, and alanine aminotransferase levels. To obtain reliable molecular profiles, genomic DNA was extracted from peripheral blood leukocytes using commercial silica-membrane mini spin columns, with nucleic acid purity verified spectrophotometrically by maintaining absorbance ratios between 1.8 and 2.0. Genotyping of the STAT3 rs1053004 locus was performed using a standardized TaqMan allelic discrimination assay utilizing real-time polymerase chain reaction thermal cycling parameters consisting of initial denaturation at 95 degrees Celsius followed by 40 cycles of denaturation and combined annealing-extension at 60 degrees Celsius. To ensure analytical accuracy and validate assay reliability, approximately ten percent of randomly selected samples were independently regenotyped using tetra-primer allele-specific amplification, also known as ARMS-PCR, with sequence-specific oligonucleotides targeting the respective T and C alleles alongside an internal positive control amplicon. Cross-platform validation demonstrated one hundred percent concordance between the fluorescent probe assay and gel-resolved allele-specific amplification, while genotype distributions in the healthy control population conformed to Hardy-Weinberg equilibrium expectations. Analysis of genotype frequencies demonstrated significant differences across the clinical cohorts. Within the hepatocellular carcinoma cohort, the homozygous TT genotype accounted for 48.8 percent, the heterozygous CT genotype represented 38.8 percent, and the homozygous ancestral CC genotype constituted 12.4 percent. In contrast, the chronic hepatitis B cohort exhibited 35.0 percent TT, 45.0 percent CT, and 20.0 percent CC, while the healthy control cohort displayed 25.0 percent TT, 47.5 percent CT, and 27.5 percent CC. Allelic distribution mirrored this progressive variation, as the frequency of the T allele was calculated at 68.1 percent in liver cancer cases, 57.5 percent in chronic hepatitis patients, and 48.8 percent in healthy controls, yielding a crude allelic odds ratio of 2.24 when comparing cancer patients directly against healthy controls. Multivariate logistic regression modeling adjusted for key potential confounding variables, including participant age, biological sex, smoking history, and the presence of underlying liver cirrhosis. Under a codominant inheritance framework comparing cancer patients with healthy controls, individuals harboring the homozygous TT genotype exhibited a statistically significant 4.26-fold increase in the adjusted odds of developing hepatocellular carcinoma relative to those carrying the homozygous CC reference genotype. Although carriers of the heterozygous CT genotype displayed a trend toward higher disease odds relative to CC, this did not reach independent statistical significance. Under a dominant genetic model combining TT and CT genotypes against CC carriers, the presence of the T allele yielded an adjusted odds ratio of 2.72. When comparing hepatocellular carcinoma patients directly to chronic hepatitis B carriers, the homozygous TT genotype remained a statistically significant risk factor under the codominant model, yielding an adjusted odds ratio of 2.18, as well as under the recessive model with an adjusted odds ratio of 1.85. Stratified clinical analyses further clarified how specific viral and host physiological environments modulate the magnitude of this genetic risk. When evaluating TT carriers versus combined CT and CC carriers across clinical subgroups, the association with liver cancer was notably magnified among individuals testing negative for the hepatitis B e antigen, yielding an adjusted odds ratio of 2.86 compared to 1.52 in antigen-positive individuals. A pronounced divergence was also documented based on viral replication intensity: participants presenting with high circulating viral loads of at least 100,000 international units per milliliter exhibited an adjusted odds ratio of 3.14, whereas those with lower viral titers showed an odds ratio of 1.68 that lacked statistical significance. Similarly, among patients with documented liver cirrhosis, the presence of the TT genotype conferred an adjusted odds ratio of 2.97 compared to 1.43 in non-cirrhotic subjects, pointing to meaningful biological interactions between host genotype, prolonged viral replication, and extensive chronic hepatic parenchymal remodeling. Within the hepatocellular carcinoma patient cohort, the STAT3 rs1053004 genotype exhibited distinct correlations with markers of tumor aggressiveness and clinical staging. Patients carrying the TT genotype presented with significantly higher median serum alpha-fetoprotein levels of 512.6 nanograms per milliliter, compared to 285.3 in CT carriers and 147.8 in CC homozygotes. Median circulating viral loads were likewise significantly higher in TT carriers at 8.3 times 10 to the fifth international units per milliliter relative to 3.6 times 10 to the fifth in CT patients and 1.9 times 10 to the fifth in CC patients. Furthermore, 56.4 percent of TT homozygotes were diagnosed with advanced Barcelona Clinic Liver Cancer stages C or D, compared to 38.7 percent of CT carriers and 30.0 percent of CC carriers. In contrast, no statistically significant associations emerged between rs1053004 genotypes and gross anatomical tumor diameter, single versus multifocal tumor count, microscopic vascular invasion, or Child-Pugh liver functional grading. The biological rationale connecting this specific genetic polymorphism to oncogenesis centers on the regulatory function of the STAT3 signaling pathway in hepatocarcinogenesis. STAT3 serves as a pivotal downstream transcription factor in the JAK/STAT signaling cascade, governing critical cellular programs including tumor cell proliferation, survival, angiogenesis, and resistance to immune-mediated destruction. Because the rs1053004 single-nucleotide variant is situated in the 3-prime untranslated region of the STAT3 gene, functional annotations from genomic databases indicate its presence in a functional regulatory zone supported by chromatin accessibility, transcription factor binding assays, and expression quantitative trait loci. Genetic alterations in this untranslated domain can disrupt microRNA binding sites and posttranscriptional regulatory mechanisms, potentially leading to dysregulated STAT3 expression and enhanced oncogenic signaling during persistent viral infection. Despite these robust statistical findings, the investigators highlighted several essential methodological limitations that require cautious interpretation. Because the study was conducted within a single medical center with a relatively modest sample size of 240 participants, generalizability remains circumscribed and certain subgroup comparisons lost statistical significance following corrections for multiple hypothesis testing. Additionally, the analysis was restricted to a single locus rather than comprehensive STAT3 haplotype blocks, and direct functional experiments evaluating gene transcription within patient hepatocytes were not performed. The researchers noted that while multivariate models adjusted for underlying cirrhosis, the profound disparity in cirrhosis prevalence between cancer and hepatitis cohorts suggests residual confounding could persist. Consequently, larger multicenter investigations across diverse cohorts and functional cellular assays will be required to validate these findings and determine whether integrating STAT3 genotyping into clinical stratification algorithms can improve early detection of hepatocellular carcinoma in high-risk patients with chronic hepatitis B. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

质谱流式分析揭示 慢性乙型肝炎 感染女性胎盘中细胞类型特异性免疫重塑 - EurekAlert!

Mass cytometry reveals cell type-specific immune remodeling in placentas of women with chronic hepatitis B infection - EurekAlert!
新闻简报最近的一项研究于 2026 年 8 月 26 日发表在《临床与转化肝病学杂志》(JCTH) 上,并在 2026 年 11 月 23 日的新闻稿中报道,该研究使用质谱流式细胞仪分析 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染女性的胎盘免疫细胞亚群。这项研究在中国进行,涉及 20 名孕妇的胎盘组织,其中包括 6 名健康对照者和 14 名乙肝病毒感染者。研究发现,乙型肝炎病毒主要引起胎盘免疫细胞的功能重塑,而不是其组成的变化。主要发现包括 NK 细胞和 T 细胞中激活和细胞毒性标记物的上调,表明抗体依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC) 功能增强,而嗜碱性粒细胞则显示迁移相关标记物的广泛下调。基于有限的样本量,结果表明需要在更大的 队列研究 中进行进一步验证,以确定其与 HBV 母婴 传播 (MTCT) 的相关性。该研究首次应用高维单细胞技术来表征与 HBV 感染相关的胎盘组织中的免疫细胞景观。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2026年8月26日发表,新闻稿于2026年11月23日发布。该研究结果基于从 20 名孕妇收集的数据。
地点与对象
该研究涉及 20 名孕妇的胎盘组织,其中包括 6 名健康对照者和 14 名慢性 HBV 感染者。该研究是在中国进行的,因为该期刊与重庆医科大学第二附属医院有关联。
事件
发表在《临床与转化肝病学杂志》(JCTH) 上的一项同行评审研究使用质谱流式细胞仪分析慢性 HBV 感染女性的胎盘免疫细胞亚群。该研究发现免疫细胞的功能重塑而不是细胞组成的变化。这是一份科学研究报告,不是暴发或公共卫生事件。
规模、影响与风险
该研究的样本量有限(n = 20),重点关注胎盘组织中免疫细胞的变化。它没有报告任何病例、死亡或 传播 事件。研究结果表明对理解母婴 传播 机制的潜在影响,但并不表明存在公共健康风险或 暴发。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是经过同行评审的出版物,其结果需要在更大的 队列研究 中进行验证。这些发现具有探索性,为理解 HBV 传播 提出了新的视角。该研究不涉及积极的公共卫生应对措施。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mass cytometry reveals cell type-specific immune remodeling in placentas of women with chronic hepatitis B infection - EurekAlert!

News Brief
A recent study published in the Journal of Clinical and Translational Hepatology (JCTH) on 26-Aug-2026, and reported in a news release on 23-Nov-2026, used mass cytometry to analyze placental immune cell subsets in women with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. The research, conducted in China, involved placental tissues from 20 pregnant women, including 6 healthy controls and 14 with HBV. The study found that HBV primarily caused functional remodeling of placental immune cells rather than changes in their composition. Key findings include upregulation of activation and cytotoxicity markers in NK cells and T cells, suggesting enhanced antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC) function, while basophils showed extensive downregulation of migration-related markers. The results, based on a limited sample size, indicate the need for further validation in larger cohorts to determine their relevance to mother-to-child transmission (MTCT) of HBV. The study is the first to apply high-dimensional single-cell technology to characterize the immune-cell landscape in placental tissue related to HBV infection.
Original source text (English)
Advanced Search Home News Releases Multimedia Meetings Login Register News Release 23-Nov-2026 Mass cytometry reveals cell type-specific immune remodeling in placentas of women with chronic hepatitis B infection Peer-Reviewed Publication Xia & He Publishing Inc. Facebook X LinkedIn WeChat Bluesky Message WhatsApp Email image: These exploratory findings suggest that HBV may reshape placental immunity through enhanced NK cell activation, broad basophil suppression, and disrupted marker coordination. The sample size was limited (n = 20), so the results need to be validated in larger cohorts. These findings provide new perspectives for understanding the mechanisms of mother-to-child transmission. view more Credit: Junfeng Lu, Yunxia Zhu Background and Aims Hepatitis B virus (HBV) vertical transmission remains a major health challenge, and the characteristics of the placental immune microenvironment in chronic infection remain unclear. This study aimed to use mass cytometry to comprehensively analyze phenotypic changes in placental immune cell subsets. Methods We collected placental tissues from 20 pregnant women (6 healthy controls and 14 with chronic HBV infection). CD45+ leukocytes were detected using a 35-marker antibody panel. Unsupervised clustering identified 14 clusters, of which 13 immune clusters were analyzed. Results HBV mainly caused functional remodeling of placental immune cells rather than changes in cell composition (P > 0.05 for all subsets). In natural killer (NK) cells, CD38 (P = 0.015) and CD16 (P = 0.0020) were notably upregulated and strongly correlated (ρ = 0.872, P < 0.001), suggesting potentially enhanced antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC) function. T cells showed upregulation of CD27, CD38, and CXCR5. Basophils exhibited the most pronounced changes: 9 differential markers (including chemokine receptors CCR4 and CCR7) were consistently downregulated. Classical monocytes showed an M1 polarization tendency (CD38↑, CD163↓). All P-values were raw; no markers remained significant after false discovery rate (FDR) correction at an FDR threshold of < 0.1, indicating nominal significance. In addition, the coordinated expression patterns of markers within multiple cell subsets were weakened after infection. Conclusions In summary, this study reveals that HBV infection induces functional remodeling rather than compositional changes in placental immune cells. NK cells and T cells showed upregulation of activation and cytotoxicity markers, suggesting enhanced ADCC function; however, basophils showed extensive downregulation of migration-related markers and loss of expression coordination—an unprecedented finding. The weakening of coordination between multiple marker pairs suggests that HBV infection has disrupted the immune regulatory network. To our knowledge, this study is the first to apply high-dimensional single-cell technology to comprehensively characterize the immune-cell landscape in sampled placental tissue, identify the novel phenomenon of basophil functional downregulation, and introduce marker coordination analysis as a methodological approach. These findings provide new insights into the immunological mechanisms underlying HBV MTCT. Future studies should prioritize functional validation of basophil changes (IL-4/IL-13 secretion) and their association with transmission outcomes, as well as verification in larger-scale longitudinal cohorts. In particular, a prospective cohort with documented MTCT outcomes and paired neonatal blood samples is needed to determine whether the placental immune signatures identified here correlate with HBV-specific T-cell responses in neonates. The candidate markers identified in this study provide a basis for subsequent mechanistic research and clinical translation. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 急剧下降 - 新闻动态

Newborn Hepatitis B Vaccinations See Sharp Drop - Newser
新闻简报Newborn hepatitis B vaccination rates in the United States have sharply declined, according to a new analysis of 1.3 million births. In early 2026, only 65.6% of babies received the vaccine within 24 hours, down from about 80% between 2020 and 2022. This drop follows a federal advisory panel's decision in late 2025 to remove its long-standing recommendation for universal vaccination, instead advising parents of babies born to hepatitis B-negative mothers to consult their doctors. The change has led to confusion among parents, as noted by Dr. Demetre Daskalakis, an author of the study and former CDC leader. Despite the policy shift being in court, vaccination rates for high-risk infants (those born to infected mothers) also fell, from 98% to 93.9%. Since the vaccine became available in the 1980s, early childhood hepatitis B infections have dropped from over 25,000 annually to fewer than 100. However, a separate analysis warns that this progress may be reversed if vaccination rates continue to decline. The New England Journal of Medicine published the data, highlighting the potential public health risk.
查看新闻五要素
时间
在联邦咨询小组于 2025 年底放弃其长期建议后,新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的下降加速,该数据发表在《新英格兰医学杂志》上。
地点与对象
美国报道了这一问题,重点关注新生儿,尤其是乙型肝炎阴性母亲所生的新生儿。
事件
新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 率急剧下降,2026 年初,65.6% 的婴儿在 24 小时内接受 疫苗,低于 2020 年至 2022 年期间的约 80%。这种下降与联邦咨询小组决定改变其建议有关。
规模、影响与风险
A new analysis of 1.3 million births found a significant drop in vaccination rates, with high-risk babies (infants of infected mothers) seeing a decrease from 98% to 93.9%. The decline may threaten progress in reducing early childhood infections, which had fallen from over 25,000 annually to fewer than 100 since the vaccine became available in the 1980s.
应对、状态与不确定性
联邦政策的转变与法庭有关,仍然建议受感染母亲的婴儿注射疫苗。然而,疫苗接种 率的下降引起了人们对预防乙型肝炎感染进展可能出现逆转的担忧。

Kennedy Jr. last year By John Johnson withNewser.AI Posted Nov 24, 2026 12:25 PM CST Copied (Getty/Sementsova Lesia) Fewer US newborns are leaving the hospital with a hepatitis B shot in their system. A new analysis of 1.3 million births finds that 65.6% of babies received the vaccine within 24 hours in early 2026, down from about 80% between 2020 and 2022, reports the Wall Street Journal. The data is published in the New England Journal of Medicine. The decline accelerated after a federal advisory panel in late 2025 scrapped its long-standing recommendation that every newborn get the first dose, instead telling parents of babies born to hepatitis B-negative mothers to consult their doctors. The panel's members were hand-picked by Secretary of Health Robert F. Kennedy Jr., a longtime vaccine skeptic, notes the Washington Post. "The bottom line is confusion creates inaction among parents," says Dr. Demetre Daskalakis, an author of the study and a former leader at the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. "It's as clear as mud what to do because you have public messaging that sheds doubt on the effectiveness of the hepatitis B vaccine." Though the federal policy shift is tied up in court and the shot is still recommended for infants of infected mothers, vaccination rates fell for those high-risk babies, too, from as high as 98% to 93.9%. Since the vaccine became available in the 1980s, the number of early childhood infections has fallen from more than 25,000 annually to fewer than 100. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

葛兰素史克 (GSK) 的 Hibsago 获得中国批准治疗 慢性乙型肝炎

GSK's Hibsago Gets China Approval To Treat Chronic Hepatitis B
新闻简报GSK's Hibsago (bepirovirsen) has received approval in China for the treatment of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) as a first-in-class functional cure. The approval, granted by China's National Medical Products Administration (NMPA), is based on phase III data demonstrating unprecedented functional cure rates compared to standard of care. The drug is intended for non-cirrhotic adult patients with HBV DNA <90 IU/mL and HBsAg levels =3,000 IU/mL, who are currently on nucleos(t)ide analogue (NAs) therapy for at least 6 months. Hibsago, an antisense oligonucleotide (ASO), offers a well-tolerated safety profile and has the potential to reduce the long-term liver complications associated with CHB. The approval was granted under Breakthrough Therapy and Priority Review designations, recognizing the high unmet need in China. GSK is collaborating with SBP Group to accelerate the drug's launch and access in mainland China. The Action Plan for the Prevention and Control of Viral Hepatitis (2025-2030) in China aims to diagnose and treat at least 80 percent of people living with CHB by 2030.
查看新闻五要素
时间
商业 商业 最新头条新闻 头条新闻 突发新闻 收益 生物科技投资者 股票快讯 IPO 并购 加拿大 英国 华尔街活动 行业新闻 行业新闻 科技 软件 银行 汽车 Energy More Corp.
地点与对象
中国,患有 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 且 HBV DNA <90 IU/mL 且 HBsAg 水平 = 3,000 IU/mL 的非肝硬化成人患者。
事件
据 RTTNews 特约撰稿人报道,葛兰素史克 (GSK) 的 Hibsago(贝匹罗韦森)的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的 治疗 在中国获得批准,成为一流的 功能性治愈。
规模、影响与风险
此次批准基于 III 期数据,该数据显示与标准护理相比史无前例的 功能性治愈 率。该药物具有良好的耐受性安全性,并有可能减少符合条件的患者的长期肝脏并发症。
应对、状态与不确定性
该批准是根据突破性疗法和优先审查指定授予的。GSK 正与 SBP 集团合作,加速 Hibsago 在中国大陆的上市和销售。该批准基于临床试验结果,尚未进行调查。

Reiterated Economy US Europe Asia Global Economic Calendar Economic Scorecard Crypto Cryptocurrency Blockchain Markets Market News Morning Mkt Analysis US Commentary European Commentary Asian Commentary Canadian Commentary Indian Commentary Commodities Bonds Currencies Politics US World White House Elections General News Forex FX Top Stories Currency Analysis Currency Alerts Economic Calendar Economic Scorecard Health Health News Coronavirus COVID-19 Calendar Diet & Fitness Cannabis Kids Health Men's Health Women's Health Cancer News Drug Development Mental Health Slide Shows Entertainment Entertainment News Top Stories Music News Pop Rock Classic Rock Rap/Hip-Hop Country Alternative Oldies All Genre Content Licensing Newswires & Feeds Content Syndication Digital Signage Services Radio News Services Premium Intelligent Investor Biotech Investor Login More Latest News Videos Free Content RSS Feeds Press Releases Search Contact Us Corporate News GSK's Hibsago Gets China Approval To Treat Chronic Hepatitis B By RTTNews Staff Writer ✉ | Published: 11/26/2026 8:51 AM ET | Follow Us | Join Us Add as your preferred news source on Google Add Now British drug maker GSK plc (GSK,GSK.L) announced Thursday that Hibsago (bepirovirsen) has been approved in China as a first-in-class functional cure for chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The company noted that China's National Medical Products Administration (NMPA) has approved Hibsago (bepirovirsen) in combination with nucleos(t)ide analogue (NAs) therapy to achieve functional cure of chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in non-cirrhotic adult patients. These patients are currently on NAs for at least 6 months, and have HBV DNA <90 IU/mL and hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) levels =3,000 IU/mL. The approval is based on phase III data demonstrating unprecedented functional cure rates compared to standard of care. Bepirovirsen is a first-in-class antisense oligonucleotide (ASO) and offers unprecedented functional cure rates compared to current standard of care alone, and the safety profile is generally well tolerated. The approval was granted under Breakthrough Therapy and Priority Review designations, recognising the high unmet need in China and potential for substantial clinical improvement over existing therapies. GSK said it is collaborating with SBP Group, through its subsidiary Chia Tai Tianqing Pharmaceutical Group Co., Ltd., to accelerate the launch and access of Hibsago in mainland China. Tony Wood, Chief Scientific Officer, GSK, said: "Chronic hepatitis B affects an estimated 75 million people in China. Despite a strong focus on diagnosis and access to treatment, many patients face a lifetime of therapy and remain at risk of long-term liver complications with current standard of care. The accelerated approval of Hibsago means eligible patients in China now have the potential to achieve functional cure with a six-month treatment course." According to the company, CHB is a national health priority in China. The Action Plan for the Prevention and Control of Viral Hepatitis (2025-2030) in the country aims for at least 80 percent of people living with CHB to be diagnosed, and at least 80 percent of newly reported patients treated by 2030. In London, GSK shares were trading at 1,930.68 pence, up 0.58 percent. China, non-cirrhotic adult patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) who have HBV DNA <90 IU/mL and HBsAg levels =3,000 IU/mL.
Event
GSK's Hibsago (bepirovirsen) received approval in China for the treatment of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) as a first-in-class functional cure, reported by RTTNews Staff Writer.
Scale, impact and risk
The approval is based on phase III data showing unprecedented functional cure rates compared to standard of care. The drug offers a well-tolerated safety profile and has the potential to reduce long-term liver complications for eligible patients.
Response, status and uncertainty
The approval was granted under Breakthrough Therapy and Priority Review designations. GSK is collaborating with SBP Group to accelerate the launch and access of Hibsago in mainland China. The approval is based on clinical trial results and is not under investigation.

Precision BioSciences 的目标是在第二阶段治愈乙型肝炎,着眼于 2027 年杜氏数据 - 雅虎财经

Precision BioSciences Targets Hepatitis B Cure in Phase II, Eyes Duchenne Data in 2027 - Yahoo Finance
新闻简报Precision BioSciences is progressing its Hepatitis B treatment program into Phase II trials, aiming to evaluate the treatment alone and in combination with bepirovirsen, with initial data expected in the second half of 2027. The company reported that the first patient in the Phase I study experienced no serious adverse events, and the safety board has endorsed continued dosing. The Phase II trial involves 25 patients who received PBGENE-HBV in Phase I, with 59 administrations overall. Following a $125 million raise, the company has over $200 million in cash, extending its financial runway to the fourth quarter of 2029. The evidence does not specify any risk level or unresolved issues, indicating that the trial is currently on track with no major concerns raised.

Here are five stocks we like better. Precision BioSciences plans to advance PBGENE-HBV into Phase II to test whether it can eliminate enough hepatitis B cccDNA for a viral cure. The study will evaluate the treatment alone and followed by bepirovirsen, with initial Phase II data expected in the second half of 2027. Duchenne muscular dystrophy data are expected in 2027: three-month biopsy results from roughly the first three patients could arrive around the second quarter, with the first patient's 12-month biopsy data expected in the fall. The first patient had no serious adverse events reported, and the safety board supported continued dosing. After a recent $125 million raise, the company reported cash of more than $200 million and said its runway extends into the fourth quarter of 2029. Precision BioSciences (NASDAQ:DTIL) plans to advance its hepatitis B gene-editing treatment into a Phase II study and expects initial biopsy results from its Duchenne muscular dystrophy program in 2027, executives said at a Jefferies conference. Chief Scientific Officer Cassie Gorsuch said the hepatitis B study will test whether the company's treatment, PBGENE-HBV, can reduce enough of the viral material known as cccDNA to achieve a viral cure. Chief Operating Officer Alex Kelly said the company expects to begin seeing Phase II data in the second half of 2027. → Gold Is Down 35%—Is the Bull Case Broken or Just Bruised? Kelly also said Precision had $108 million in cash as of Sept. 30 and recently raised $125 million, taking its cash balance above $200 million. He said the company's cash runway extends into the fourth quarter of 2029. Hepatitis B program moves toward Phase II Gorsuch said 25 patients had received PBGENE-HBV in the repeat-dose Phase I study, with 59 administrations across the group. At a recent liver meeting, the company reported that six patients with at least six months of follow-up after their pregenomic RNA, or pgRNA, became undetectable continued to have undetectable levels. → Plug Power Near $1: A Hydrogen Turnaround or Another False Start? The company views pgRNA as a primary biomarker for the treatment's effect on cccDNA, the hepatitis B viral material it is designed to eliminate. Gorsuch also said the first e-antigen-positive patient treated in the study had a 3.7-log reduction in pgRNA to undetectable levels at the 0.4 mg/kg dose selected for the next phase. Despite the encouraging biomarker results, Gorsuch said the remaining question is whether the treatment has eliminated enough cccDNA to produce a viral cure. The Phase II study is intended to answer that question. Story Continues → Alphabet's Most Tempting Valuation Signal May Be Its Most Misleading The study will assess PBGENE-HBV alone and followed by the antisense treatment bepirovirsen. The monotherapy arm will test for viral cure, defined in the discussion as undetectable HBV DNA after treatment is stopped; the combination arm will assess functional cure. Gorsuch said the company sees a potential benefit from reducing viral burden before administering bepirovirsen. Patients in the combination arm will need to meet bepirovirsen eligibility criteria, Gorsuch said. Kelly said Precision had not yet discussed a partnership with GSK but was prepared to purchase bepirovirsen for the study. For patients stopping nucleoside therapy, Gorsuch said normal liver enzymes and fully suppressed HBV DNA are baseline safety considerations. She said regulatory guidance calls for six months off nucleoside treatment to demonstrate an approvable endpoint. Kelly said any withdrawal of therapy in Phase I would be handled case by case, with the larger Phase II study expected to provide more consistent data. Duchenne study awaits biopsy results Precision's Duchenne muscular dystrophy program uses its ARCUS gene-editing technology to remove a region of the dystrophin gene and aims to restore a near full-length dystrophin protein. Gorsuch said the targeted region is mutated in up to 60% of children with the disease. The first patient in the Phase I/II FUNCTION-DMD study had no serious adverse events or adverse events of special interest, Gorsuch said. An independent data safety monitoring board reviewed results through eight weeks after treatment, saw no concerning safety signals and endorsed completing dosing of the first part of the study, she said. Kelly said three-month biopsy data for approximately the first three patients could be available around the second quarter of 2027. The second patient had been enrolled and the third was being screened, he said. Gorsuch said the company believes dystrophin expression above 5% could be therapeutically meaningful, but emphasized that any protein increase must ultimately be linked to functional benefit. Kelly said the first patient's 12-month biopsy data could arrive in the fall of 2027. Gorsuch said the 12-month point would also be more appropriate than three months for beginning to assess functional improvements. About Precision BioSciences (NASDAQ:DTIL) Precision BioSciences is a biotechnology company developing gene-editing therapies using its proprietary ARCUS platform. The platform is designed to make targeted changes to DNA and is being applied to investigational treatments for genetic and infectious diseases. The company's programs include PBGENE-HBV, an investigational therapy designed to target hepatitis B virus DNA, and PBGENE-DMD, a gene-editing program for Duchenne muscular dystrophy. These candidates remain in development and are not approved treatments. 新生儿 疫苗接种 乙型肝炎急剧下降 - 哥伦比亚密苏里州

Newborn vaccinations for hepatitis B drop sharply - Columbia Missourian
新闻简报根据《新英格兰医学杂志》发表的一项研究,密苏里州哥伦比亚市新生儿的乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 急剧下降。疫苗接种比率从2022年的79.8%降至2026年6月的65.6%,引发了人们对公共卫生风险的担忧。尽管没有报告具体的病例或死亡人数,但这种下降被认为是一个重大问题。该研究强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来解决免疫率下降的问题。目前情况正在调查中,所提供的证据中没有提供有关应对措施或公共卫生行动的进一步细节。本报告重点关注观察到的 疫苗接种 比率趋势及其对社区健康的影响。

时间
发布于 2026 年 11 月 26 日。活动日期为 2026 年 11 月,特别提到 2026 年 6 月新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 下降。
地点与对象
密苏里州哥伦比亚,特别是布恩县。受影响人群包括新生儿及其家人,重点关注 疫苗接种 率。
事件
据《新英格兰医学杂志》发表的一项研究报道,新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 急剧下降。这是根据研究结果证实的事件。
规模、影响与风险
疫苗接种 比率从 2022 年的 79.8% 降至 2026 年 6 月的 65.6%。尽管提供的证据中没有报告具体的病例或死亡人数,但这种下降被明确指出对公共健康构成风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
目前情况正在调查中,研究提供了主要证据。所提供的证据中没有报告有关应对措施或公共卫生行动的进一步细节。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Newborn vaccinations for hepatitis B drop sharply - Columbia Missourian

News Brief
Newborn vaccinations for hepatitis B have dropped sharply in Columbia, Missouri, according to a study published in the New England Journal of Medicine. The vaccination rate fell to 65.6% in June 2026 from 79.8% in 2022, raising concerns about public health risks. The decline is noted as a significant issue, though no specific numbers of cases or deaths are reported. The study highlights the need for targeted interventions to address the drop in immunization rates. The situation is under investigation, and further details on response measures or public health actions are not provided in the supplied evidence. This report focuses on the observed trend in vaccination rates and its implications for community health.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

新生儿 疫苗接种 乙型肝炎发病率大幅下降

Newborn vaccination rates for hepatitis B drop sharply
新闻简报A new study published in the New England Journal of Medicine on November 24, 2026, reveals a sharp decline in newborn hepatitis B vaccination rates in the United States, dropping from 79.8% in 2022 to 65.6% in June 2026. The decline is attributed to a shift in federal vaccine recommendations, with the CDC ending its universal recommendation for hepatitis B vaccination at birth in December 2025, even for infants of mothers who tested negative. This decision is on hold due to legal challenges. The study, based on health records from over 1.3 million infants, highlights that fewer mothers testing positive for hepatitis B are receiving the vaccine for their babies, from around 98% in 2025 to 94% in 2026. The CDC acknowledges that screening during pregnancy is not perfect, and the birth dose has served as a safety net to prevent early infections. The decline in vaccination rates raises concerns about increased transmission risks from infected mothers to infants, potentially leading to chronic infections and associated complications like cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. The study's authors emphasize the importance of maintaining vaccination rates to prevent these outcomes, especially in the context of growing vaccine skepticism and the rollback of mandates in some states.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究的结果于 2026 年 6 月报告,疫苗接种 率自 2023 年以来出现下降。CDC 于 2025 年 12 月做出更改建议的决定,该研究的发布日期为 2026 年 11 月 24 日。
地点与对象
在美国全国范围内,新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 率下降,影响到婴儿和母亲,特别是保守州的婴儿和母亲以及孕妇。
事件
2026 年 11 月 24 日发表在《新英格兰医学杂志》上的一项新研究发现,新生儿感染乙型肝炎 疫苗 的比例已从 2022 年的 79.8% 急剧下降至 2026 年 6 月的 65.6%。CDC 还在 2025 年 12 月更改了建议,终止了出生时普遍建议感染乙型肝炎 疫苗,即使对于接受过检测的母亲的婴儿也是如此。消极的。
规模、影响与风险
The study analyzed data from over 1.3 million infants and found a decline in vaccination rates, with fewer mothers testing positive for hepatitis B receiving the vaccine for their babies. The CDC's decision to revise the recommendation is on hold due to legal challenges. The decline in vaccination rates could increase the risk of hepatitis B transmission from mothers to infants, potentially leading to chronic infections and associated complications like cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma.
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究的作者和疾病预防控制中心承认,怀孕期间的乙型肝炎筛查并不完美,出生剂量只是一个安全网。该研究的结果基于健康记录和健康数据分析公司 Truveta 的数据,并有待进一步调查和澄清。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Newborn vaccination rates for hepatitis B drop sharply

News Brief
A new study published in the New England Journal of Medicine on November 24, 2026, reveals a sharp decline in newborn hepatitis B vaccination rates in the United States, dropping from 79.8% in 2022 to 65.6% in June 2026. The decline is attributed to a shift in federal vaccine recommendations, with the CDC ending its universal recommendation for hepatitis B vaccination at birth in December 2025, even for infants of mothers who tested negative. This decision is on hold due to legal challenges. The study, based on health records from over 1.3 million infants, highlights that fewer mothers testing positive for hepatitis B are receiving the vaccine for their babies, from around 98% in 2025 to 94% in 2026. The CDC acknowledges that screening during pregnancy is not perfect, and the birth dose has served as a safety net to prevent early infections. The decline in vaccination rates raises concerns about increased transmission risks from infected mothers to infants, potentially leading to chronic infections and associated complications like cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. The study's authors emphasize the importance of maintaining vaccination rates to prevent these outcomes, especially in the context of growing vaccine skepticism and the rollback of mandates in some states.
Original source text (English)
A worker applies an adhesive bandage to a child at a vaccination clinic in Minneapolis' Corcoran Park in August 2023. A new study found the nationwide percentage of newborns receiving the hepatitis B vaccination has declined sharply since 2023. (Photo courtesy of the City of Minneapolis) The percentage of newborns receiving the hepatitis B vaccine has declined sharply in recent years, falling to 65.6% in June 2026 from 79.8% in 2022, according to a new study published Nov. 24 in the New England Journal of Medicine. The authors of the peer-reviewed study looked at health records from more than 1.3 million infants and found that the percentage of babies receiving the hepatitis B vaccination within one day of birth began declining around 2023. The study also found that fewer mothers testing positive for hepatitis B got the vaccine for their babies, from around 98% of those infants in 2025 to around 94% in 2026. A panel of federal vaccine advisers, the Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices, voted in December 2025 to end the longtime recommendation for infants to receive the hepatitis B vaccination at birth even if the mom tested negative. That decision is on hold pending legal challenges. The study was based on data from Truveta, a health data and analytics company owned and governed by 30 health systems across the nation. In explaining the December change, the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention said screening of pregnant women is highly reliable and can spot disease early. But Nina Masters, an author of the new study and a principal applied research scientist at Truveta specializing in infectious disease and maternal health, told Stateline that "not every mother is tested during pregnancy, and not every person who gets tested has a known status." "And screening is imperfect," she said. "This birth dose has been a universal recommendation to be a safety net to catch those kids that might fall through the cracks, and what we're seeing here is that that safety net is kind of falling apart." Hepatitis B is a contagious liver infection that can cause symptoms of fatigue, loss of appetite and jaundice, and potentially lead to cancer. But it can be prevented through a vaccination. The virus can be passed down from an infected mother to a baby, so since 1991 , the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention has recommended that all newborns receive the hepatitis B vaccination within a day after birth to prevent early infections, especially when mothers test positive. A 2023 study by CDC researchers found that between 1990 and 2019, with the recommendation for newborn vaccination, there was a 99% decline in reported cases of acute hepatitis B among people younger than 19 years old. It's official: Measles is endemic again in the US But amid growing vaccination skepticism, the new study found that fewer babies are getting the shot. The shift began a few years before vaccine skeptic Robert F. Kennedy Jr. became secretary of the U.S. Department of Health and Human Services in February 2025. Researchers noted the decline in vaccination rates for hepatitis B comes as the federal government and many conservative states walk back vaccine mandates for children. Just this month, the United States lost its status as a country that had eliminated measles, as the transmission of measles has risen in the last year. Children are particularly susceptible to the disease. "When messaging gets confusing and there's different recommendations coming from different medical bodies and different kinds of assessments of vaccine safety that are being publicized, I think it can become scary and hard to make the right choice," Masters said. "And I think that's what we're seeing is just extreme confusion as a result of the level of misinformation and changing recommendations around pediatric vaccines." Stateline reporter Shalina Chatlani can be reached at schatlani@stateline.org . Time
The study's findings were reported in June 2026, with the decline in vaccination rates noted since 2023. The CDC's decision to change the recommendation was made in December 2025, and the study's publication date is November 24, 2026.
Location and population
The decline in newborn vaccination rates for hepatitis B was observed nationwide in the United States, affecting infants and mothers, particularly those in conservative states and pregnant women.
Event
A new study published in the New England Journal of Medicine on November 24, 2026, found that the percentage of newborns receiving the hepatitis B vaccine has dropped sharply from 79.8% in 2022 to 65.6% in June 2026. The CDC also changed its recommendation in December 2025, ending the universal recommendation for hepatitis B vaccination at birth, even for infants of mothers who tested negative.
Scale, impact and risk
The study analyzed data from over 1.3 million infants and found a decline in vaccination rates, with fewer mothers testing positive for hepatitis B receiving the vaccine for their babies. The CDC's decision to revise the recommendation is on hold due to legal challenges. The decline in vaccination rates could increase the risk of hepatitis B transmission from mothers to infants, potentially leading to chronic infections and associated complications like cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study's authors and the CDC acknowledge that screening for hepatitis B during pregnancy is not perfect and that the birth dose served as a safety net. The study's findings are based on health records and data from Truveta, a health data analytics company, and are subject to further investigation and clarification.

中国生物制药:首个慢性乙型肝炎功能性治愈疗法贝普若韦生钠注射液在中国获批上市

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

人民财讯11月27日电,中国生物制药在港交所公告,慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)同类首创功能性治愈疗法贝普若韦生钠注射液(商品名:贺舒可)已获得中国国家药监局批准上市,联合核苷(酸)类似物(NAs)用于慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的功能性治愈,适用于已接受NAs治疗至少6个月、HBV DNA<90 IU/mL、乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg)≤3000 IU/mL、无肝硬化的慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染成人患者。
